### [1] [Did You Steal My Shot? Pioneering Camera Motion Plagiarism Detection in Generative Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22267)
*Chengguo Zhang,Ping Ping*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个相机运动分析基准，提出结合流体动力学涡度线索的运动表示方法，显著提升了对生成视频中相机运动抄袭的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机运动具有高商业价值，但现有相似度检测方法因训练数据中视觉内容与运动纠缠，且传统光流无法充分表征复杂运动，导致难以有效检测生成视频中的相机运动抄袭。

Method: 1. 构建包含11种运动风格的相机运动数据集及评估协议；2. 提出一种新的运动表示方法，在光流基础上引入流体动力学的涡度（vorticity）线索以增强对运动的捕捉能力。

Result: 所提出的检测器在抄袭检测任务上比最强基线提升了3.02倍，且在生成视频上保持有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将版权保护范围从静态内容扩展至动态相机运动，为高价值知识产权提供了新的保护手段。

Abstract: Camera motion often reflects directorial intent and requires professional equipment, making it a high value form of intellectual property. However, generative video models can imitate such high value camera motions with simple prompts, while existing similarity detection methods mainly operate on visual content and fail to capture deeper motion similarity. This is mainly because their training data entangles camera motion with visual content. Moreover, traditional optical flow is insufficient to represent complex camera motions. We therefore build the first benchmark for camera motion analysis, including a motion dataset with \textbf{11} motion styles and evaluation protocols. Furthermore, we propose a motion representation that augments optical flow with vorticity cues from fluid dynamics, thereby better capturing motions. Experiments show that our detector achieves a \textbf{3.02*} improvement in plagiarism detection over the strongest baseline and remains effective on generative videos. We believe our work extends copyright protection beyond static content to dynamic camera motion.

</details>


### [2] [Enabling Vision and Cross-Modal Learning for Multimodal Stroke Recurrence Prediction: An Interpretable Two-Step Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22271)
*Christian Gapp,Elias Tappeiner,Martin Welk,Karl Fritscher,Stephanie Mangesius,Constantin Eisenschink,Philipp Deisl,Michael Knoflach,Astrid E. Grams,Elke R. Gizewski,Rainer Schubert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过自监督预训练和选择性参数冻结策略，有效缓解了多模态脑卒中复发预测中的模态不平衡问题，提升了影像与表格数据的整合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态模型中因模态不平衡导致的对主导模态过度依赖及互补信息利用不足的问题，并探索自监督预训练对跨模态行为的影响。

Method: 在3D CTA扫描上进行自监督预训练，结合两种不同的参数冻结策略进行微调；将性能与基线模型及从头训练的模型进行对比，并分析模态贡献。

Result: 自监督预训练显著优于非预训练模型及先前基线，最佳Vision Transformer模型成功克服了单模态崩溃（unimodal collapse），揭示了视觉特征与性别、冠心病（CHD）间的临床相关交互模式。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练和策略性微调有助于实现更平衡的模态利用，并促进有意义的跨模态交互，为多模态医疗模型提供了有效优化路径。

Abstract: Multimodal stroke recurrence prediction requires effective integration of heterogeneous clinical and imaging data, yet modality imbalance often causes models to over-rely on dominant modalities and underutilize complementary information. While self-supervised pretraining and selective parameter freezing are commonly employed to improve representation learning and fine-tuning stability, their effect on modality contributions and cross-modal behavior in multimodal medical models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate whether image pretraining on 3D CTA scans reduces modality imbalance and improves cross-modal integration for stroke recurrence prediction, a clinically critical task we recently addressed. To this end, two multimodal neural networks are pretrained in a self-supervised manner and subsequently fine-tuned using two distinct freezing strategies. Their performance and modality utilization are compared against both the baseline model from our previous work and models trained entirely from scratch in this study. Our results demonstrate that self-supervised pretraining enables more effective utilization of the multimodal image-tabular dataset, outperforming both the prior baseline and all non-pretrained models. Notably, the best-performing Vision Transformer based neural network successfully overcomes unimodal collapse. Synergy analysis reveals significant interactions between vision and both gender and CHD, suggesting clinically relevant patterns for stroke recurrence. Overall, our findings demonstrate that self-supervised pretraining and strategic fine-tuning support more balanced modality utilization and enable meaningful cross-modal interactions. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ChristianGappGit/SSL_Pretraining.

</details>


### [3] [Moonworks Lunara: Modeling Artistic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22272)
*Yan Wang,Yanzu Wang,Maitreyee Joshi,Samiha Sadeka,Partho Hassan,Reza Jarral,Sayeef Abdullah,Sabit Hassan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“艺术智能”概念，发布Moonworks Lunara模型。该模型采用Diffusion Mixture Transformer架构和新型训练算法，在美学质量、情感共鸣等指标上超越FLUX.2、Qwen-Image等主流模型，兼具高性能与低延迟，推动视觉智能从“图像准确”向“意义诠释”转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在定义并实现“艺术智能”，即在保留语义、艺术和构图结构真实性的同时，为创造性可能性留出空间，解决现有模型在深层意义诠释和想象力表达方面的不足。

Method: 1. 构建Diffusion Mixture Transformer架构；2. 开发新型训练算法，通过信息量大的样本获取和针对性注入人类创作的艺术作品来迭代演化数据分布；3. 使用GPT-5.6 Sol作为评估器及盲测人类评估进行基准测试。

Result: Lunara在Aesthetic Quality（8.473 vs 8.457）排名第一，情感共鸣排名第二，内容完整性具竞争力。盲测人类评估亦将其排第一。在CLIPScore、LAION Aesthetic Predictor及GenEval（对比16个模型）中均表现强劲。模型拥有小于10B的活跃参数规模和小于10秒的推理延迟。

Conclusion: Lunara将艺术智能推向了前沿，证明了视觉智能的前沿已从“能否生成正确的图像”转变为“能多深地诠释意义并将其实现为富有想象力和表现力的世界”。

Abstract: We formulate \emph{Artistic Intelligence} as exploration driven world realization, leaving space for creative possibility while preserving the semantic, artistic, and compositional structure that must remain true. Moonworks Lunara, a text-to-image model, implements this framework with a novel Diffusion Mixture Transformer architecture. A new training algorithm iteratively evolves the data distribution through informative sample acquisition and targeted injection of human-created art. We benchmark Lunara against seven image-generation models, including FLUX.2-Klein-4B, Qwen-Image (20B), and GPT-Image-1-Mini. With GPT-5.6 Sol as evaluator, Lunara ranks first in \emph{Aesthetic Quality (8.473 vs. 8.457 GPT-Image-1-mini)}, second in \emph{Emotional Resonance}, and remains competitive in \emph{Content Integrity}. A blind human evaluation over the same evaluation set corroborates the automated metrics, ranking Lunara first. It also stays among the strongest models under conventional measures including CLIPScore and LAION Aesthetic Predictor. On GenEval, Lunara achieves competitive performance against a broader set of 16 models, including GPT Image 2 and Seedream 4.0. These results place Lunara at the frontier with Artistic Intelligence while maintaining a sub-10B active-parameter footprint and sub-10-second inference latency. Lunara advances the general visual intelligence frontier by shifting the question from whether models can get images right to how deeply they can interpret meaning and realize it as imaginative, expressive worlds.

</details>


### [4] [Performance vs Consistency: Evaluating a Foundation Model in Lung-RADS Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22281)
*Benjamin Renoust,Pierre Baudot,Tiffany Foriel,Yousra Haddou,Charles Voyton,Pierre-Henri Siot,Ezequiel Geremia,Danny Francis,Jean-Christophe Brisset,Valérie Bourdès,Sylvain Bodard,Benoit Huet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究评估了医学基础模型 MedGemma 及其微调版本在肺癌筛查 Lung-RADS v2022 评估中的表现。结果显示，原生模型性能不足（AUC 0.70），而微调后模型（AUC 0.83）性能低于放射科医生均值（0.90）但具有一致性。结论是微调模型可作为临床决策支持的互补工具，尤其在专家资源有限时，但需进一步外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型在多种医学影像任务中表现出色，但在结构化临床解释设置（如 Lung-RADS 评估）中的性能尚不明确。此外，即使有标准化框架，放射科医生之间仍存在显著的判读变异性，亟需探索 AI 模型在此场景下的潜力与局限性。

Method: 使用 NLST 数据集，对比三位对象：1) 12名放射科医生独立进行 Lung-RADS v2022 评估；2) 未经微调的通用医学基础模型 MedGemma；3) 针对肺癌检测与诊断微调后的 MedGemma 版本。采用多读者设计量化读者间变异性，并以 AUC 为主要评价指标。

Result: 1) 放射科医生平均 AUC 为 0.90，个体间差异显著（范围 0.80-0.94）。2) 原生 MedGemma 模型 AUC 仅为 0.70，未达到临床相关性能标准。3) 微调后的 MedGemma 模型 AUC 提升至 0.83，处于放射科医生性能分布的下限区间。4) 模型在固定条件下输出确定性结果，消除了运行间变异性，这与人类医生的读者间变异性形成对比。

Conclusion: 微调的基础模型在预测一致性方面优于人类医生，尽管峰值准确率较低，但可作为临床决策支持的潜在互补工具，特别适用于缺乏专家经验的场景。然而，由于研究基于病例富集队列且未考虑真实世界患病率及外部验证，其直接临床推广能力受限。

Abstract: Foundation models have recently demonstrated strong capabilities across a wide range of medical imaging tasks. However, their performance in structured clinical interpretation settings remains insufficiently explored. In lung cancer screening, interpretative variability persists despite standardized frameworks such as Lung-RADS. In this study, we evaluate MedGemma, a medical general-purpose foundation model derived from Gemini and its fine-tuned version adapted for lung cancer detection and diagnosis, compared against radiologists performing Lung-RADS v2022 assessment on the NLST dataset. Twelve radiologists independently evaluated each case in a multi-reader design, enabling quantification of inter-reader variability. Radiologists achieved a mean AUC of 0.90, with substantial variability across readers (range: 0.80-0.94). The native foundation model achieved an AUC of 0.70, failing to reach clinically relevant performance. In contrast, fine-tuning significantly improved performance to an AUC of 0.83, placing the model within the lower range of individual radiologists performance. These findings highlight a trade-off between peak accuracy and prediction consistency. Unlike radiologists, under fixed conditions, the model produces deterministic outputs, removing inter-run variability under identical inputs, in contrast to inter-reader variability observed among radiologists. This supports the role of fine-tuned foundation models potential complementary tools for clinical decision support, particularly in settings with limited expertise. However, evaluation is performed on a case-enriched cohort from NLST and does not account for real-world prevalence or external validation, limiting direct clinical generalization.

</details>


### [5] [Brain-to-Image Generation: Reconstructing Visual Stimuli from EEG using Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22282)
*Harshit Goyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在THINGS-EEG2数据集上建立了一个可复现的单被试基线，旨在验证脑电图（EEG）能否通过视觉嵌入空间检索观看过的刺激。研究采用卷积编码器将EEG映射到图像特征，结果显示模型实现了显著高于随机水平的粗粒度语义解码准确率，但无法实现像素级的忠实重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于头皮测量的时空分辨率限制以及配对EEG-图像数据集规模较小，从EEG中重构视觉刺激面临巨大挑战。现有生成模型训练数据匮乏，且难以评估EEG是否能在语义层面有效检索视觉信息。

Method: 1. 使用紧凑的时空卷积编码器，将重复平均后的EEG信号映射到512维ViT-B/32图像特征。
2. 采用概念不重叠的验证集进行模型选择，并使用官方测试画廊进行评估。
3. 进行会话平衡消融实验以分析重复平均的影响。
4. 测试模型跨被试泛化能力及直接条件生成器的性能。
5. 区分直接重建与基于预扩散先验的语义渲染。

Result: 1. 在Top-1, Top-5, Top-10召回率分别达到12.83%、39.17%和58.00%，显著高于随机水平（0.5%, 2.5%, 5.0%）。
2. 增加测试重复次数有助于提升排名效果。
3. 未经适应地将Subject 01模型应用于其他被试会导致性能急剧下降，证实了EEG信号的个体特异性。
4. 无外部视觉权重的直接条件生成器产生噪声主导的输出，未能实现有效重建。

Conclusion: 在闭集、重复平均协议下，EEG支持高于随机水平的粗粒度语义解码，但不支持对刺激像素的忠实恢复。研究强调了EEG解码的个体特异性及当前技术在直接图像重建上的局限性。

Abstract: Reconstructing visual stimuli from electroencephalography (EEG) is difficult because scalp measurements have high temporal but limited spatial resolution, and paired EEG-image datasets remain small relative to modern generative-model training corpora. We present a reproducible single-subject baseline on THINGS-EEG2 that first tests the more defensible question of whether EEG can retrieve the viewed stimulus in a visual embedding space. A compact temporal-spatial convolutional encoder maps repetition-averaged EEG (63 by 250) to provided 512-dimensional ViT-B/32 image features. Model selection uses a concept-disjoint validation split, and final evaluation uses the official 200-image, 200-concept test gallery. Across three training seeds, the model obtains 12.83 +/- 0.58%, 39.17 +/- 1.76%, and 58.00 +/- 1.73% image recall at 1, 5, and 10 (mean +/- sample standard deviation), compared with analytical chance levels of 0.5%, 2.5%, and 5.0%. A session-balanced ablation shows that averaging more test repetitions generally improves ranking. Applying the Subject 01 model to the other nine subjects without adaptation causes a sharp performance drop, exposing subject specificity. We further report exploratory stress tests of direct conditional generators trained without external visual weights: single-subject and ten-subject variants produce noise-dominated outputs, with early validation improvements reversing after one to four epochs. Finally, we distinguish direct reconstruction from semantic rendering with a pretrained diffusion prior. The results support above-chance coarse semantic decoding under a closed-set, repetition-averaged protocol, but do not support faithful recovery of stimulus pixels.

</details>


### [6] [Rethinking Streaming Video Diffusion Model: Context, Execution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22283)
*Hongchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出流式视频扩散的统一分析框架，对比不同历史条件策略，发现渐进式历史在质量和效率上均优于全去噪历史，并验证LoRA适配的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索流式视频扩散生成的设计空间，以平衡生成质量与计算效率，明确模型、采样器、历史条件及训练策略之间的关系。

Method: 构建统一分析框架，研究三种代表性历史选择策略（clean, same-level, progressive history），并在VBench基准上进行评估；同时结合流水线执行和LoRA适配技术优化训练参数。

Result: Same-level和progressive历史分别获得85.24和85.60的VBench分数，优于clean历史的84.45；progressive历史显著提升主体一致性和运动连贯性，并通过流水线处理实现1.57-2.83倍的速度提升；LoRA适配仅需2.15%参数即可改善生成质量。

Conclusion: 完全去噪的历史并非高质量流式生成的必要条件，应联合设计历史条件、执行调度和训练策略以优化性能。

Abstract: Understanding the design space of streaming video diffusion is essential to exploring its potential for generation quality and computational efficiency. We develop a unified analytical framework that relates model and sampler choices, historical conditioning, execution scheduling, and training strategies. The framework accommodates a broad family of causal context-selection policies and makes their computational dependencies and training-inference alignment explicit. Within this design space, we study three representative policies: clean, same-level, and progressive history. On the full VBench prompt set, same-level and progressive history achieve aggregate scores of 85.24 and 85.60, respectively, compared with 84.45 for the clean-history reference. Long-video comparisons further show improved subject consistency and more coherent motion with progressive history. By allowing multiple denoising nodes to be processed together, progressive-history pipelining achieves $1.57$-$2.83\times$ steady-state DiT speedups under our evaluated conditions. We additionally find that LoRA adaptation of the DMD fake-score network improves generation quality using only 2.15% as many trainable fake-score parameters as full-parameter adaptation. Together, these findings show that fully denoised history is not a prerequisite for high-quality streaming generation and motivate the joint design of historical conditioning, execution, and training.

</details>


### [7] [Complementary rPPG-Derived and Lip-Region Frequency Cues for Talking-Face Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22284)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究两种轻量级视觉线索（rPPG波形和唇部DCT系数）在Celeb-DF++数据集上检测Talking-face deepfakes的效果。发现唇部DCT与rPPG具有互补性，拼接融合能提升域内检测性能，但在跨生成器泛化时存在局限，且唇部DCT优于全脸DCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于rPPG的deepfake检测方法在不同生成器之间表现不均，需要探索其他视觉线索以增强检测的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 在Celeb-DF++数据集的七种TF方法上，采用独立于主体的协议，评估RhythmFormer提取的rPPG衍生波形和唇部区域离散余弦变换（DCT）系数这两种轻量级视觉线索。通过单模基线、拼接融合（Concat fusion）以及留一生成器外（leave-one-generator-out）评估进行分析。

Result: 1. 域内检测中，唇部DCT在除SadTalker外的所有方法上匹配或超过rPPG衍生的1D ResNet；拼接融合达到AUC 0.891，优于单模基线（0.824和0.827）。
2. 跨生成器评估中，两种线索表现出不同的转移特性：每种线索对三个保留的方法转移效果明显更好，但IP-LAP接近随机水平。
3. 拼接融合平均AUC为0.798，但在DCT转移较差时低于单独使用rPPG，表明静态融合仅部分利用了互补性。
4. 唇部DCT在七种方法中的六种上优于全脸DCT。

Conclusion: rPPG衍生信号被视为经验性线索而非必然的心脏起源信号。唇部DCT与rPPG在特定条件下具有互补性，但简单的静态融合无法完全解决跨生成器泛化问题，需进一步研究更有效的融合策略。

Abstract: Talking-face (TF) deepfakes are detected unevenly by rPPG-based methods across generators. We study two lightweight visual-only cues, rPPG-derived waveforms extracted by RhythmFormer and lip-region discrete cosine transform (DCT) coefficients, on the seven TF methods of Celeb-DF++ under a subject-independent protocol. In-domain, lip-region DCT matches or exceeds the rPPG-derived 1D ResNet on every method except SadTalker, and Concat fusion reaches AUC 0.891 against 0.824 and 0.827 for the unimodal baselines. Under leave-one-generator-out evaluation the cues split: each transfers clearly better to three held-out methods, and IP-LAP is near chance for both. Concat averages 0.798 but falls below rPPG alone where DCT transfers poorly, so static fusion only partly exploits this complementarity. Lip-region DCT outperforms full-face DCT on six of seven methods. We treat the rPPG-derived signal as an empirical cue and do not claim it is cardiac in origin.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond the Survey: A Systematic Empirical Study of Detection and Association in Visual MOT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22291)
*Linh Van Ma,Juhua Hu,Wei Cheng,Unse Fatima,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多目标跟踪（MOT）算法进行了全面的实验评估，揭示了检测质量比关联策略对整体性能影响更大，且现代深度学习检测器配合专用重识别模型效果最佳，但端到端Transformer方法虽鲁棒性强却计算成本高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述多为理论分类或体系结构，缺乏基于公开实现的严谨实验视角；旨在为研究人员和从业者提供方法选择和系统设计方面的实用指导。

Method: 提出统一流水线图整合“Tracking-by-Detection”和端到端深度学习两大分支的核心组件；在MOT16/17/20、SportsMOT、DanceTrack、CrowdTrack等标准数据集上进行广泛的实证研究，量化检测与关联模块的贡献。

Result: 1. 检测质量主导关联策略表现，检测器改进带来超10%的性能增益，而优化关联策略仅提升不足5%；2. 现代深度学习检测器结合专用重识别模型显著优于联合检测与嵌入方法；3. 基于Transformer的端到端方法对检测质量波动更具鲁棒性，但伴随高昂的计算成本。

Conclusion: 检测结果质量在多目标跟踪系统中起决定性作用；研究结果为在不同性能和鲁棒性需求下设计和优化MOT系统提供了关键洞察和实践依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive experimental evaluation and detailed analysis of state-of-the-art multi-object tracking algorithms, with an emphasis on quantifying the individual contributions of detection and association components to overall tracking performance. Unlike existing surveys that primarily offer theoretical categorizations or taxonomies of tracking methods, our work adopts a rigorous experimental perspective grounded in publicly available implementations, providing practical guidance for researchers and practitioners in method selection and system design. We introduce a unified pipeline diagram that consolidates the core components across the two main branches of visual multi-object tracking: tracking-by-detection and end-to-end deep learning paradigms, and systematically analyze the object detection, feature extraction, and data association modules. Through extensive empirical studies on standard benchmarks, including MOT16, MOT17, MOT20, SportsMOT, DanceTrack, and CrowdTrack datasets, we reveal critical insights: (1) detection quality dominates association strategy performance, with detector improvements yielding more than 10% gains compared to less than 5% from refined association strategies; (2) modern deep learning detectors paired with specialized re-identification models significantly outperform joint detection and embedding approaches; and (3) transformer-based end-to-end methods exhibit greater robustness to detection quality variations but at a substantial computational cost. Our findings from extensive experiments provide key insights into component-level effects in MOT, particularly the dominant influence of detection quality relative to association, while offering practical insights for designing and optimizing MOT systems under varying performance and robustness requirements. Code and experimental setups are available at github.com/linh-gist/VisualMOT.

</details>


### [9] [Validating, Not Sampling: Region-Level Robustness of Vision-Language and Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22293)
*Bogdan Aron,Christopher Brix,Benedikt Brückner,Yanghao Zhang,Panagiotis Kouvaros,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了H²V-M框架，对6种VLM和5种VLA在连续的光度和几何图像扰动区域进行了首次鲁棒性验证。结果表明，模型鲁棒性主要取决于扰动类型而非模型本身，且小扰动即可导致VLA动作改变；同时，模型家族比模型大小对鲁棒性的影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）和视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）基准测试仅采样离散扰动，无法保证在未测试区域不存在失败案例。为了评估这些模型在真实世界应用中面对微小图像变化时的决策稳定性，需要一种能覆盖连续扰动区域并提供严格正确性保证的验证方法。

Method: 基于H²V验证框架，引入了一种边际感知收敛规则H²V-M。该方法旨在降低32B参数规模模型的验证成本，通过在光度（如亮度偏移）和几何（如相机旋转）扰动的整个连续区域上进行系统性搜索，以发现反例并验证鲁棒性。

Result: 1. H²V-M在模型查询次数上比H²V提升了一个数量级，并能比随机采样更快地找到反例，同时提供 soundness guarantees（正确性保证）。
2. 实验显示，VLMs在大角度相机旋转下的鲁棒性优于VLAs。
3. 对于VLAs，即使极小的扰动（±1°）也常在许多情况下改变其指令动作。
4. 鲁棒性主要受扰动类型影响，其次受模型家族影响，而与模型大小的关系较小。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前主流VLMs和VLAs在连续图像扰动下的脆弱性，特别是VLAs对微小几何变化的敏感性。提出的H²V-M框架为大规模多模态模型的鲁棒性验证提供了高效且可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. There, a small perturbation to the recorded camera image may change a decision significantly. However, existing benchmarks for these models only sample perturbations, which does not guarantee the absence of a failure in the untested region. We present the first robustness validation of six VLMs (drawn from the Gemma, InternVL, LLaVA, and Qwen families) and five VLAs (drawn from the GR00T, OpenVLA, and $π$ families) over entire continuous regions of photometric and geometric image perturbation: brightness shifts, camera rotations, and their composition. To this end, we build on the validation framework H$^2$V and introduce H$^2$V-M, a margin-aware convergence rule that makes validation affordable at the 32B parameter scale. We demonstrate that H$^2$V-M outperforms H$^2$V by an order of magnitude in model queries and that it finds counterexamples faster than random sampling while providing soundness guarantees. Our VLM and VLA robustness validation shows that robustness is mostly dependent on the perturbation type, rather than the model, and that VLMs are more robust to large camera rotations than VLAs. For VLAs, even perturbations as small as $\pm1^\circ$ can change the commanded action in many cases. We also show that robustness depends more on model family than on model size.

</details>


### [10] [Mind the Gaps: A Curated Benchmark for Form Field Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23679)
*Iheb Brini,Omar Moured,Hamza Gbada,Elisa Barney*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出mini-CommonForms基准，旨在解决现有表单字段检测（FFD）数据集标注噪声大、不一致的问题，通过高质量注释支持可复现的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表单字段检测因依赖布局和空白而非可见前景内容而具有挑战性；现有大规模数据集依赖启发式标注流程，导致标签噪声和不一致，阻碍了可靠评估。

Method: 构建精心策划的mini-CommonForms FFD基准，提供一致且高质量的注释，并对最先进的检测方法进行详细评估。

Result: 提供了一个用于文档自动化和辅助应用的可复现研究基准，数据集和代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作为表单字段检测领域提供了高质量的数据基准，有助于推动相关技术的标准化评估与应用发展。

Abstract: Form Field Detection (FFD) is a fundamental component of document understanding systems, enabling applications ranging from large-scale industrial digitization to accessible form interaction for automated analysis. Unlike conventional object detection tasks, FFD is inherently challenging because fields are often defined by layout structure and whitespace rather than visible foreground content. Existing large-scale datasets frequently rely on heuristic annotation pipelines, resulting in noisy and inconsistent labels that hinder reliable evaluation. In this work, we introduce mini-CommonForms, a carefully curated FFD benchmark with consistent, high-quality annotations, and present a detailed evaluation of state-of-the-art detection approaches. The benchmark is designed to support reproducible research in document automation and accessibility-oriented applications. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/moured/mini-commonforms

</details>


### [11] [On The Robustness-Resolution Tradeoff In Temporal Quantization Of Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22295)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Ruhi Sharmin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究事件管道中异步时间戳离散化对表示稳定性的影响，证明硬时间分箱的不连续性，并提出线性双分箱插值作为最优连续编码方案，显著降低表示漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件管道通常将异步时间戳离散化后再进行学习，但这一过程的稳定性直接依赖于时间分辨率。现有的硬时间分箱方法存在不连续性，微小的时间戳偏移可能导致事件质量在分箱间跳跃，影响模型稳定性。

Method: 从表示层面对时间分辨率依赖性进行研究：1) 证明硬时间分箱是不连续的；2) 定义一类非负、保质量、保分辨率的连续编码器类，并证明该类中每个编码器的全局L1灵敏度至少为2/Delta（Delta为分箱宽度）；3) 提出线性双分箱插值方法，证明其达到该灵敏度下限，且具有局部支撑和一阶矩保持的唯一性。

Result: 在SHD、N-MNIST和DVS128 Gesture数据集上的实验表明：在线性插值下，平均表示漂移降低47-72%，同时保持近乎不变的清洁准确率；在DVS Gesture上实现零预测翻转；在SHD上，测得的漂移与1/Delta呈高度相关（R^2 = 0.992）。

Conclusion: 线性双分箱插值是满足特定约束的最优连续编码器，能有效提升事件数据表示的稳定性，降低因时间戳离散化带来的漂移，且不影响分类精度。

Abstract: Event pipelines often discretize asynchronous timestamps before learning. This step looks harmless, but its stability depends directly on temporal resolution. We study this dependence at the representation level. We first show that hard temporal binning is discontinuous: an arbitrarily small timestamp shift near a boundary can move unit event mass between bins. We then define a class of nonnegative, mass-preserving, resolution-faithful continuous encoders and prove that every encoder in this class has global L1 sensitivity at least 2/Delta, where Delta denotes bin width. Linear two-bin interpolation attains this limit. Local support and first-moment preservation also make it unique. Experiments on SHD, N-MNIST, and DVS128 Gesture support the analysis. Across uniform timestamp budgets, linear interpolation lowers mean representation drift by 47-72% while keeping clean accuracy nearly unchanged. On DVS Gesture, it produces zero prediction flips across all tested budgets and three seeds. On SHD, measured drift follows 1/Delta with R^2 = 0.992.

</details>


### [12] [Authority-Preserving Evaluation of Medical Vision-Language Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22302)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AuthEval框架，用于评估医疗视觉语言模型建议与本地医生最终决策之间的“权威差距”，区分模型建议质量与实际执行动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往假设本地审核会保留模型的建议，但现实中本地服务会根据政策、容量和患者背景修改或拒绝建议，导致“建议质量”与“选定动作质量”是两个不同的评估目标，现有的证据转移存在偏差。

Method: 引入AuthEval日志与评估框架，记录模型建议和最终选定的动作；根据声明的本地标准对选定动作评分，并在可行时对未被采纳的建议进行同等规则评分；计算并报告由建议变更率和分数变化共同决定的“权威差距”。

Result: 在ISIC 2019数据集上使用MedGemma模型模拟本地审核发现：相似的变更率在不同约束条件下（容量 vs 安全）产生的权威差距不同（容量下为+0.744，安全下无显著差距）；评估单元的选择（图像加权 vs 病灶感知集群加权）也会导致差距方向反转（从+0.374变为-0.206）。

Conclusion: 权威差距的大小和符号不仅取决于建议变更率，还受评分变化和评估权重影响；AuthEval能澄清研究记录是支持关于模型本身的声明、工作流的声明，还是两者兼有。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models can propose how urgently a skin lesion should be reviewed, but the local service retains authority to accept or replace that proposal under referral policy, capacity, and locally held patient context. Proposal quality and selected-action quality are therefore distinct evaluation targets, and benchmark evidence transfers between them only when local review preserves the expected action score. We introduce AuthEval, a logging and evaluation framework that records both actions, scores the selected action under declared local criteria, and, where feasible, scores the declined proposal under the same rule. It reports the resulting authority gap only when the record supports it. Because the gap is the product of the proposal-change rate and the mean score change on changed cases, that rate alone determines neither its magnitude nor its sign. On ISIC 2019, with MedGemma and simulated local review, two constraint regimes with similar change rates produced an optimistic image-equal gap under capacity ($+0.744$ simulator units) but no detectable gap under safety. The declared evaluation unit also mattered: under mixed constraints the gap reversed from $+0.374$ to $-0.206$ when weighting shifted from image to lesion-aware cluster. AuthEval thus clarifies whether a study's records support claims about the model, the workflow, or both.

</details>


### [13] [GameReplica: A Benchmark for Black-Box Visual Game Replication by Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22308)
*Boyu Qiao,Zixin Tang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Wenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GameReplica框架，评估编码智能体在“黑盒”条件下仅通过视觉观察和交互来逆向工程并复现游戏的能力。实验显示当前模型难以兼顾视觉保真度与规则一致性，且随难度增加性能差距拉大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注从文本规范生成代码，但在缺乏源代码和文档的“黑盒”条件下，智能体能否仅凭视觉反馈和主动交互推断规则并复现可执行系统尚不明确。

Method: 构建GameReplica闭环评估框架，涵盖感知、探索、归纳、复现和验证全流程。包含25款游戏的125个任务（5种核心机制，5个难度等级），要求智能体仅通过截图和操作接口访问目标游戏，生成自包含的可运行副本并由外部程序自动验证。

Result: 最佳模型（Claude Opus 4.8）得分71.6%，其余模型仅4.0%-42.9%。所有模型均呈现“视觉保真度远高于实现和规则一致性”的模式。随着难度从L1升至L5，弱模型成绩急剧下降，强模型下降幅度较小。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体在全链路黑盒游戏复现方面面临巨大挑战，尤其在从像素反馈中归纳深层游戏规则方面能力不足，视觉外观复制相对容易但机制复现困难。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks usually evaluate implementation after the target behavior has been specified in text, code, or demonstrations. Existing research has extensively evaluated the ability of coding agents to generate programs from textual specifications. However, under black-box conditions where neither source code nor documentation is available, it remains underexplored whether an agent can induce the rules solely through visual observation and active interaction and reproduce the target system as a verifiable executable system. To this end, we present GameReplica, a closed-loop evaluation framework for end-to-end black-box game replication that covers the full perception, exploration, induction, reproduction, and verification pipeline. GameReplica comprises 125 tasks spanning 25 games across 5 core mechanism families, with each game instantiated at five difficulty levels. The tasks require an agent to access the target game only through screenshots and an action interface, induce the key visual elements and gameplay rules from pixel feedback and interaction outcomes, and generate a self-contained, runnable game replica that can be automatically verified by an external program. Experiments show that current coding agents still face substantial challenges in end-to-end black-box replication: the best-performing model (Claude Opus 4.8) achieves an overall score of 71.6\%, while the remaining models score only 4.0\%--42.9\%. Further analysis reveals a consistent pattern across all models: visual-fidelity scores are substantially higher than implementation- and rule-consistency scores, indicating that agents replicate visual appearance more readily than game mechanics. The difficulty levels further amplify the performance gap: from L1 to L5, the overall score of weaker agents drops sharply, whereas that of the best-performing agent declines only slightly.

</details>


### [14] [Yarn tracking of large-scale 3D textile reinforcements using topological material features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22315)
*Hafsa El Herichi,Arturo Mendoza,Yanneck Wielhorski,Hugues Talbot,Stéphane Roux*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于切片分析的半自动X射线CT图像追踪方法，用于在粗分辨率下识别和跟踪大型工业风扇叶片预成型体中的纺织增强纱线，实现了高精度、低训练需求的自动化标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高通过生成高保真数值模型进行模拟的可靠性，解决在粗分辨率（>140 μm）X射线CT图像中半自动追踪风扇叶片干预成型体中纺织增强结构的挑战。

Method: 采用正交于主纱线方向的平面进行可扩展的切片分析；统计建模纱线的横截面形状、三维空间连续性与运动、以及与相邻纱线的相对空间排列；通过变分公式将这些统计属性整合到追踪框架中，优化连续横截面平面上的所有纱线中心位置。

Result: 成功追踪了超过3,000根经纱，跨越1,500个切片，追踪成功率高于90%。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模自动化纺织增强结构标注提供了有前景的途径，有助于更高效地表征复杂复合材料结构中的材料特性。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of CT images has become increasingly important to enhance the reliability of simulations through the generation of high fidelity numerical models. This study addresses the challenging task of semi-automatically tracking textile reinforcements in fan blade dry preforms using X-ray CT images captured at coarse resolutions (i.e., above 140 $μ$m). Our approach offers a scalable, slice-based analysis conducted on planes orthogonal to the main yarn directions, applied to a large-scale real industrial component. This enables accurate identification and tracking of yarn paths while requiring minimal training. The method models three key yarn properties statistically: their typical cross-section shape, their continuity and movement in the 3D space, and their spatial relative arrangement with respect to neighboring yarns. These statistical properties are integrated into a tracking framework via a variational formulation that optimizes all yarn center positions in successive cross-section planes. The method tracks more than 3,000 warp yarns across 1,500 slices and achieves a tracking success rate above 90%. Overall, this work demonstrates a promising approach toward large-scale, automated textile reinforcement annotation, paving the way for more efficient material characterization in complex composite structures.

</details>


### [15] [ALPINE: Adaptive Localization for Parameter- and Sample-Efficient Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22323)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种超轻量级（2.2万-3.5万参数）的空间关系架构，用于少样本图像分类。在严格匹配的协议下，该模型在CIFAR-FS和MiniImageNet上相比Prototypical Networks、Relation Networks和MAML实现了5-shot准确率提升，参数量减少27-53%，收敛更快且泛化能力更强。消融实验表明，内容自适应的补丁定位器是性能提升的主要驱动因素，而非成对关系计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少样本学习研究主要关注准确率，而忽视了达到该准确率所需的参数量和训练样本预算，这对缺乏大规模计算资源的实践者构成现实约束。旨在开发一种低资源消耗且高效的少样本学习方案。

Method: 构建了一种结合固定Gabor边缘能量引导与窗口式内容自适应补丁定位器的超轻量级空间关系架构。采用严格的等集束预算协议（250元训练集、5个种子、每种子600个评估集）进行评估。通过假性检验消融实验（推理时置零关系token及完全重训练）来验证各组件贡献。

Result: 1. 在CIFAR-FS和MiniImageNet上，5-shot准确率均优于三种基线模型；2. 参数量比基线减少27-53%；3. 收敛所需的训练集束更少；4. 在未见的细粒度领域（CUB-200-2011鸟类数据集）上无需重新训练即表现出更好的泛化能力；5. 对50%遮挡和25%空间平移更具鲁棒性；6. 容量扫描显示准确率在2.2万至3.5万参数附近出现真实平台期；7. 确认内容自适应补丁定位器是性能主要驱动力，而非关系计算。

Conclusion: 该超轻量级架构在保证甚至提升少样本分类性能的同时，显著降低了参数需求和计算负担，证明了其高效性和鲁棒性。研究结果强调了内容自适应机制的重要性，并提供了完整的可复现数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Few-shot learning research is predominantly evaluated on accuracy alone, with limited attention to the parameter and training-sample budgets required to reach that accuracy - a real constraint for practitioners without large-scale compute. We present an ultra-lightweight (22,249-34,917 parameter) spatial-relational architecture for few-shot image classification that combines fixed Gabor edge-energy guidance with a windowed, content-adaptive patch locator. Under a strictly matched, iso-episode-budget protocol (250 meta-training episodes, 5 canonical seeds, 600 evaluation episodes per seed), our architecture achieves 5-shot accuracy gains, consistent across all five seeds, over Prototypical Networks, Relation Networks, and MAML on both CIFAR-FS and MiniImageNet, while using 27-53% fewer parameters than any baseline. It also converges in fewer training episodes, generalizes better to an unseen fine-grained domain (CUB-200-2011 birds, zero retraining), and is more robust to 50% occlusion and 25% spatial translation than all three baselines. A series of falsification ablations - zeroing relational tokens at inference and retraining without them entirely - shows that the architecture's pairwise relational computation, while present, is not the primary driver of its performance; the content-adaptive patch locator is. We report this honestly, together with a capacity sweep showing a genuine accuracy plateau near 22-35k parameters, and release full seed-level results and checkpoint hashes for reproducibility.

</details>


### [16] [Dimensionality reduction for AI based hyperspectral image classification based on XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22333)
*Vladimir Zeljković,Branka Stojanović,Harald Ganster,Aleksandar Nešković*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究利用基于AI的降维技术（特别是CNN和多通道高光谱成像）解决木材回收中材料循环利用有限的问题，并通过可解释AI（XAI）提升该过程的可持续性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决木材回收过程中材料循环利用受限的挑战，提高回收过程的可持续性和效率。

Method: 应用卷积神经网络（CNNs）处理多通道高光谱成像（HSI），将特征空间从RGB扩展至200多个光谱通道，并结合可解释AI（XAI）方法进行降维和特征优化。

Result: 提出了一个解决方案框架，通过AI驱动的降维技术优化了木材回收流程，增强了其在可持续性和效率方面的表现。

Conclusion: 结合CNN、高光谱成像和XAI的方法能有效改善木材回收中的材料利用率，为相关领域的可持续发展提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This research addresses the challenge of limited material recycling in wood recycling processes by leveraging artificial intelligence (AI)-based dimensionality reduction. Our study explores the application of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) in multi-channel hyperspectral imaging (HSI), extending beyond RGB channels to over 200 spectral channels. Dimensionality reduction within this context involves streamlining the feature space for AI system training and inference. Focusing on explainable AI (XAI) methods, this paper contributes to a broader research initiative, presenting a solution framework that enhances the sustainability and efficiency of wood recycling processes.

</details>


### [17] [Hierarchical Aggregation of Semantic Uncertainty in 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22351)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Wallace Moreira Bessa,Julio A. Placed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外训练或采样的框架，利用现有3DSG中的检测置信度和嵌入信息，通过闭式计算量化语义不确定性并传播至房间级断言，从而提升机器人对地图可靠性的判断及检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇3D场景图（3DSGs）将所有对象节点视为同等确定，导致机器人在查询地图时无法区分哪些条目不可靠。传统的语义不确定性估计方法需要重复采样、训练或使用保留标签，这些在部署系统的查询阶段均不可用。

Method: 构建了一个框架，利用3DSG中已有的检测置信度和视觉-语言嵌入，将其转换为条目正确的概率。该概率通过包含层次结构传播，形成对房间是否包含查询类别的信念。该方法在逻辑尺度上将四种信号结合为闭式解，无需额外的感知处理或训练。针对共享探测器和词汇表的对象，采用完全相关聚合策略，避免将重复错误误认为重复证据。

Result: 在HM3DSem数据集上评估，该框架相比最先进3DSG系统，提高了对象检索准确率，并降低了其读取的图结构中房间级断言的错误率。

Conclusion: 通过有效整合现有3DSG数据中的不确定性信号，该方法在不增加计算负担和训练需求的情况下，显著增强了3D场景图的语义鲁棒性和查询可靠性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) ground each object node in a vision-language embedding, yet they record every entry as equally certain, so a robot querying the map cannot tell which of its entries are unreliable. Estimators of semantic uncertainty could supply that distinction, but they require repeated sampling of a model, training, or held-out labels, none of which are available to a deployed system at query time. We present a framework that exploits the detector confidence and the embeddings a 3DSG already stores, converts them into a probability that an entry is correct, and propagates that probability through the containment hierarchy into a belief that a room contains a queried class. Four signals, each paired with the object-level error it indicates, are converted to probabilities at the logit scale learned by the vision-language model and combined in closed form with no additional perception or training. Objects sharing a detector and a vocabulary fail together, so the framework aggregates them in the fully correlated limit, where an aggregation under independence would treat one repeated error as repeated evidence. Evaluated on HM3DSem against a state-of-the-art 3DSG system, the framework improves object retrieval and lowers the error of the room-level assertions of the graph it reads.

</details>


### [18] [MarsRecon: Self-Supervised and Multimodal Surface Representations for Mars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22379)
*Akshay Naik,Marius F. R. Juston,Jay Mahajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MarsRecon，一种针对火星HiRISE影像的地理空间感知预训练与检索管道，通过掩码自编码器和多模态对齐解决地质标签稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率轨道影像提供了丰富的火星表面记录，但稀疏的地质标签限制了监督式表示学习的效果。

Method: 构建MarsRecon管道：校准NASA行星数据系统产品，提取有效地理参考图块；使用掩码自编码器在无标签影像上训练视觉编码器；冻结视觉编码器，将其特征与观测文本、坐标及局部-全局图像上下文进行对齐。

Result: 主要模型系列的重建损失从0.1751降至0.1342；在保留测试集上，最强本地-主模型实现图像到文本召回率@10为0.3787，文本到图像召回率@10为0.9161，以及局部到全局召回率@10为0.4350。

Conclusion: 建立了一个可行的火星特定预训练和检索管道，但需进一步控制裁剪重叠并进行下游地质评估以评估其嵌入的更广泛效用。

Abstract: High-resolution orbital imagery offers a rich record of the Martian surface, but sparse geological labels limit supervised representation learning. We present MarsRecon, a geospatially aware pipeline for learning visual and multimodal representations from HiRISE observations of Olympus Mons. The pipeline calibrates NASA Planetary Data System products, extracts valid georeferenced patches, and trains a masked autoencoder on unlabeled imagery. Increasing input resolution and filtering invalid tokens reduced held-out reconstruction loss from 0.1751 to 0.1342 in the principal Stage A model series. We then freeze the visual encoder and align its features with observation text, coordinates, and local--global image context. The strongest current local-primary model achieves image-to-text recall@10 of 0.3787, text-to-image recall@10 of 0.9161, and local-to-global recall@10 of 0.4350 on the held-out test split. These results establish a working Mars-specific pretraining and retrieval pipeline; further crop-overlap controls and downstream geological evaluations are needed to assess the broader utility of its embeddings.

</details>


### [19] [Style as Cover: Deep Image Steganography via Stylized Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22392)
*Qi Li,Jidong Yang,Huaike Yu,Chunpeng Wang,Suo Gao,Herbert Ho-Ching Iu,Yuantian Miao,Bin Ma,Xiao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StyleStegaNet，一种基于风格隐藏而非封面匹配的图像隐写框架。通过将秘密信息隐藏在风格化图像中，解决了传统方法在原始封面泄露时的脆弱性问题。该方法通过四阶段任务解耦和渐进式训练策略，实现了在风格转换下对秘密信息的可靠恢复，并在公开数据集上证明了其有效性及抗检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像隐写技术大多依赖于“封面保留传输”（cover-preserving transmission），即生成的隐写图像需尽可能接近原始封面。然而，一旦原始封面被暴露或能被可靠近似，这种范式变得非常脆弱。因此，需要一种新的隐写范式来增强安全性。

Method: 提出了StyleStegaNet框架，用“风格隐藏传输”替代传统的封面匹配。具体包括：1) 基于公开的参考风格生成风格化隐写图像；2) 将隐写不可见性从封面级伪装重新定义为行为级伪装（基于风格变换）；3) 将整体任务解耦为四个协调阶段：隐写生成、风格化传输、结构保持重建和秘密恢复；4) 采用渐进式三阶段训练策略，利用小波域约束和感知监督引导可恢复信息向结构表示靠拢。

Result: 1) 实验表明StyleStegaNet在DIV2K和MS-COCO数据集上有效；2) 分析了秘密可恢复性主要局限于归一化结构子空间，解释了直接风格化基线失败的原因以及重建引导恢复路径的必要性；3) 使用两个深度检测器进行的少样本图像隐写分析显示，检测准确率接近随机猜测（约51%），证明了其极强的隐蔽性。

Conclusion: StyleStegaNet通过引入风格化隐藏机制，成功解决了传统封面保留隐写在封面泄露场景下的安全问题。该方法通过解耦任务和特定的训练策略，在保持高隐蔽性的同时实现了秘密信息的可靠恢复，为图像隐写提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Image steganography hides secret message within normal images, with most existing works relying on cover-preserving transmission. However, such a paradigm becomes vulnerable once the original cover is exposed or can be reliably approximated. In this paper, we propose StyleStegaNet, a stylized image hiding framework that replaces cover matching with style-concealment transmission. Instead of transmitting a cover-like stego image, StyleStegaNet generates stylized stego images conditioned on publicly available style references, redefining steganography invisibility from cover-preserving concealment to behavior-level camouflage based on style transformation. Such a setting poses a substantial challenge to reliable secret recovery, since neural stylization can significantly alter the feature statistics exploited by deep hiding methods. To address this challenge, StyleStegaNet decouples the overall task into four coordinated stages: stego generation, stylized transmission, structure-preserving reconstruction, and secret recovery. Moreover, StyleStegaNet is optimized with a progressive three-stage training strategy, in which wavelet-domain constraints and perceptual supervision guide the recoverable information toward structural representations. We further provide an analysis showing that secret recoverability is largely restricted to the normalized structural subspace, offering a mechanistic explanation for why directly stylized baselines fail and why a reconstruction-guided recovery path is necessary. Extensive experiments on DIV2K and MS-COCO datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of StyleStegaNet. And few-shot image steganalysis with two deep detectors further shows detection accuracy near random guessing, approximately 51\%.

</details>


### [20] [Spatiotemporal Flux Probing for Single-Photon Videography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22479)
*Jerry Yan,Matteo Forlivesi,Bowen Tan,Andrew Xie,Siddharth Somasundaram,Sotiris Nousias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时空通量探测理论的算法，通过利用光子到达的全局时空相关性，在极端光子稀疏条件下实现高速视频重建、速度选择性成像及跨模态泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖局部时空窗口聚合光子检测以提高信噪比，但这会丢弃全局结构信息，且在低光环境下因光子在空间和时间的稀疏性而失效。

Method: 构建时空通量探测理论，开发一种直接从光子流中估计底层强度傅里叶系数的算法，利用光子到达的全局时空模式中的相关性来恢复运动和光照信息。

Result: 该方法显著减少了恢复快速运动和时变光照所需的光子数量；实现了能自动将视频重新聚焦于特定检测运动的速度选择性摄影技术；并在单光子、事件相机和脉冲相机等多种传感模态上展现了良好的通用性。

Conclusion: 利用光子到达的全局时空相关性而非局部聚合，可以更高效地从极端稀疏光子数据中恢复动态场景的高分辨率时空信息，具有广泛的适用性和性能优势。

Abstract: We address the problem of recovering high-speed videos from dynamic scenes under extreme photon sparsity. Existing methods rely on aggregating photon detections in local spatiotemporal windows to improve signal-to-noise ratio; however, this local grouping discards global structure and fails in low-light regimes where photon detections are sparse in space and time. In this work, we show that the information needed to recover both motion and illumination is encoded in correlations over the full space-time pattern of photon arrivals. Building on this insight, we develop a spatiotemporal flux probing theory and an algorithm that estimates the Fourier coefficients of the underlying intensity directly from the photon stream. We demonstrate that our approach (1) recovers fast motion and temporal illumination dynamics with substantially fewer photons than prior methods, (2) enables velocity-selective videography that automatically refocuses video onto specific detected motions, and (3) generalizes across sensing modalities including single-photon, event, and spike cameras.

</details>


### [21] [Event-Frame Fusion for Inter-Frame Segmentation via Event-Guided Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22500)
*Dalia Hareb,Jean Martinet,Benoit Miramond,Elisabetta Chicca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合帧相机和事件相机的混合视觉架构，利用稀疏脉冲神经网络（SNN）进行运动估计和语义分割，实现了高达500 Hz的分割速率和低能耗，有效解决了传统相机在运动模糊、过曝等挑战条件下的信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于帧的自动驾驶导航方法受限于运动模糊、眩光、延迟及低时间分辨率（20-30 FPS），导致帧间信息丢失；而纯事件基算法在精度上仍不及帧基方法，因为多数分割方法是为密集帧数据设计的。

Method: 构建混合视觉系统：(1) 使用42k参数的紧凑型脉冲神经网络（SNN）进行运动估计；(2) 使用0.84M参数的轻量级事件驱动SNN进行基于帧的语义分割，通过插值帧间运动来细化分割结果。

Result: 该框架实现了高达500 Hz的分割速率，每次推理能耗低于1.87 mJ，并能在GPU上以高达200 Hz的频率实时执行。此外，该方法能有效补偿受模糊或过曝光影响的帧中的信息丢失，提升了恶劣条件下的感知鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 混合视觉架构通过结合帧与事件数据的优势，显著提高了语义分割的速度、能效及在挑战性环境下的鲁棒性，为自主导航提供了更精确高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation requires precise and efficient semantic segmentation, yet existing frame-based approaches remain limited by motion blur, glare, latency, and the low temporal resolution (20-30 FPS) of conventional cameras, which leads to information loss between frames. Event cameras have emerged as an alternative sensing modality, capturing intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and sparse outputs. However, event-based algorithms still fall short of frame-based ones in accuracy, as most segmentation methods are designed for dense frame data. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid vision architecture that combines conventional frame-based and event-based cameras. The system integrates two complementary components: (1) a compact Spiking Neural Network (SNN) with 42k parameters for motion estimation, and (2) a lightweight event-driven SNN with 0.84M parameters for frame-based semantic segmentation, which interpolates motion between frames to refine segmentation results. By predicting inter-frame segmentations, the framework achieves segmentation rates of up to 500 Hz with an energy consumption below 1.87 mJ per inference, while maintaining real-time GPU execution at frequencies up to 200 Hz. Additionally, our approach compensates for information loss in frames affected by blur or overexposure, enabling more robust perception in challenging conditions.

</details>


### [22] [Rethinking Vision Architectures with Gated Linear Attention and KAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22506)
*Ali Mehizel,Oussama Khaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LKAT（线性Kolmogorov-Arnold Transformer），结合门控线性注意力与KAN前馈网络，在ImageNet-100等数据集上优于ViT和MLP-Mixer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Transformers依赖二次复杂度自注意力和固定激活MLP；线性注意力虽降低复杂度但仍耦合固定MLP；现有视觉KAN多保留MHSA或忽略注意力，缺乏有效结合。

Method: 构建各向同性ViT编码器：集成分块门控线性注意力（GLA）与两层KAN前馈网络，并提供I/O感知的RBF-KAN融合内核。

Result: LKAT-B在ImageNet-100超越ViT-B/16、ViT-5-B及Mixer-B/16；Tiny/Small/Base变体在CIFAR/ImageNet任务中表现一致且具备良好迁移能力。

Conclusion: 门控线性注意力与基于径向基函数的KAN作为互补归纳偏置，可有效提升中等规模视觉表示学习效果。

Abstract: Vision Transformers allocate most parameters to multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) for channel mixing, while token interactions usually rely on quadratic multi-head self-attention (MHSA). Linear attention reduces sequence complexity to O(N), but remains coupled with the same fixed-activation MLP as softmax Transformers. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) instead place learnable univariate maps on edges, yet prior vision KANs keep MHSA or omit attention entirely. We introduce LKAT (Linear Kolmogorov-Arnold Transformer), an isotropic ViT encoder that couples chunkwise Gated Linear Attention (GLA) with a two-layer KAN feed-forward, and we provide an I/O-aware fused RBF-KAN kernel for the radial-basis grid maps. Under a shared DeiT-style recipe we compare LKAT with ViT, ViT-5, and MLP-Mixer. LKAT-B exceeds ViT-B/16, ViT-5-B, and Mixer-B/16 on ImageNet-100. Tiny/Small/Base LKAT variants scale consistently on CIFAR-10/100, and ImageNet-100 pretraining transfers to CIFAR fine-tuning. The results support gated linear attention and KAN-based radial-basis functions as complementary inductive biases for mid-scale visual representation learning.

</details>


### [23] [AdaMerge: Tuning-Free Patch Compression for Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22562)
*Jianxin You,Kun Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaMerge，一种无需数据集调参的即插即用文档压缩方法，通过检测合并余弦序列中的“悬崖”点自动确定每份文档的压缩边界，在长文档和短文档基准上均优于或持平于需网格搜索调参的SOTA方法PtM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多向量视觉文档检索（VDR）模型（如ColPali）存储和延迟成本高；现有压缩方法中SOTA的Prune-then-Merge (PtM) 虽性能优异，但需针对每个数据集进行复杂的网格搜索以调整聚类预算参数，缺乏通用性和效率。

Method: 基于层级聚类产生的合并余弦序列存在明显的“悬崖”现象（区分冗余与显著信号），且该临界点在大量数据集中分布集中。提出AdaMerge：(1) 通过对合并余弦轨迹进行间隙分析，自动检测每份文档独有的“悬崖”点以确定压缩边界；(2) 构建注意力加权聚类质心以保留显著信号。

Result: 在长文档基准ViDoRe-V2上，AdaMerge显著优于经调优的PtM（p < 10^-4）；在短文档基准ViDoRe-V1上，达到与调优PtM相当的性能（接近无损）。该方法仅需约10ms/文档的计算开销，且全局超参数统一，无需数据集特定调参。

Conclusion: AdaMerge通过利用合并余弦序列的固有结构特性，实现了高效、通用的文档压缩，消除了对数据集特定超参数调优的需求，同时保持了高精度的视觉文档检索性能。

Abstract: Multi-vector visual document retrieval (VDR) models such as ColPali and ColNomic achieve strong accuracy by representing each document with hundreds to thousands of patch-level embeddings, at substantial storage and latency cost. Existing compression methods either prune unimportant patches or merge similar ones into clusters; the recent state-of-the-art merging method Prune-then-Merge (PtM) consistently outperforms pruning-only baselines at high compression, but requires a per-dataset cluster budget m to be tuned by grid search. We observe that the merge-cosine sequence produced by hierarchical clustering exhibits a sharp cliff separating mergeable redundancy from salient signal, and that the location of this cliff is concentrated in a narrow band across more than 11,000 documents from 14 datasets. This suggests the merge boundary can be detected per document rather than tuned per dataset. Building on this observation, we propose AdaMerge, a plug-and-play compression method that (i) detects each document's own cliff via gap analysis on the merge-cosine trajectory, and (ii) builds attention-weighted cluster centroids to preserve salient signal. On the long-document benchmark ViDoRe-V2 (4 datasets, two backbones), AdaMerge significantly outperforms tuned PtM across the operating range (p < 10^-4); on the short-document benchmark ViDoRe-V1 (10 datasets, two backbones), where all merging methods are already near-lossless, AdaMerge matches tuned PtM without any per-dataset tuning. AdaMerge adds only about 10 ms per document and exposes a single global hyperparameter shared across all datasets and backbones.

</details>


### [24] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Counterfactual Diagnosis of End-to-End and VLA Driving Policies Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22582)
*Ruolin Yang,Zilin Huang,Buoyue Wang,Zhengyang Wan,Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 指出传统的排行榜排名无法反映策略在特定场景（如行人附近）的真实行为。提出一种基于反事实编辑的“检查程序”，通过移除行人或改变光照来诊断驾驶策略的因果反应，揭示了现有策略在避障、距离规划和稳定性方面的严重缺陷，并提供了更细致的性能画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端和视觉-语言-动作（VLA）驾驶策略评估主要依赖排行榜排名，但排名仅反映结果而非背后的行为逻辑，导致难以预测策略在新地点的表现。例如，在nuScenes或NAVSIM上的高分并不能保证策略在遇到行人等复杂场景时能做出安全反应。

Method: 提出“反事实检查”方法：选取数百个真实帧，对每帧进行两种编辑（移除行人或模拟夜间光照），并由独立检测器验证编辑有效性。通过分析规划轨迹的变化作为诊断依据，构建两个因果轴和五个测试维度：规划行驶距离、看到行人是否带来安全性、响应是否随危险程度缩放、无需求时的计划变动幅度以及对无关光照变化的敏感度。

Result: 在246个NAVSIM近行人场景中，当行人位于规划路径上时，仅1.9%的响应为真正的避让行为；中位间距变化最多为0.03米，中位规划距离变化最多为0.08米。在左舵到右舵驾驶的预注册测试中，暴露率和特异性排序、光照判决及碰撞结果具有可转移性，但点值和危险敏感性判决不可转移。不同策略表现出显著差异：有的因规划短而安全，有的在无人行让路情况下覆盖类人距离，有的在对无需反应的扰动下表现不稳定。

Conclusion: 传统的评分指标掩盖了策略行为的细微差别，新的反事实检查框架能有效分离这些差异，提供关于策略安全性、稳定性和敏感性的详细画像。该研究强调了从单一排名转向多维度因果诊断的重要性，并承诺公开代码和编辑后的帧数据以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: End-to-end and vision-language-action (VLA) driving policies are compared by leaderboard rank, but a rank reports an outcome, not the behaviour behind it, so it predicts poorly how a policy will behave at a new site. On six released policies, rank on nuScenes open-loop error or on NAVSIM's leaderboard does not carry over to scenes with a pedestrian near the ego corridor at a new site. We propose a counterfactual check-up: a few hundred real frames, each edited two ways (pedestrian removed, or re-lit by a night-style perturbation), every edit verified by an independent detector, and the change in the planned trajectory read as a diagnosis rather than a score. From these edits two causal axes are read, and five exams built on them separate what a score merges: how far the policy plans to drive, whether seeing the pedestrian buys safety, whether that response scales with danger, whether the plan moves when nothing requires it, and how much an irrelevant lighting change moves it. On 246 NAVSIM near-pedestrian scenes, in the cells where the pedestrian lies on the planned path only 1.9% of responses are genuine avoidance, and under our open-loop protocol the median clearance change is at most 0.03 m and the median change in planned distance at most 0.08 m for every policy. In a pre-registered test from left- to right-hand drive, the exposure and specificity orderings, the lighting verdict and the collision outcome transfer, while point values and the hazard-sensitivity verdict do not. Read as a selection report, the profiles say which policy is safe because it plans short, which covers a human-like distance without yielding, and which is unsteady under a change that requires no reaction, and they price each verdict: most settle within a few dozen frames, hazard sensitivity needs hundreds. Code and edited frames will be released.

</details>


### [25] [Seeing is not Enough: Vision-Language Models Perceive Evidence but Fail to Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22588)
*Yuyang Dai,Bofei Huang,Hongbo Zhang,Haoran Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型（VLMs）在识别视觉证据后仍做出矛盾决策的“过程失败”问题，提出VPAC-Bench基准和SRT干预方法。研究表明，显式过程结构化干预可将错误率从95%降至13%以下，且当先验与场景决策边界对齐时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs虽能正确识别视觉证据，但在处理模糊情况时，常出现已识别的证据无法约束最终决策的“过程失败”现象，导致决策与视觉证据相悖。

Method: 1. 构建VPAC-Bench基准：涵盖9类真实图像过程家族，标注当前活动阶段及邻近阶段转换。2. 提出状态-相关性-目标（SRT）干预：要求模型在回答前将可见证据关联至相关过程状态。3. 对比实验：评估通用SRT、边界对齐SRT与思维链（CoT）基线在不同任务中的表现。

Result: 1. 过程失败普遍存在：超过95%的模糊案例中，模型即使枚举出正确视觉候选项，仍过度承诺于单一答案。2. 通用干预有效：显式过程结构化干预将错误率降至13%以下，且不损害明确案例的性能。3. 边界对齐优势显著：当已知相关阶段转换时，边界对齐SRT在装配、物理状态转换、导航、交通及物体使用等功能性任务中，显著优于通用过程提示和所有测试的思维链基线。

Conclusion: 过程先验在与其特定的场景决策边界对齐时最有用。这证明了基于过程的视觉推理需要采用边界感知的先验选择策略，而非通用的思维链方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) perform strongly on visual question answering benchmarks, yet often make decisions that contradict visual evidence they have already identified correctly. We distinguish perceptual failure, where relevant evidence is not recognized, from process failure, where recognized evidence fails to constrain the final decision. We introduce VPAC-Bench, a benchmark spanning nine real-image process families, with each image annotated by its current activity stage and nearby stage transition. We also propose State-Relevance-Target (SRT), a family of structured process-prior interventions that requires models to connect visible evidence to the relevant process state before answering. Across multiple VLMs, process failure is widespread: models that correctly enumerate visual candidates still over-commit to a single answer in more than 95% of ambiguous cases. An explicit process-structured intervention reduces this rate to below 13% without degrading performance on unambiguous cases. However, the transfer of process priors is model-dependent, and generic SRT does not consistently outperform strong chain-of-thought baselines. When the relevant stage transition is known, boundary-aligned SRT substantially outperforms generic process prompting and all tested chain-of-thought baselines across assembly, physical state transition, navigation and traffic, and object-use affordance tasks. These results show that process priors are most useful when aligned with the scene's specific decision boundary, motivating boundary-aware prior selection for process-grounded visual reasoning.

</details>


### [26] [X-Beat: An Explainable Framework for ECG Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22631)
*Mohammad Sadman Tahsin,Haitham Y. Adarbah,Afzel Noore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出X-Beat框架，结合迁移学习与事后解释性（Grad-CAM）及可靠性评估，用于ECG图像分类。ResNet-50表现最佳（准确率91.94%），模型聚焦波形相关区域且低置信度预测多为错误，为临床可信AI提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高性能深度学习模型在临床部署中因缺乏透明度和可靠性验证而难以应用的问题，旨在支持医疗领域可信AI系统的发展。

Method: 构建X-Beat基准框架，对四种心脏类别进行分类；使用ImageNet预训练的CNN骨干网络（EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50, DenseNet-121, MobileNetV3-Large）进行统一训练协议评估；引入Grad-CAM可视化解释、解释稳定性、区域敏感性分析及基于置信度的可靠性评估。

Result: ResNet-50性能最优，准确率达91.94%，宏观F1分数0.9098，AUC高达0.995；解释分析显示模型主要关注波形相关区域；可靠性评估表明大多数错误预测发生在低置信度情况下。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个结构化、可复现的基准，用于同时评估ECG图像分类中的预测性能和解释可靠性，有助于开发用于临床决策支持系统的可信和可解释AI组件。

Abstract: Accurate automated interpretation of electrocardio- grams (ECGs) is essential for early detection of cardiac condi- tions such as myocardial infarction and rhythm abnormalities. However, many high-performing deep learning models remain difficult to deploy in clinical settings due to limited transparency and lack of reliability validation. In this work, we present X- Beat, an explainable and reliability-aware benchmark framework for ECG image classification designed to support trustworthy AI systems in healthcare. The proposed framework combines transfer learning with post-hoc explainability and systematic reliability evaluation across four cardiac classes: Abnormal Heartbeat, History of Myocardial Infarction, Myocardial In- farction, and Normal Heartbeat. Multiple ImageNet-pretrained CNN backbones, including EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50, DenseNet- 121, and MobileNetV3-Large, are evaluated under a unified training protocol. Beyond standard performance metrics, we incorporate Grad-CAM-based visual explanations together with additional analyses, including explanation stability, regional sen- sitivity, and confidence-based reliability assessment, to examine whether model predictions are supported by clinically meaningful evidence. Experimental results show that ResNet-50 achieves the best performance, reaching 91.94% accuracy and a macro F1- score of 0.9098, with strong class separability (AUC up to 0.995). Explanation analyses indicate that the model primarily focuses on waveform-relevant regions, while reliability evaluation suggests that most incorrect predictions occur with lower confidence. Overall, this work provides a structured and reproducible bench- mark for evaluating both predictive performance and explanation reliability in ECG image classification, contributing toward the development of trustworthy and interpretable AI components for clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [27] [ConsistWorld: Evidence Routing for Consistent Multi-Agent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22641)
*Qianxun Xu,Xianfang Zeng,Xinyao Liao,Wei Cheng,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConsistWorld，一种多智能体世界模型，通过历史证据路由和同行信息共享机制，实现从单张共享图像生成多视角、长时间一致的视频流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视频世界模型仅支持单观察者视角的连贯生成，难以扩展到多智能体场景，缺乏在因果流式处理下跨独立控制视角和时间间隔的一致性保证。

Method: 提出两种核心机制：1) 姿势条件记忆检索（Pose Conditioned Memory Retrieval），利用相机几何信息从所有智能体的历史中检索相关观测证据；2) 可见性门控同行共享（Visibility-Gated Peer Sharing），根据历史覆盖范围和当前视角重叠度调节并发同行信息。两者共同确定进入上下文的历史观察和同行信息的贡献位置。

Result: 在证据共享案例及视频长度、智能体数量泛化实验中，ConsistWorld实现了强大的跨时间和跨智能体一致性，同时保持了具有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: ConsistWorld通过结合相机几何约束的内存检索与同行共享机制，有效解决了多智能体视频生成中的长期记忆召回和协同探索问题，实现了高质量的多视角时空一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video world models enable temporally coherent generation for a single observer. Extending them to multiple agents requires consistency across independently controlled views and temporal gaps under causal streaming. We present ConsistWorld, a multi-agent world model that generates camera-controlled video streams of a static scene from one shared image. We formulate consistency as routing evidence from committed multi-agent history and concurrently generated peer views to the tokens being generated. Pose Conditioned Memory Retrieval selects relevant historical observations from all agents, recovering evidence beyond the recent context window. Visibility-Gated Peer Sharing regulates current peer information according to estimated historical coverage and current-view overlap. Together, they determine which historical observations enter the context and where concurrent peer information contributes, supporting long-term recall and coordinated exploration. Both mechanisms use camera geometry and maintain a bounded active context for a fixed agent count and retrieval budget. Experiments on evidence sharing cases and video length and agent number generalizations show that ConsistWorld achieves a strong cross-time and cross-agent consistency while preserving competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [28] [Math2Visual-X: A Modular Framework for Pedagogically Aligned Lower-Primary Math Visuals Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22647)
*H. D. E. Maduranga,S. K. Munasinghe,K. P. T. I. Weerasekara,Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于符号的视觉生成框架Math2Visual-X，旨在解决小学低年级数学应用题（MWP）可视化生成的难题。该框架结合LLM路由、工作表导向模块和SVG资产获取机制，在覆盖范围和可扩展性上优于现有方法，并在人工评估中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉表征有助于低年级学生理解数学应用题，但生成适合课堂使用的可视化内容仍面临困难。现有的符号系统控制性强但覆盖范围有限，而端到端的文本转图像系统往往难以满足精确的数学约束。

Method: 构建了一个符号视觉生成框架，包含：1) 基于大语言模型（LLM）的路由层；2) 三个面向工作表的生成模块；3) 两种用于开放世界SVG资产获取的回退机制。

Result: 与Stable Diffusion XL、Nano Banana和GPT Image等基线模型相比，Math2Visual-X在人工评估中展现出最强的整体性能，证明了其在自动MWP视觉生成方面的可扩展性和教学合理性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展且符合教学逻辑的方法，能够有效解决数学应用题的自动化视觉生成问题，具有更广泛的问题覆盖能力和更高的资产生成扩展性。

Abstract: Visual representations can help lower-primary learners understand Math Word Problems, but generating classroom-usable visuals remains difficult. Existing symbolic systems are controllable but limited in coverage, while end-to-end text-to-image systems often fail to satisfy exact mathematical constraints. This paper presents a symbolic visual generation framework for lower-primary MWP generation with broader problem coverage and more scalable asset generation. The framework includes an LLM-based routing layer, three worksheet-oriented generation modules, and two fallback mechanisms for open-world SVG asset acquisition. A human evaluation comparing Math2Visual-X with Stable Diffusion XL, Nano Banana, and GPT Image showed that the proposed method achieved the strongest overall performance. The results indicate that the framework offers a scalable and pedagogically grounded approach for automatic MWP visual generation.

</details>


### [29] [DOA-SORT: Directional Occlusion-Aware Multi-Object Tracking with Distributional Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22706)
*Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 \ours{} (Directional Occlusion-Aware SORT) 的在线免训练多目标跟踪器，通过显式建模遮挡引起的方向性观测偏差（左、右、上、下），显著提升了 DanceTrack 数据集上的关联质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动主导型跟踪器通常将遮挡视为标量惩罚，忽略了遮挡造成的方向性观测偏差：不同方向的遮挡会导致检测框的位置和形状发生不同程度的扭曲，现有方法未能捕捉这一特性。

Method: 1. 推断前后顺序并估计方向性遮挡覆盖范围和深度；2. 构建由一个干净观测分量和四个方向性遮挡观测分量组成的混合模型；3. 使用包含中心、面积、置信度和纵横比的五维观测向量，并根据预测的遮挡和检测置信度自适应调整观测噪声；4. 在关联和轨迹恢复中使用方向性混合似然，并结合模糊惩罚和局部顺序一致性交换减少身份错误。

Result: 在 DanceTrack 验证集上，相比 OA-SORT，HOTA 从 63.00 提升至 66.34，AssA 从 45.10 提升至 49.57，IDF1 从 62.19 提升至 65.28。性能提升主要集中在关联质量上，检测精度保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式建模方向性遮挡偏差，有效解决了部分遮挡和检测波动导致的身份关联问题，在无 Re-ID 协议下展现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Identity association in multi-object tracking (MOT) is vulnerable to partial occlusion, truncated detections, and fluctuating confidence scores. Existing motion-dominant trackers commonly represent occlusion as a scalar penalty. This treatment misses the directional observation bias caused by occlusion: left, right, top, and bottom occlusions distort the location and shape of a detection in different ways. We propose \ours{} (Directional Occlusion-Aware SORT), an online and training-free tracker that models these biases explicitly. First, it infers a soft front--back ordering from box overlap and relative bottom positions, and estimates directional occlusion coverage and depth. It then constructs a mixture of one clean and four directional occlusion observation components. The model uses a five-dimensional observation comprising box center, area, confidence, and aspect ratio, and adapts observation noise to predicted occlusion and detection confidence. The directional mixture likelihood is used in high-confidence association, low-confidence association, and track recovery; ambiguity penalties and local order-consistency swaps further reduce identity errors among nearby objects. On the DanceTrack validation split, \ours{} improves HOTA from 63.00 to 66.34, AssA from 45.10 to 49.57, and IDF1 from 62.19 to 65.28 over OA-SORT with the same detector and evaluation protocol. The gains are concentrated in association quality while detection accuracy remains stable. Additional local evaluations on MOT17 and MOT20 train splits characterize cross-dataset behavior under the same no-ReID tracking protocol.

</details>


### [30] [PanoSeg3R: Feed-Forward 3D Semantic Segmentation for Panoramic Images with an Automatic Data Curation Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22687)
*Heechan Yoon,Dongki Jung,Phuc Nguyen,Ming Lin,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoSeg3R是一个前馈式3D全景语义分割框架，通过单次前向传播联合预测3D几何和多视角语义。它基于预训练重建主干和查询式掩码解码器，并利用自动化全景数据策展管道生成伪标注以增强零样本泛化能力，在ScanNet++等数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多针对透视输入设计，缺乏能直接处理全景输入并高效进行3D几何与语义联合预测的前馈式框架；同时，高质量全景3D标注数据稀缺限制了模型性能及零样本泛化能力。

Method: 提出PanoSeg3R框架，包含支持全景图像的预训练重建主干网络和一个查询式掩码解码器，实现单次前向传播下的3D几何与多视角语义联合预测；引入自动全景数据策展管道，利用现成基础模型的互补优势生成可靠的伪语义标注，扩展训练数据。

Result: 在ScanNet++上3D mIoU提升高达16.02；使用策展后的训练数据后，在Stanford2D3D和ToF-360上的零样本性能分别提升了4.26和43.28 mIoU，达到当前最佳水平。

Conclusion: PanoSeg3R通过高效的单步前馈架构和大规模伪标注数据增强策略，显著提升了3D全景语义分割的精度和零样本泛化能力，为全景3D理解提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We present PanoSeg3R, a feed-forward framework for 3D panoramic semantic segmentation. Unlike existing methods designed for perspective inputs, PanoSeg3R jointly predicts 3D geometry and multi-view semantic segmentation in one single forward pass. Built upon a pretrained reconstruction backbone that supports panoramic images, our approach extends feed-forward 3D reconstruction with a query-based mask decoder. Furthermore, we introduce an automatic panorama data curation pipeline that leverages the complementary strengths of off-the-shelf foundation models to generate reliable pseudo semantic annotations, substantially expanding the training data and improving zero-shot generalization. PanoSeg3R achieves state-of-the-art performance on panoramic 3D semantic segmentation, improving 3D mIoU by up to 16.02 on ScanNet++, while the curated training data further improves zero-shot performance by up to 4.26 and 43.28 mIoU on Stanford2D3D and ToF-360, respectively. Website: https://harryyoon777.github.io/PanoSeg3R/

</details>


### [31] [Vision2CAD: A Visual Agent Harness for Explicit Geometry Referencing and Localization in Parametric CAD Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22688)
*Xi Cheng,Chenxi Zhai,Hang Cheng,Mingyu Fan,Pingfa Feng,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vision2CAD，一种结合视觉语言模型推理与确定性CAD内核操作的视觉智能体框架，通过ID接口、坐标转换和投影边定位解决参数化建模中的几何参考选择与约束建立难题，并在GERD数据集上显著提升了生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化CAD模型生成方法在几何参考选择、草图平面局部坐标解释以及向外部几何投影建立草图约束方面面临挑战，导致难以保证几何准确性和特征依赖关系的稳定性。

Method: 提出Vision2CAD框架，整合VLM推理与确定性CAD操作；引入基于ID的接口以支持显式几何选择，设计局部坐标桥接器将视图坐标转换为草图坐标，并利用投影边定位支持外部草图约束；同时构建Geometry Explicit Reference Dataset (GERD)以对齐每一步建模的命令、几何状态及ID。

Result: 在GERD-EVL和DeepCAD测试子集上，Vision2CAD分别将mIoU提升11.1%和5.6%，并将Chamfer距离降低17.3%和41.8%；参数编辑实验和消融研究证实了参数依赖关系的有效保留。

Conclusion: Vision2CAD通过视觉-语言推理与确定性强化的结合，有效解决了参数化建模中的关键几何与约束问题，实现了更高精度的CAD模型生成并保持了稳定的特征依赖关系。

Abstract: Generating parametric CAD models requires accurate geometry and stable feature dependencies. Existing methods face challenges in selecting geometric references, interpreting sketch-plane local coordinates, and establishing sketch constraints to projected external geometry. We present Vision2CAD, a visual agent harness that combines vision-language model (VLM) reasoning with deterministic CAD kernel operations. An ID-based interface supports explicit geometry selection, a local-coordinate bridge converts view coordinates into sketch coordinates, and projected-edge localization supports external sketch constraints. These mechanisms establish feature dependencies within the supported modeling operations and constraint types. We also introduce the Geometry Explicit Reference Dataset (GERD), which aligned commands, geometry states and IDs at every modeling step. On GERD-EVL and a DeepCAD test subset, Vision2CAD improves mIoU by 11.1\% and 5.6\% and reduces Chamfer distance by 17.3\% and 41.8\%, respectively. Parameter-editing experiments and ablation studies further proved the preservation of parametric dependencies.

</details>


### [32] [ZIL: Zero-shot Image-to-LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22716)
*Zijun Li,Xiaotian Sun,Xuelun Shen,Yao Dai,Sheng Ao,Yangyang Shi,Jakob Engel,Zhipeng Cai,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ZIL，首个用于零样本非同步图像到LiDAR配准的基础模型。通过Vision和Point Transformer编码输入，联合回归相对位姿与预测3D坐标，显著提升精度。在7个公开数据集（1.4M帧）上训练，无需额外标注即可实现跨域泛化，较SOTA方法大幅降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进（SOTA）方法主要存在两个局限：1) 多假设同帧输入，难以处理远距离帧的图像和点云；2) 依赖特定领域训练，无法泛化到未见场景。旨在解决这些问题，实现零样本、非同步条件下的鲁棒配准。

Method: 提出ZIL基础模型，使用Vision Transformer和Point Transformer分别编码图像和点云。除了回归相对位姿外，还学习预测3D坐标以提高精度。采用混合数据训练，并对相机内参和LiDAR垂直轴原点进行归一化处理，以实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在5个域内和零样本基准测试中，ZIL显著优于先前SOTA方法，单模型即能保持一致性高性能。翻译误差和旋转误差最高分别减少87%和76%。

Conclusion: ZIL作为首个零样本非同步图像到LiDAR配准基础模型，通过联合位姿回归与3D坐标预测及数据归一化策略，有效解决了跨帧和跨域泛化问题，性能显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Image-to-LiDAR registration estimates the camera pose of an image with respect to a LiDAR point cloud. It has diverse applications in autonomous driving, robot navigation etc. However, state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods still 1) mostly assume same-frame inputs, struggling with the image and point cloud from distant frames; 2) rely on domain-specific training, failing to generalize to unseen scenarios. We propose ZIL, the first foundation model for zero-shot non-synchronized image-to-LiDAR registration. ZIL encodes the input image and point cloud with the Vision and Point Transformers. In addition to regressing the relative pose, ZIL also learns to predict 3D coordinates, which substantially improves the pose accuracy without additional annotations. Interestingly, naive mix-data training cannot enable zero-shot generalization, which requires normalization on both camera intrinsics and the LiDAR vertical-axis origin. Trained on 7 public datasets with 1.4M LiDAR frames, ZIL consistently and significantly outperforms previous SOTA with a single model across 5 in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, reducing the translation and rotation errors by up to 87% and 76% (shown in Fig. 1). Code and models are available at https://github.com/ZijunLi7/ZIL.

</details>


### [33] [MM-ContextFold: Context Folding for Multimodal Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23121)
*Yang Tian,Fan Liu,Jingyuan Zhang,Zhenyang Li,Yupeng Hu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MM-ContextFold框架，通过动态加载图像和文本摘要折叠机制，解决多模态代理检索中的上下文爆炸问题，显著提升准确率并降低上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct等多模态代理框架将原始多模态输入和交互历史保留在单一不断增长的上下文中，导致上下文爆炸。虽然已有方法压缩冗余文本，但针对高token视觉内容的管理策略仍缺乏有效探索。

Method: 基于对约10,000条轨迹的系统实证研究（发现持续保留原始图像会增加输出熵并降低任务准确率），提出训练-free的MM-ContextFold框架。该框架仅按需加载原始图像：维护一个用于高层规划的纯文本主上下文，并为依赖图像的子任务生成临时分支上下文。在分支中完成子任务后，将结果折叠为简洁文本摘要回主上下文，随即丢弃图像和分支痕迹。

Result: 在五个骨干模型上的七个多模态代理检索基准测试中，相比ReAct方法，平均准确率提升6.3个百分点，同时工作上下文长度减少27.5%。

Conclusion: MM-ContextFold通过解耦主上下文与分支上下文，有效解决了视觉内容冗余导致的上下文膨胀问题，实现了更高效的推理和更高的任务准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal Agentic Retrieval (MAR) requires agents to solve complex information-seeking tasks by iteratively invoking external tools. Typical frameworks such as ReAct maintain raw multimodal inputs and the accumulating interaction history in a single, ever-growing context, leading to the context explosion problem. While existing methods alleviate this issue by compressing redundant text, effective strategies for managing token-intensive visual content remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we first conduct a systematic empirical study of approximately 10,000 trajectories. The results show that as visual cues are progressively extracted through external tools and textualized into the context, raw images become increasingly redundant. Continued image retention is associated with higher output entropy and can even degrade task accuracy. Motivated by these findings, we propose MM-ContextFold, a training-free framework that loads raw images only when needed. It maintains a persistent, text-only main context for high-level planning and spawns ephemeral branch contexts for image-dependent subtasks. Within each branch, the agent loads the relevant images, completes the subtask, and folds the result back into the main context as a concise textual summary; the images and branch trace are then discarded. Experiments on seven MAR benchmarks across five backbone models show that MM-ContextFold improves average accuracy by 6.3 percentage points over ReAct while reducing the working context length by 27.5\%.

</details>


### [34] [Towards Robust Classroom Attendance: A Comprehensive Evaluation of Face Detection and Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22750)
*Himani Trivedi,Hiren Patel,Ridham Patel,Krutika Patel,Nancy Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Visage Face数据集以解决课堂人脸识别难题，FaceLiVTv2-M模型在检测与识别任务中表现最优（准确率99.75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统考勤方式效率低且易出错；现有面部识别数据集多针对受控环境设计，无法反映教室中光照变化、角度差异及遮挡等真实挑战。

Method: 构建包含16,234张样本的Visage Face数据集（涵盖多角度、多变光照、不同表情及部分遮挡）；基于YOLO进行人脸检测，并测试七种先进人脸识别模型共十三种配置。

Result: FaceLiVTv2-M模型表现最佳，Top-1/Top-5准确率达到99.75%，推理时间为6.459毫秒。

Conclusion: Visage Face数据集是一个贴近现实且具有挑战性的课堂人脸识别基准，验证了所提方案的有效性。

Abstract: Manual attendance methods, such as paper or register-based systems, take a lot of time, can lead to errors, and are easy to falsify. Face recognition is more reliable, but it frequently struggles in classrooms because lighting and other conditions can vary. Face recognition datasets are designed for regulated environments and do not capture the actual challenges found in classrooms. To address this, a new face detection and recognition dataset, the Visage Face dataset, comprising 16,234 face samples, is proposed for the task of face detection and recognition. The photos are taken from different angles and under varying lighting conditions, with students showing a range of expressions, and some faces partly covered to reflect real-life situations. A YOLO-based system is used to detect faces and tested seven advanced face recognition models with thirteen configurations: LVFace, QCFace, FaceLiVTv2, TopoFR, EdgeFace, TransFace, and GhostFaceNets. Of these, FaceLiVTv2-M performed best, with 99.75% Top-1/Top-5 accuracy and an inference time of 6.459 ms. These results show that the Visage Face Dataset is a realistic and challenging benchmark for face recognition in classroom attendance.

</details>


### [35] [DriveReferee: Geometric Safety Verdicts Need Not Be Learned for Driving World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22762)
*Fengcheng Yu,Dhruv Parikh,Junjie Ye,Maulik Bhatt,Thang Vu,Igor Vasiljevic,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveReferee，通过从摄像头观测中学习几何特征以预测场景状态，并直接应用显式几何规则（如碰撞检测）来评估生成轨迹的安全性，无需学习具体的判决模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成世界-动作模型（WAMs）主要依赖专家模仿优化动作分支，缺乏对生成轨迹的闭环几何验证。现有闭环评估器需要部署时不可用的特权场景状态，而通过学习传感器特征来构建验证器的方法存在局限。

Method: 引入DriveReferee，利用学习的几何读取模块从摄像头观测中预测场景表示，直接执行显式的几何安全规则（而非学习规则本身）。训练时，基于真实状态评分自采样轨迹并将偏好蒸馏至WAM策略；部署时，基于预测状态评估生成轨迹并在必要时选择更安全的替代方案。

Result: 在匹配候选者和推理预算下，DriveReferee的性能持平或优于所有学习型验证器和启发式基线。研究表明，给定相同的预测状态和轨迹，学习判决结果并未带来可测量的下游收益，且需大量标注数据。在NAVSIM navtest上，仅使用单目视觉输入且无外部训练数据的情况下，达到92.02 PDMS得分。

Conclusion: 显式几何规则结合场景状态预测比端到端学习验证器更有效、更高效，为自动驾驶中的轨迹安全性验证提供了无需特定判决训练的可靠方案。

Abstract: Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and vehicle actions, while their action branches remain primarily optimized by expert imitation. Yet imitation provides no explicit closed-loop geometric verdict for generated trajectories, making verification important during both training and deployment. Closed-loop evaluators can check collision and drivable-area violations, but require privileged scene state unavailable at deployment. Existing approaches often close this gap by learning a verifier from sensor features. For these geometric checks, the rule itself is explicit. For example, collision is determined by whether the rolled-out ego footprint overlaps occupied vehicle space. What is unavailable at deployment is the scene state needed to apply the rule. We introduce DriveReferee, which uses a learned geometry readout to predict the scene representation from camera observations and executes the geometric safety rule directly rather than learning it. The resulting analytic referee evaluates collision and drivable-area safety from a scene state and candidate trajectory. During training, it scores self-sampled trajectories on ground-truth state and distills the resulting preferences into the WAM policy. At deployment, the same referee evaluates generated trajectories on this predicted state and selects a safer alternative when needed. The analytic referee requires no verdict-specific training, and its decisions follow an explicit geometric rule. Under matched candidates and inference budgets, it matches or outperforms all learned-verifier and heuristic baselines. Given the same predicted state and trajectory, learning the verdict provides no measurable downstream gain despite requiring tens of thousands of evaluator-labeled training examples. On the full NAVSIM navtest, DriveReferee reaches 92.02 PDMS with single-camera visual input and no external training data.

</details>


### [36] [Human-Level Accuracy, Non-Human Strategies: Revealing Model-Human Divergence in Video Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22788)
*Fanhong Li,Shurui Zheng,Zi Yin,Junbo Cui,Lei Ji,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过分布评估框架对比视频基础模型与人类在物理推理任务上的表现，发现尽管V-JEPA2等模型准确率接近人类，但其决策策略与人类存在显著分歧，主要依赖场景统计规律而非显式前向模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型在物理推理基准测试中已达到人类水平准确率，但仅凭准确率无法区分模型是进行了类似人类的“前向模拟”，还是利用了可见场景中的“统计规律性”。需要一种能揭示模型内部策略差异的评估方法。

Method: 提出分布评估框架，将模型种子和人类评审员视为群体，比较共识度、不确定性和策略。在Physion基准上评估V-JEPA2、VideoMAEv2和DINOv2三种ViT-L架构，分析模型-人类分歧及归因特征。

Result: V-JEPA2准确率（73.2%）接近人类（74.2%），但模型-人类分歧率（26.4%）远高于人类-人类分歧率（4.8%）。模型在几何推理上优于人类，但在引力动力学和因果链推理上劣于人类。所有架构均表现出非人类策略，且不可观测结果特征比可见场景属性更能预测这种分歧。

Conclusion: 视频基础模型并未执行类似人类的前向模拟，而是更依赖场景级的统计规律性。这种系统性策略分歧无法仅通过准确率衡量，需采用分布评估框架进行深层诊断。

Abstract: Video foundation models now reach human-level accuracy on physical-reasoning benchmarks, yet such tasks require predicting unobserved physical outcomes. Do these models perform human-like forward simulation, or do they exploit statistical regularities in visible scenes? Accuracy alone cannot distinguish these strategies. We introduce a distributional evaluation framework that treats model seeds and human raters as populations, enabling comparison of consensus, uncertainty, and strategy. On the Physion benchmark, we evaluate three ViT-L architectures (V-JEPA2, VideoMAEv2, DINOv2). V-JEPA2 narrows the accuracy gap to ~1 percentage point (73.2% vs. 74.2%), yet model-human disagreement reaches 26.4%, far exceeding human-human disagreement (4.8%), with substantially lower agreement (kappa ~ 0.48 vs. 0.91).
