### [71] [MinCU: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Grounded Minimal-Change Understanding in Image Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23336)
*Chaoqian Mu,Wenhao Wu,Zichen Liang,Jiaxu Li,Lijun Wang,Yifan Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MinCU基准和SG-ISA方法，解决多模态大模型在细微图像差异定位与描述上的不足，显著提升联合性能并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅孤立评估语义比较或单图定位，缺乏对忠实描述与物理定位联合能力的评估；细粒度差异定位是多模态大模型的关键但未充分探索的能力。

Method: 1. 构建MinCU基准：包含仅含单一原子变化（类别、属性、数量或位置）的图像对，评估模型的描述、定位及实体识别能力。2. 提出SG-ISA方法：结构化自回归方法，采用“思考-定位-描述”序列，通过语义提示和离散空间锚点作为隐式定位脚手架，生成描述与边界框。

Result: 最强闭源模型及R1风格推理模型在MinCU上表现不佳，难以同时产出准确描述和定位框。SG-ISA微调相比传统思维链方法，在定位准确性和描述质量上实现显著联合提升，并将推理Token开销降低约26%。

Conclusion: 隐式中间空间接口比单纯依赖模型规模更能有效支持 grounded dual-image understanding（接地双图像理解）。

Abstract: Localizing and describing fine-grained differences between near-identical images is a critical yet underexplored capability for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing benchmarks largely assess semantic comparison or single-image grounding in isolation, without jointly requiring faithful description and physical localization. To bridge this gap, we introduce MinCU, a benchmark for grounded minimal-change understanding, where each sample consists of an image pair differing by a single atomic variation in object category, attribute, count, or spatial position, and models are evaluated on their ability to describe the change, localize the changed regions, and identify the changed entity. We further propose Semantic-Guided Implicit Spatial Anchors (SG-ISA), a structured autoregressive method that decomposes prediction into a Think-Locate-Describe sequence. SG-ISA first predicts a semantic cue for the changed concept, then uses discrete spatial anchors as an implicit localization scaffold, and finally generates the change description together with the grounding box. Experiments reveal that even the strongest closed-source MLLMs and recent R1-style reasoning models struggle on MinCU, with most failing to jointly produce accurate descriptions and grounding boxes. Compared to the previous chain-of-thought method, fine-tuning with SG-ISA yields substantial joint improvements in grounding accuracy and description quality while reducing reasoning-token overhead by approximately 26%. These results suggest that an implicit intermediate spatial interface can be more effective than relying solely on model scale for grounded dual-image understanding.

</details>


### [72] [AniPrO: Interpretable Anime Image Provenance Detection via Multi-Dimensional Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23345)
*Yan Liu,Baoxiang Huang,Zi'an Wang,Wenbo Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AniPrO框架，针对动漫风格图像构建包含15,000个样本的多维度描述数据集及基准测试，旨在解决现有检测器忽略动漫特有线索的问题，提升对真人绘制、AI修复和文生图图像的溯源与鉴别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在动漫图像创作中广泛应用，版权归属和内容治理亟需区分真人绘制、AI修复及文生图图像。现有检测器多聚焦真实照片，忽视了动漫特有的扁平化上色、夸张结构和线条控制等特征，存在技术空白。

Method: 基于AnimeDL-2M候选池，筛选构建AniPrO数据集（覆盖Real、Inpainting、Text2Image三类，共15,000个平衡样本），并提供结构化五维描述。同时建立AniPrO-SFD-Bench和AniPrO-MFR-Bench两个基准，分别从统计特征判别和多模态融合推理角度评估溯源检测性能。

Result: 实验表明，结构化语义引导揭示了系统性的AI生成偏差（如全局视觉合理性与局部细节连贯性之间的差距），显著提升了包括高难度修复样本在内的检测准确率。

Conclusion: AniPrO通过引入多维度描述和多基准评估，有效弥补了动漫图像溯源领域的不足，为理解AI生成偏差和优化内容治理提供了可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly used in anime-style image creation, distinguishing human-drawn, AI-inpainted, and text-to-image images is important for copyright attribution, visual provenance, and content governance. Existing AI-generated image detectors mainly target real-world photographs and often overlook anime-specific cues such as flat coloring, exaggerated structures, and artistic line control. To address this gap, we propose AniPrO, a multi-dimensional description-enhanced framework for interpretable anime image provenance. Built upon AnimeDL-2M, AniPrO contains 15,000 balanced samples from a 35,000-image candidate pool, covering Real, Inpainting, and Text2Image categories with structured five-dimensional descriptions. We further introduce AniPrO-SFD-Bench and AniPrO-MFR-Bench to evaluate provenance detection from statistical feature discrimination and multimodal fusion reasoning perspectives. Experiments show that structured semantic guidance reveals systematic AI-generation biases, such as the gap between global visual plausibility and local detail coherence, and improves the detection of challenging inpainting samples. The dataset and code will be released at: https://github.com/YAN-LIU05/AniPrO.

</details>


### [73] [BiView-Touch: Learning Bimanual Tactile Representations by Cross-Hand Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23352)
*Chenxin Liang,Youchen Lai,Chuqiao Lyu,Tianxing Chen,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiView-Touch框架，利用双手触觉数据的互补性，通过掩码重建和对比学习挖掘跨手时空依赖关系，显著提升低标签下的触觉表征学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉表征学习方法主要独立建模双手或仅将双手信息用于下游预测，忽视了双手间存在的交叉手（cross-hand）结构与互补性。

Method: 1. 构建BiView-Touch框架，从可见的目标手区域和对侧同步完整手部数据中补全掩码的目标手潜在特征；2. 使用几何条件方向解码器的学生编码器预测完整视图EMA潜在目标；3. 引入时序和布局反事实机制以增强对同步及解剖学组织信息的敏感性；4. 发布BVT-20数据集进行多任务验证。

Result: 在HumanTouch数据集上，冻结表征在低标签设置下优于基线方法：仅用5%标签时，双侧腕部运动识别平衡准确率提升7.1%，力驱动交互阶段识别提升14.1%；具备跨记录会话、预训练语料库及未见双侧任务的迁移能力。

Conclusion: BiView-Touch成功学习了基于时间对齐和解剖学组织的结构化跨手依赖性，而非仅仅受益于双输入本身，证明了利用双手触觉互补结构的有效性。

Abstract: Bimanual interaction produces complementary tactile views of the same physical process, yet existing tactile representation learning largely models the two hands independently or combines them only for downstream prediction, leaving their cross-hand relationship unexplored. To exploit this overlooked structure, we introduce BiView-Touch, a tactile-only framework that completes masked target-hand latents from the remaining visible target-hand regions and the synchronized full contralateral hand. A student encoder with a geometry-conditioned directional decoder predicts full-view EMA latent targets, while temporal and layout counterfactuals encourage sensitivity to synchronized and anatomically organized source information. Controlled ablations and source-context interventions show that BiView-Touch learns structured cross-hand dependence on temporally aligned and anatomically organized contralateral tactile context, rather than benefiting from bilateral input alone. On the public HumanTouch dataset, its frozen representations consistently outperform representative self-supervised baselines across low-label settings. With only 5\% downstream labels, BiView-Touch achieves relative balanced-accuracy gains of 7.1\% on bilateral wrist-motion recognition and 14.1\% on force-derived interaction-phase recognition. We further introduce BVT-20, a 20-task bilateral tactile dataset, and demonstrate transfer across recording sessions and pretraining corpora, including transfer to a held-out bimanual task. Our code and dataset details are available on the anonymous project page: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/biview-touch-review-site-050C/.

</details>


### [74] [The Right Future for Action: Learning Action-Relevant Predictive States in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23369)
*Qiwen Gu,Jifan Li,Bingjie Gao,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ARPS（动作相关预测状态），通过训练视频特征以预测未来视觉变化，在推理时仅利用当前观测生成的紧凑预测状态进行动作控制，显著提升了泛化能力和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成式世界模型（WAMs）在推理阶段使用内部视频特征进行控制时，缺乏明确表征目标的问题；发现可预测的未来变化并不等同于线性解码动作的便利性，且线性可读的动作信息具有空间集中性。

Method: 构建ARPS作为视频与动作专家间的紧凑预测接口：1) 使用时域条件状态预测器聚合中间视频特征为紧凑状态；2) 采用未来表征监督，训练状态的不同部分预测不同未来时刻的视觉表征及其相对于当前的变化；3) 推理时移除监督分支，仅使用基于当前观测计算的预测状态。

Result: 消融实验证明未来监督显著改善了分布偏移下的泛化能力；在LIBERO基准上达到99.2%的成功率，无需适应即可迁移至LIBERO-Plus（87.3%），性能超过Fast-WAM达39.2个百分点。

Conclusion: ARPS通过分离训练时的未来预测监督与推理时的即时状态利用，有效解决了生成式模型的控制表征模糊问题，实现了高效、高鲁棒性的机器人动作控制。

Abstract: Generation-free world action models (WAMs) retain future-video prediction during training but act from internal video features at inference, leaving unclear what these features should preserve for control. Our representation diagnostics show that representations with more predictable future changes need not make linear action decoding easier. Observed future changes provide additional action information beyond the present, and linearly readable action information is spatially concentrated. These findings motivate Action-Relevant Predictive States (ARPS), a compact predictive interface between the video and action experts. ARPS uses a horizon-conditioned state predictor to aggregate intermediate video features into a compact state that supplies all visual context to the action expert. Future-representation supervision trains different parts of this state to predict visual representations at different future times, together with their changes relative to the present. At inference, the supervision branch is removed, and the action expert only uses the learned predictive state computed from current observations. Controlled ablations show that future supervision substantially improves generalization under distribution shift. ARPS achieves 99.2% success on LIBERO and transfers to LIBERO-Plus without adaptation, reaching 87.3% and exceeding Fast-WAM by 39.2 percentage points.

</details>


### [75] [Vision-Wireless Fusion for Multi-User Localization: A Cross-Modal Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23372)
*Can Zheng,Jiguang He,Guofa Cai,Henk Wymeersch,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于导频索引信道状态信息（CSI）与视觉融合的框架，用于解决复杂城市环境中多用户定位的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的城市环境中，无线测量易受噪声、遮挡和多径效应影响而产生歧义；虽然视觉观测能提供互补的空间上下文，但如何有效融合两者以实现准确的多用户定位仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建视觉-无线融合框架，利用正交导频索引在CSI令牌序列中保留用户身份。模型将每个导频索引的CSI观测编码为查询令牌，通过交叉注意力从空间视觉记忆中检索特定用户信息；同时利用CSI令牌间的自注意力捕捉用户间交互，最终生成多模态表示进行逐用户定位。

Result: 在不同数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能上持续优于基于模型的基线、仅使用CSI的基线以及通用的多模态融合基线。进一步实验验证了该模型在不同无线和视觉条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过结合正交导频索引CSI与视觉空间记忆，并运用交叉注意力和自注意力机制处理多模态数据，该框架能有效提升复杂环境下多用户定位的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate multi-user localization is challenging in complex urban environments, where wireless measurements can become ambiguous under noise, blockage, and multipath, while visual observations provide complementary spatial context. This paper presents a vision-wireless fusion framework for multi-user localization using pilot-indexed channel state information (CSI). Orthogonal pilot indices preserve the identities of the communicating UEs in the CSI token sequence and localization outputs. The model encodes each pilot-indexed CSI observation as a query token and uses cross-attention to retrieve user-specific information from spatial visual memory. Self-attention among CSI tokens further captures inter-user interactions, while the resulting multimodal representations are used for user-wise localization. Experiments on different datasets show consistent improvements over model-based, CSI-only, and multimodal-fusion baselines. Further experiments evaluate the model under different wireless and visual conditions.

</details>


### [76] [LiteTex-GS: Fast and Lightweight Texturing for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23380)
*Zhiwei Li,Yijia Guo,Yishi Lu,Liwen Hu,Hong Rao,Shengbo Chen,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteTex-GS，一种轻量级高斯纹理化框架，通过紧凑初始化、自适应分辨率分配和剪枝策略，在保持渲染质量的同时显著降低内存和优化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D高斯方法虽能缓解高频细节重现问题，但离散高斯与连续2D网格间的结构鸿沟导致复杂的参数化和严重的计算开销，破坏了高斯溅射原有的效率优势，亟需解决详细纹理化与计算敏捷性之间的平衡问题。

Method: 1. 紧凑初始化：为每个高斯分配最小局部纹理，仅对重建误差显著的基元逐步分配更高分辨率；2. 贡献与面积感知剪枝：消除低效用高斯以维持简洁几何骨架；3. 分辨率感知更新规则：缓解纹理上采样导致的梯度稀释，确保快速稳定收敛。

Result: 在标准新视角合成基准测试中，该方法实现了具有竞争力或更优的渲染质量，同时相比现有的纹理化高斯基线方法，使用了显著更少的参数和更短的训练时间。

Conclusion: LiteTex-GS有效解决了纹理化高斯溅射中的计算效率瓶颈，证明了通过动态资源分配和高效剪枝可以在保证高质量渲染的同时实现轻量化和高效率。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time novel view synthesis, but its tightly coupled geometry and appearance representation often require a large number of primitives to reproduce high-frequency texture details, leading to substantial memory and optimization costs. Recent textured 2D Gaussian methods alleviate this limitation by attaching texture maps to Gaussian primitives. However, bridging the fundamental structural gap between discrete Gaussians and continuous 2D grids requires complex parameterizations that introduce severe computational overhead. This overhead fundamentally compromises the original efficiency of Gaussian Splatting, making the balance between detailed texturing and computational agility an unresolved challenge. To address these challenges, we propose LiteTex-GS, a fast and lightweight texturing framework for Gaussian Splatting. Our method initializes an extremely compact representation, assigning minimal local texture to each Gaussian and progressively allocates higher resolution only to primitives with significant reconstruction errors. To maintain a streamlined geometric scaffold, we introduce a contribution- and area-aware pruning strategy that eliminates low-utility Gaussians. Furthermore, to mitigate the gradient dilution caused by texture upsampling, we design a resolution-aware update rule that preserves rapid and stable convergence. Extensive experiments on standard novel view synthesis benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves competitive or superior rendering quality while using substantially fewer parameters and less training time than existing textured Gaussian baselines.

</details>


### [77] [ProxyBuild: Text-Guided Structured 3D Building Generation with Mesh-Anchored Procedural Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23386)
*Xiang Tang,Ruotong Li,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProxyBuild框架，通过Mesh-Anchored Procedural Proxy (MAPP)中间表示和解耦生成流程，实现从文本到可编辑、结构清晰3D建筑的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型输出不可分割的单网格或无交互渲染；程序化建模虽可生成层级结构建筑，但规则编写繁琐，且结合LLM仍难以解决几何约束下的程序化规则问题。

Method: 1. 引入MAPP作为中间表示，将建筑组件锚定在几何壳上，解耦为代理预测和代理到资产实例化两阶段；2. 构建含MAPP标注的数据集，训练面-边大双图编码器以推断面与边的语义角色；3. 结合LLM解析的文本风格和属性参数，整合空间放置逻辑与硬约束，实现高精度资产检索与组装。

Result: 显著缓解过度平滑、组件碰撞和结构损坏等问题；能从多样来源准确解析无语义壳；在多项指标上优于基线方法，能稳健生成结构清晰、细节丰富且支持后续编辑的3D建筑。

Conclusion: ProxyBuild为VR和数字孪生等下游应用提供了可靠且具交互性的内容基础。

Abstract: Text-guided 3D building generation holds tremendous application potential, yet existing generative models typically output inseparable single meshes or non-interactive rendered representations. While procedural modeling can generate editable buildings with hierarchical structures, rule authoring is laborious, and even with the aid of large language models (LLMs), it remains challenging to effectively solve procedural rules under geometric constraints. In this paper, we propose ProxyBuild, a novel hybrid framework for structured building generation. We introduce the Mesh-Anchored Procedural Proxy (MAPP) as a novel intermediate representation, which tightly anchors building components onto geometric shells, thereby decoupling the generation task into two phases: proxy prediction and proxy-to-asset instantiation. First, we construct a building dataset with MAPP annotations to train our designed face-edge bigraph encoder. By explicitly modeling the feature interactions of topological elements on heterogeneous mesh graphs, this encoder accurately infers the semantic roles of faces and edges. Subsequently, conditioned on textual styles and attribute parameters parsed by LLMs, we accomplish high-precision asset retrieval and assembly by integrating a spatial placement logic with hard constraints. Extensive experiments show that ProxyBuild not only significantly mitigates common issues in building generation such as over-smoothing, component collisions, and structural corruptions, but also accurately parses semantic-free shells from diverse sources. Outperforming prior baselines across various metrics, our method can robustly generate structurally clear, detail-rich, and post-editable 3D buildings from text, thereby providing a reliable and interactive content foundation for downstream applications such as virtual reality and digital twins.

</details>


### [78] [Enhancing Shrimp Disease Detection via Deep Learning and Data Refinement for Resilient Aquaculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23397)
*Vinh Canh-Thanh Truong,Hai-Binh Pham,Ngoc Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次将视觉Transformer (ViT) 和自监督学习 (SSL) 应用于对虾疾病检测，通过监督迁移学习和对比学习框架（SimCLR），在4,348张图像的数据集上实现了96%和85%的分类准确率，有效解决了数据标注挑战和性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决水产养殖中对虾疾病导致的巨大损失，以及现有自动化检测方法面临的性能瓶颈和数据标注困难问题。

Method: 提出了两种深度学习流水线：1. 监督迁移学习方法，利用ImageNet预训练的ViT-Small/16和EfficientNet骨干网络；2. 自监督对比学习框架（SimCLR），使用ViT-Small编码器从无标签图像中提取鲁棒特征，随后进行微调。目标分类为四种状态：健康、黑鳃病(BG)、白斑综合征病毒(WSSV)及两者混合感染。

Result: 监督方法达到96%的准确率并快速收敛，优于传统通用模型；自监督(SSL)方法在无大量标签的情况下也达到了85%的高竞争力验证准确率，建立了可持续水产养殖监测的新基准。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了ViT和SSL在对虾疾病检测中的有效性，为解决水产养殖中的病害监控难题提供了高性能且数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Shrimp diseases continue to cause devastating losses in the aquaculture industry, driving a critical need for robust, automated detection. This work contributes the first application of Vision Transformers (ViT) and Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) to the shrimp farming domain, addressing both performance bottlenecks and data labeling challenges. We propose two deep learning pipelines to classify four key diseases: Healthy, Black Gill (BG), White Spot Syndrome Virus (WSSV), and a co-infection of both using a dataset of 4,348 images. First, our supervised transfer-learning approach leverages ImageNet-pretrained ViT-Small/16 and EfficientNet backbones. Second, we introduce a contrastive learning framework (SimCLR) with a ViT-Small encoder to extract robust representations from unlabeled images prior to fine-tuning. Our results establish strong new baselines for sustainable aquaculture monitoring. The supervised approach achieves an outstanding 96% accuracy with fast convergence, outperforming traditional generic models, while the label-efficient SSL approach reaches a highly competitive 85% validation accuracy.

</details>


### [79] [ScaleBlind: Point Cloud Completion under Unknown Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23404)
*Shenghui Wu,Chen Wang,Yuan Feng,Guangshun Wei,Yuanfeng Zhou,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaleBlind框架，利用2D基础模型先验实现无需真实世界尺度（GT-scale）的点云补全，解决现有方法依赖特权信息的缺陷，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的点云补全方法在训练和测试时隐式依赖真实世界尺度（GT-scale）进行归一化，这在现实推理中不可用，导致部署受限及产生严重伪影（如过度/欠补全、嵌套壳）。观察到2D图像生成基础模型具备强大的几何理解和多视图一致性生成能力，可作为无尺度3D补全的有力先验。

Method: 提出ScaleBlind框架：1) 从渲染的局部视图“梦想”出完整的多视图外观；2) 将推断出的缺失区域提升回3D以获得几何感知的粗略补全；3) 通过强大的跨模态融合网络与原始局部点云进一步细化。该方法消除对GT-scale信息的依赖，建立2D生成先验与3D点云补全之间的桥梁。

Result: 实验证明该框架优越性，ScaleBlind成为点云补全任务的新最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 利用2D基础模型先验可有效解决点云补全中对真实尺度的依赖问题，实现更实用的3D形状重建，并在性能上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Point cloud completion aims to infer a complete 3D shape from a partial point cloud and serves as a fundamental building block for downstream tasks such as reconstruction, editing, and simulation. Despite the recent progress, existing learning-based methods often implicitly rely on access to the ground-truth shape scale (GT-scale) during both training- and testing-time normalization, assuming privileged information that is unavailable in real-world inference. This hidden assumption limits practical deployment and can lead to severe completion artifacts, e.g., over- or under-completion and nested shells, once the oracle GT-scale cue is removed. We observe that the recent foundation image generation models exhibit a strong capability of understanding objects and geometries, and producing multi-view consistent renderings, making them promising priors for GT-scale-free 3D completion. Motivated by this insight, we propose ScaleBlind, a novel framework that leverages foundation-model-based image completion to recover global scale directly from partial inputs and then faithfully produces the 3D completion. Specifically, ScaleBlind dreams out complete multi-view appearances from rendered partial views, lifts the inferred missing regions back into 3D to obtain a geometry-aware coarse completion, and further refines it via a powerful cross-modal fusion network with the original partial point cloud. By harnessing 2D foundation priors, our method eliminates the need for accessing GT-scale information at inference. Moreover, it provides a principled bridge between 2D generative priors and 3D point cloud completion. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our framework, making ScaleBlind the new state-of-the-art for the point cloud completion task.

</details>


### [80] [Accurate Motion Estimation with Bézier Control Point for Efficient Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23408)
*Shuhao Han,Chenyang Wu,Chun-Le Guo,Zheng-Peng Duan,Zhen Li,Ming-Ming Cheng,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ABC-Inter算法，通过贝塞尔控制点和解耦特征估计模块解决帧间插值中的运动模糊和非均匀运动建模问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有帧插值模型因训练集运动模糊假设和推理时均匀运动假设，导致生成中间帧模糊且不准确。

Method: 1. 设计精确光流估计模块（AFM），通过解耦双帧特征并映射坐标以优化光流估计；2. 引入贝塞尔控制点，利用输入帧和中间真值帧计算，消除训练集运动模糊；3. 在推理阶段，利用多帧和多重光流直接计算贝塞尔控制点以建模非均匀运动，无需重训；4. 第二阶段微调贝塞尔控制点估计模块，实现仅用两帧的非线性运动估计。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到最先进（SOTA）性能，并具有出色的视觉感知质量。

Conclusion: ABC-Inter通过改进光流估计和引入贝塞尔控制点有效解决了帧插值中的模糊和非均匀运动难题，提升了生成帧的准确性和视觉效果。

Abstract: In frame interpolation tasks, motion ambiguity in the training set causes models to generate blurry intermediate frames. Moreover, the assumption of uniform motion between frames during inference further leads to inaccuracies in the generated intermediate frames. To tackle these challenges, we propose an Accurate motion estimation algorithm with Bézier Control point, ABC-Inter, for efficient frame Interpolation. Specifically, ABC-Inter designs an Accurate Flow estimation Module (AFM) by decoupling two-frame features and mapping to corresponding coordinates to better estimate the optical flow between the two frames. Furthermore, ABC-Inter eliminates motion ambiguity in the training set by introducing Bézier control points that are computed using the input frames and the intermediate ground-truth (gt) frames. This allows the model to estimate accurate optical flow between two frames during the training process, thereby solving the blurriness problem in the generated intermediate frames during inference. Benefiting from the more accurate flow estimation between two frames, we can introduce additional frames and directly use multiple flows to calculate Bézier control points for modeling non-uniform motion without retraining the model. Simultaneously, to realize the estimation of non-linear motion using only two frames, we also introduce a new Bézier control point estimation module which achieves better motion estimation between the two frames by performing fine-tuning on the model in the second stage. Experimental results demonstrate that our ABC-Inter achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmark datasets and exhibits excellent visual perception.

</details>


### [81] [Retrieval Geometry Shapes Cache-Based Clip Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23409)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Md. Samiul Alim,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Meharun Nesa,Mohammad Abu Yousuf,Alex Lamb,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出缓存测试时适应（TTA）的性能高度依赖于检索空间。通过实验发现，使用DINOv2作为检索编码器比ImageNet-A/MAE等显著提升增益。据此提出MARC系统，结合CLIP预测与DINOv2检索，以低成本实现高效、鲁棒的零样本分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于缓存的测试时适应方法通常固定特征空间进行图像-图像检索，忽视了检索空间本身对适应效果的影响程度。需要探究如何优化检索空间以提升模型在分布偏移数据上的表现。

Method: 1. 控制变量实验：固定内存库，仅改变检索编码器，评估不同检索空间（如CLIP, MAE, DINOv2等）对缓存增益的影响；
2. 提出MARC（Memory Augmented Retrieval for CLIP）：一种免训练系统，使用冻结的CLIP进行预测，DINOv2-B进行检索，并通过单一融合权重结合两者结果。

Result: 1. 检索空间选择至关重要：相同内存下，DINOv2-L在ImageNet-A上增益高达+19.7点，远超其他编码器；
2. MARC性能优越：单视图缓存修复错误数（1074±21）优于64视图集成（878±4），且成本仅为后者的约1/7；
3. 在四个ImageNet分布偏移任务中，MARC平均OOD准确率达67.91%，在匹配参数量和视图数下优于图基缓存系统（64.17% vs 62.75%），且速度快2.6倍。

Conclusion: 检索空间是构建鲁棒缓存式测试时适应系统的首要设计要素。MARC证明了结合高质量检索编码器（如DINOv2）与预训练模型（如CLIP）可在保持低计算成本的同时，显著提升远程传感、科学成像及视觉环境变化场景下的模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cache-based test-time adaptation improves CLIP predictions by storing and retrieving examples from the target stream while keeping the model frozen. However, existing methods largely treat the feature space used for image-image retrieval as fixed, leaving open how much adaptation depends on the retrieval space itself. We study this question by fixing the memory and changing only the retrieval encoder, finding that the same memory can yield very different gains: across sixteen retrieval spaces, ImageNet-A cache gain ranges from at most +0.44 points for CLIP and MAE to +19.7 +/- 0.4 for DINOv2-L, while label-free retrieval-space selection retains 98% of oracle gain on ImageNet-V2. These results show that memory quality depends not only on which examples are stored, but also on how they are retrieved. Motivated by this finding, we propose MARC (Memory Augmented Retrieval for CLIP), a training-free system that uses frozen CLIP for prediction and DINOv2-B for retrieval with a single fusion weight. A single-view cache repairs 1074 +/- 21 baseline errors, compared with 878 +/- 4 for a 64-view ensemble, at roughly one seventh of the cost. Across four ImageNet distribution shifts, MARC reaches a 67.91% OOD average and, at matched DINOv2-B scale and eight views, achieves 64.17 +/- 0.31% versus 62.75 +/- 0.15% for a graph-based cache system while running 2.6 times faster. Overall, our results establish retrieval space as a first-order design choice for robust cache-based adaptation in remote sensing, scientific imaging, and changing visual environments.

</details>


### [82] [Semi-automated reconstruction of indoor geometry from 360-degree video for CFD-based airflow analysis in classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23425)
*Dhruv Gamdha,James Afful,Shambhavi Joshi,Ulrike Passe,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种半自动化工作流，通过单张360度视频重建可编辑的CFD模拟几何模型，解决大规模建筑通风评估中几何获取成本高、场景分解难及布局重构繁琐三大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算流体动力学（CFD）在评估室内通风和污染物传输方面应用广泛，但受限于高昂的扫描硬件成本、手动CAD建模耗时以及难以快速重构物体布局以进行对比研究，限制了其规模化部署。

Method: 1. 使用神经辐射场（NeRF）从360度视频重建密集点云；2. 利用文本提示SAM 3分割生成2D实例掩膜，通过多视图一致性和深度带过滤提升为3D；3. 使用八叉树分离物体实例，并通过连通图修复遮挡空隙；4. 采用迭代最近点（ICP）拟合椅子模板，程序化生成桌子几何；5. 提供基于浏览器的编辑器用于质量控制和布局配置；6. 使用稳态雷诺平均OpenFOAM求解器驱动瞬态被动标量传输模拟。

Result: 该工作流在消费级工作站上每房间仅需2-5小时即可将捕捉数据转化为几何模型。在教室控制实验中发现，随着家具增加，半清除时间呈非单调变化；跨房间比较显示清除行为不可外推，证实了逐室几何获取的必要性。该方法成功应用于两个大学教室和一个阶梯礼堂。

Conclusion: 该低成本的几何获取工作流使得针对教室等空间的基于几何解析的比较通风研究变得切实可行，解决了传统方法在成本和灵活性上的局限。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) is widely used to evaluate ventilation and contaminant transport in occupied buildings, but deployment at scale is limited by three bottlenecks: acquiring room geometry without costly scanning hardware or manual CAD modeling, decomposing the scene into individually manipulable objects, and reconfiguring those objects for alternative layouts without re-capturing the room. We present a semi-automated workflow that converts a single 360-degree video of a room into individually editable, simulation-ready geometry assets. A dense point cloud is reconstructed using Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), and 2D instance masks from text-prompted SAM 3 segmentation are lifted to 3D using multi-view consensus and depth-band filtering. Points are separated into object instances with an octree, and occlusion gaps are healed with a connectivity graph. Chair templates are fitted by Iterative Closest Point (ICP) alignment, and table geometry is generated procedurally. A browser-based editor supports quality assurance and rapid construction of alternative layout configurations. A steady Reynolds-averaged OpenFOAM solution then drives transient passive-scalar transport; the setup is verified using a mesh-sensitivity study and validated against an IEA Annex 20 benchmark. We apply the workflow to two university classrooms and a tiered lecture-hall auditorium. The capture-to-geometry pass takes two to five hours per room on a consumer workstation. In a controlled obstruction sequence in one classroom, the modeled half-clearance time varies non-monotonically as furniture is added, and a cross-room comparison indicates that clearance behavior cannot be reliably extrapolated between rooms, motivating per-room geometry acquisition. By making that acquisition low-cost, the workflow makes geometry-resolved comparative ventilation studies practical for spaces such as classrooms.

</details>


### [83] [RSPDBench: Benchmarking Vision Foundation Models on Earth Observation Tasks Under Physically Grounded Remote-Sensing Product Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23427)
*Tanjim Bin Faruk,Khondaker Masfiq Reza,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RSPDBench基准，通过物理真实的遥感产品退化（而非通用图像噪声）评估视觉基础模型的鲁棒性，发现复合退化导致显著性能下降，证明现有评估方法不足以反映实际部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对地球观测（EO）视觉基础模型的评估多基于干净下游基准，但实际运营中的遥感产品本身包含空间、辐射、对齐、噪声及协调等缺陷。通用图像损坏或广泛域偏移评估无法隔离这些产品级故障模式，导致对模型实际鲁棒性的误判。

Method: 构建RSPDBench基准，包含5个EO数据集、7个基础模型和2个监督基线。采用经过审计的原始退化和复合产品链进行评估，以各模型在干净状态下的原生协议为基准，衡量其在面对结构化缺陷时的性能下降幅度。

Result: 退化敏感性具有强结构性：分辨率条件和通道分组编码器保护不同的故障轴；同一物理缺陷可能对不同模型产生相反影响。复合退化链暴露出孤立退化无法预测的失败模式，存在模型依赖的放大、饱和或组件主导现象，性能额外下降最高达38个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅靠干净准确率或通用扰动测试无法充分表征EO鲁棒性。必须针对遥感产品带入部署的结构化缺陷进行测量，才能真实反映模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision foundation models targeting Earth observation (EO) tasks are commonly evaluated on clean downstream benchmarks, but operational EO products can already contain spatial, radiometric, alignment, noise, and harmonization defects before reaching the model. Existing robustness evaluations often use generic image corruptions or broad domain shifts, which do not isolate these product-level failure modes. We introduce \textbf{RSPDBench}, a physically grounded \textbf{r}emote-\textbf{s}ensing-\textbf{p}roduct \textbf{d}egradation \textbf{b}enchmark for vision foundation models. RSPDBench evaluates five EO datasets, seven foundation-model entries, and two supervised baselines under audited primitive degradations and compound product chains. Each model is evaluated under its clean-selected native protocol, with robustness measured as the drop from its own clean baseline. Our analysis reveals that degradation sensitivity is strongly structured: resolution-conditioned and channel-grouped encoders protect different failure axes, and the same physical defect can hurt one model while helping another. Compound chains expose failures that isolated degradations do not predict, with model-dependent amplification, saturation, or component dominance, and excess drops up to $38$ percentage points beyond the strongest component. These results show that EO robustness cannot be characterized by clean accuracy or generic perturbation tests alone; it must also be measured against the structured defects that remote-sensing products carry into deployment.

</details>


### [84] [HOIBlender: Blending Lightweight Detection with Vision-Language Priors for Efficient Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23431)
*Junwen Chen,Keiji Yanai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HOIBlender，一种高效的人-物交互检测器。通过融合检测器视觉token、空间推理和BLIP-2语义先验，在轻量级解码管道中实现高性能，无需重型编码器或后处理校准，显著减少训练时间并提升长尾类别识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI检测方法虽精度提升，但依赖重型Transformer编码器、复杂去噪调度或事后语义校准，导致计算开销大、效率低。旨在解决严重长尾监督下的检测效率与精度平衡问题，简化架构以提升推理速度和参数预算友好性。

Method: 构建基于RF-DETR/LW-DETR风格及DINOv2主干的HOIBlender。核心设计包括：1）从多尺度投影器直接选取Top-K图像条件token作为候选，移除专用编码器阶段；2）双阶段解码器：先稳定人-物几何结构，再通过渐进式BLIP-2先验融合进行动词和HOI分类；3）利用BLIP-2文本嵌入初始化分类器权重以应对长尾类别；4）采用分组查询训练优化且不影响推理成本。

Result: 在Nano、Small、2XL三种模型规模下，HOIBlender在HICO-DET数据集上均优于SOV-STG-VLA和Hybrid-SOV-VLA。特别是在仅9个训练周期的情况下，达到44.49 Default Full mAP，同时保持具有竞争力的延迟和参数量。

Conclusion: 轻量级检测、结构化空间-语义解码与深度集成的视觉-语言先验可融合为单一高效HOI管道，证明了简化架构结合强大先验在提升效率和性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires grounding an interacting human-object pair and recognizing the verb that links them, often under severe long-tail supervision. Recent methods improve accuracy with stronger detectors and vision-language priors, but many still stack heavy transformer encoders, intricate denoising schedules, or post-hoc semantic calibration on top of the detector. We present \textbf{HOIBlender}, an efficient HOI detector named after its core design principle: blending detector-grounded visual tokens, spatial subject-object reasoning, and BLIP-2 semantic priors inside one lightweight decoding pipeline. HOIBlender builds on an RF-DETR/LW-DETR-style foundation with a DINOv2 backbone and selects top-$K$ image-conditioned tokens directly from the multi-scale projector as subject and object candidates, removing the dedicated encoder stage retained by prior HOI methods. A dual-stage decoder first stabilizes human-object geometry and then performs verb and HOI classification through progressive BLIP-2 prior fusion, with classifier weights initialized from BLIP-2 text embeddings for long-tail categories. Grouped-query training further enriches optimization without increasing inference cost. Across three model scales (Nano, Small, 2XL), HOIBlender consistently outperforms SOV-STG-VLA and Hybrid-SOV-VLA on HICO-DET, reaching $44.49$ Default Full mAP in only $9$ training epochs while maintaining competitive latency and parameter budgets. These results show that lightweight detection, structured spatial-semantic decoding, and deeply integrated vision-language priors can be blended into a single efficient HOI pipeline.

</details>


### [85] [GAPS: Generative Active Pseudo-view Selection for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23436)
*Hongfei Zhu,Haochen Deng,Sitao Zhang,Ling Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合预训练扩散模型与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的交替优化框架，通过生成几何一致性的伪视图解决稀疏视角下3DGS存在的伪影和几何断裂问题。引入GAPS策略平衡重建信息量与生成可靠性，并配合不确定性过滤和DropGaussian正则化提升鲁棒性，在LLFF和Mip-NeRF 360数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏观测下的新视角合成存在严重约束不足的问题。尽管3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）支持实时渲染，但在少视角训练时会产生浮动伪影、几何破碎和背景褪色等问题。

Method: 1. 使用预训练图像扩散模型生成几何一致的伪视图以增强3DGS监督；2. 通过深度条件ControlNet、IP-Adapter风格迁移、LoRA场景适应和img2img结构锚定约束生成过程；3. 提出生成式主动伪视图选择（GAPS），采用退火调度从保守插值过渡到探索性外推；4. 引入双准则准入门控和不确定性加权损失拒绝不可靠生成；5. 使用密度自适应DropGaussian减少复杂场景中的过拟合。

Result: 在LLFF数据集（3/6/9视角）上，平均PSNR分别比原始3DGS提升0.40/0.89/0.70 dB；在Mip-NeRF 360数据集（12/24视角）上提升1.18/0.80 dB。所有设置下SSIM提升且LPIPS降低。消融实验证明主动选择和密度自适应正则化的必要性，完整方法在无限360度场景中能将LPIPS降至无伪视图基线以下。

Conclusion: 该交替优化框架有效解决了稀疏视角下3DGS的几何不一致性问题，通过结合扩散模型生成能力和主动视图选择策略，显著提升了新视角合成的质量和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse observations is severely under-constrained. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time rendering, it produces floaters, broken geometry, and washed-out backgrounds when trained with few views. We propose an alternating optimization framework that uses a pre-trained image diffusion model to generate geometrically consistent pseudo-views for additional 3DGS supervision. Generation is constrained by depth-conditioned ControlNet, IP-Adapter style transfer, LoRA scene adaptation, and img2img structural anchoring. We introduce Generative Active Pseudo-view Selection (GAPS) to balance reconstruction informativeness and generative reliability when choosing target views. Its annealing schedule shifts from conservative interpolation early in training to exploratory extrapolation later, gradually covering unobserved regions. A dual-criterion admission gate and uncertainty-weighted losses reject unreliable generations, while density-adaptive DropGaussian reduces overfitting in complex scenes. On LLFF with 3/6/9 views, our method improves average PSNR over vanilla 3DGS by 0.40/0.89/0.70 dB. On Mip-NeRF 360 with 12/24 views, the gains are 1.18/0.80 dB. SSIM improves and LPIPS decreases in every setting. Ablations show that active selection and density-adaptive regularization are both necessary; only the full method reduces LPIPS below the no-pseudo-view baseline on unbounded 360-degree scenes.

</details>


### [86] [PhysReflect: Geometry and Perception Guided Diffusion for Physically-Plausible Mirror Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23442)
*Shuheng Ge,Hongwei Ren,Li Zhang,Xiangqian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysReflect：一种通过几何与感知引导的扩散框架，解决镜像反射中的物理不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的镜像反射常存在几何畸变（如位置偏移、方向错位等），且传统方法仅依赖潜在空间噪声重建损失，无法直接施加针对反射的几何和感知约束。

Method: 提出PhysReflect框架，在训练每一步将预测的干净潜在变量解码为像素空间，并通过两个可微目标应用退火监督：1) 几何损失：利用SAM2-based TwinTrack机制进行边界感知监督，通过稀疏极线对应和密集边界投影对齐强制空间一致性；2) 感知损失：结合语义一致性损失（基于DINOv2特征保持身份和外观）和光照一致性损失（基于单目几何先验正则化深度、表面法线和照明）。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中，PhysReflect在几何、感知和物理合理性指标以及定性视觉效果上均优于现有的镜像反射方法。

Conclusion: PhysReflect通过显式的几何和感知监督，有效提升了生成图像中镜像反射的物理合理性和视觉质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-quality images, yet often violate the physical laws governing mirror reflections. Reflections often suffer from geometric aberrations, including positional offsets, directional misalignment, proportional imbalance, and structural distortion. These failures remain evident even in contemporary state-of-the-art generative systems. Existing methods itigate this problem through synthetic data scaling or auxiliary depth conditioning, yet their merely reliance on latent-space noise reconstruction losses as implicit supervision prevents direct enforcement of reflection-specific geometric and perceptual constraints. To bridge this gap, we present PhysReflect, a geometry and perception guided diffusion framework that decodes the predicted clean latent into pixel space at each training step and applies annealed supervision through two complementary differentiable objectives. The Geometric Loss enforces mirror-induced spatial consistency through sparse epipolar correspondence and dense boundary projection alignment, where a SAM2-based TwinTrack mechanism provides stable in-mirror localization for boundary-aware supervision. The Perceptual Loss preserves reflected appearance by combining Semantic Consistency Loss, which maintains reflected identity and appearance via DINOv2 features, and Lighting Consistency Loss, which regularizes depth, surface-normal, and illumination coherence under monocular geometry priors. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that PhysReflect outperforms prior mirror-reflection methods in geometric, perceptual, and physical-plausibility metrics, as well as qualitative visual results.

</details>


### [87] [Rule-Constrained Assignment for Cue-Ball Identification in Broadcast Snooker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23450)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Yuezhong Wu,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于规则约束的分配问题模型来解决广播斯诺克中的母球识别难题，通过联合分配候选对象到已知库存（1个母球、最多15个红球和6个彩球），显著提高了识别准确率（从88.1%提升至95.5%）及后续指标分析的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统独立评估每个候选对象并与固定白色原型比对，在广播环境下易受光照变化（使彩球显白）、球员或设备遮挡/干扰的影响，导致母球识别错误。

Method: 将母球识别建模为规则约束的分配问题，联合检测到的候选对象与受限的斯诺克库存（1个母球、至多15个红球、6个已知位置的彩球）。通过计算将候选对象分配给“白色槽位”的增量成本来选择母球，传统外观测试作为单槽位情况下的特例。

Result: 在419手标 shots 上，身份识别准确率从88.1%提升至95.5%，在保留场地测试中从80.5%提升至95.2%。在CueLift指标恢复系统中，覆盖范围从36.2%提升至55.4%（基于6,241个可计分shots）。在另一组2,529个shots的独立评估中也保持了这一优势。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了广播环境下的光照和遮挡干扰问题，显著提升了母球识别精度和指标分析的覆盖率，优于传统独立评估方法。

Abstract: Accurate cue-ball identification is essential for metric analysis of broadcast snooker. Existing systems evaluate each candidate independently against a fixed white prototype and reject candidates above an appearance threshold. Under broadcast conditions, illumination changes can make colored balls appear white, while intrusions from players and equipment can obscure the cue ball or introduce competing candidates. We formulate cue-ball identification as a rule-constrained assignment problem that jointly assigns detected candidates to the bounded snooker inventory: one cue ball, up to 15 reds, and six colors with known spots. The cue ball is selected by the incremental cost of assigning each candidate to the white slot, and the conventional appearance test follows as the one-slot case. On 419 hand-annotated shots, our method improves identity accuracy from 88.1% to 95.5%, and from 80.5% to 95.2% on held-out venues. Within CueLift, our metric state-recovery system, the assignment expands coverage from 36.2% to 55.4% over 6,241 scorable shots. Assigning an estimate to every shot in a separate evaluation on 2,529 shots preserves this advantage.

</details>


### [88] [Algebraic Consistency Alone Does Not Certify Temporal Structure in Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23478)
*Di Wen,Ruodi Zhang,Kailun Yang,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文质疑了近期潜在动作模型通过代数约束（可加性、反对称性）来证明其捕捉到时间结构的结论。研究发现，误差降低主要源于解码器的重构特性而非真正的时间结构学习，且破坏配对后模型仍能保持低误差，下游任务也未显示优势。作者建议采用更严格的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期方法声称通过对潜在动作代码施加可加性和反对称性正则化，能显著减少错误，并以此作为代码捕获时间结构的无标签证书。然而，这一结论的合理性受到质疑，需要验证这种误差减少是否真的反映了时间结构的学习，还是仅仅是重构偏差的结果。

Method: 1. 分析现有方法的代数约束与重构之间的关系；2. 在五个源域上对比训练后未受约束模型与训练锚点的表现；3. 测试在时间配对被破坏后重新训练的受限模型性能；4. 评估潜在代码在LIBERO-GOAL和LIBERO-SPATIAL下游任务中的优势；5. 测试违反对比目标（violation-contrastive objective）的效果；6. 提出包含基线校正指标、配对破坏重训练和种子预算分析的验证协议。

Result: 1. 重建过程驱动解码过渡趋向于状态特征差，任何配对都满足所述代数恒等式，度量无法区分真实时间结构与无关状态或坐标约定；2. 未受约束模型已能达到83-97%的误差减少比例；3. 残余误差更多由解码器家族决定而非学习内容；4. 时间配对被破坏后重新训练的受限模型在每类域上的误差仍低于实数数据上的未受约束模型；5. 保留时间配对在下游任务中无一致优势，且随着代数误差改善，线性动作解码能力下降；6. 违反对比目标仅在重构预算内产生边际分离效果。

Conclusion: 现有方法关于代数约束捕获时间结构的结论不成立，误差降低主要由解码器特性驱动。作者推荐缺乏的验证协议包括：基线校正指标、对破坏配对进行重训练以及种子预算分析，以提供更可靠的评估。

Abstract: Latent action models infer a code for the transition between two frames of action-free video. Recent methods regularise this code to compose additively and reverse antisymmetrically, and report order-of-magnitude reductions in the resulting errors as a label-free certificate that the code has captured temporal structure. We show that this conclusion does not follow. Reconstruction drives the decoded transition toward a difference of state features, for which both identities hold for any pairing, a solution the metric cannot distinguish from one encoding nuisance state or a coordinate convention. Across five source domains, a trained but unconstrained counterpart already achieves 83-97% of the reduction relative to an untrained anchor. The residual fold is governed as much by the decoder family as by what is learned. A constrained model retrained after its temporal pairing is destroyed still reaches, in each domain, a lower error than the unconstrained model on real data. Downstream, preserving the temporal pairing yields no consistent advantage on LIBERO-GOAL or LIBERO-SPATIAL, and across the tested arms the code's mean linear action decodability falls as the algebraic error improves. We also test the most direct repair, a violation-contrastive objective that requires the algebra to fail on destroyed pairings: in the tested configurations it yields only a marginal separation within the reconstruction budget, on training and test triples alike. We recommend a validation protocol that these methods currently lack: a baseline-corrected metric, retraining on destroyed pairings, and a seed-budget analysis.

</details>


### [89] [CE$^4$L: Continual Ego, Exo, and Ego-Exo Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23492)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Yuchen Liu,Qingyu Shi,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个统一的多视角持续学习基准CE⁴L及高效基线方法VISTA，解决具身智能中多视角连续学习的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习研究多局限于单外部视角(exo-only)，无法反映具身智能在真实世界中同时发生任务切换和视角变化（ego/exo）下的行为表现。

Method: 构建包含四种任务的CE⁴L基准；提出视频增量子空间路由任务适配器(VISTA)，通过轻量级适配器存储任务特定更新，并利用基于二阶统计量的残差距离进行免训练路由。

Result: VISTA在多视角设置下保持竞争力并达到最先进(SOTA)的整体性能；实验揭示了不同持续学习方法在CE⁴L设置下效果的显著差异。

Conclusion: CE⁴L填补了多视角连续学习空白，VISTA证明了参数高效方法在处理跨视图对应、异步性和异构语义目标方面的有效性。

Abstract: Perception for embodied agents is video-based, often multi-view (ego, exo, or both), and inherently continual, with simultaneous task and viewpoint shifts. Yet continual learning (CL) remains dominated by exo-only recognition tasks, obscuring behavior under these real-world coupled shifts. We introduce Continual Ego, E}xo, and Ego-Exo Learning (CE$^4$L), a unified multi-view CL benchmark spanning four representative tasks: cross-view referenced skill assessment, temporal action segmentation, cross-view association, and action anticipation & planning. CE$^4$L highlights challenges largely absent in prior CL benchmarks, including cross-view correspondence, view-dependent asynchrony, and heterogeneous semantic objectives. To this end, we propose Video Incremental Subspace-routed Task Adapters (VISTA), a parameter-efficient baseline method that stores task-specific updates in lightweight adapters and performs training-free routing via residual distance to task-specific whitened subspaces estimated from second-order statistics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the significantly varied efficacy of representative CL methods across CE$^4$L settings, while VISTA is consistently competitive and achieves state-of-the-art overall performance. Our source code for benchmarks and methods is available at https://github.com/AnAppleCore/CE4L .

</details>


### [90] [Pay More Attention To Text In High-Resolution MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23495)
*Zhongkuan Mao,Wenzhuo Zhao,Xianjie Liu,Yidong Wang,Zhao Gao,Ronghao Xian,Yao Jiang,Yi Zhang,Liangjian Wen,Keren Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviSpec，一种无需训练的编译器，通过生成互补的证据规范来解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在高分辨率推理中的瓶颈。实验证明，明确指定视觉证据比单纯扩展视觉访问更有效，在多个基准测试上实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率MLLMs的失败常被归因于视觉问题，导致人们依赖缩放、裁剪等视觉干预手段。然而研究表明相关视觉证据已编码在中间表示中，且仅改进视觉侧并不足以解决问题。研究旨在探究是否文本引导视觉搜索存在未被发现的瓶颈，特别是问题表述未能准确指定所需定位的视觉证据。

Method: 引入EviSpec，这是一种无需训练的编译器。它能在保留原始问题进行最终推理的同时，推导出互补的证据规范。通过匹配控制的实验设计，隔离了“证据规范”与“定位”各自的作用，以验证其有效性。

Result: 1. 在搜索预算固定的情况下，结构化的证据规范相比通用请求带来了8.6%的相对增益。2. 在证据几何结构匹配的情况下，EviSpec定位的证据相比随机证据带来了14.8%的相对增益。3. 在五个MLLMs上，EviSpec在V*Bench、HR-Bench-4K和HR-Bench-8K三个基准上分别实现了平均10.4%、8.8%和12.4%的相对增益。4. 在VQA和幻觉专注型基准测试中也达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 研究的控制实验证明了将收益归功于“指定寻找何种证据”而非仅仅“扩展视觉访问”。EviSpec通过解决语言层面的瓶颈，显著提升了MLLMs在高分辨率推理及通用视觉问答任务中的性能。

Abstract: Failures of high-resolution MLLMs are commonly attributed to a visual problem, motivating zooming, cropping, and related visual interventions to recover fine-grained evidence or suppress interference. Yet recent studies suggest that relevant visual evidence is already encoded in intermediate representations, indicating that visual-side improvements alone insufficient. This raises a natural question: does the remaining bottleneck lie in the text that guides visual search? We identify a previously overlooked linguistic bottleneck: questions formulated for answering do not necessarily specify the visual evidence required for localization. To address this mismatch, we introduce EviSpec, a training-free compiler that derives complementary evidence specifications while preserving the original question for final reasoning. We further validate it through matched-control experiments that isolate the roles of evidence specification and localization. With the search budget fixed, structured evidence specifications yield an 8.6% relative gain over generic requests. With evidence geometry matched, the evidence localized by EviSpec yields a 14.8% relative gain over random evidence. Together, these controls isolate the benefit of specifying what evidence to seek rather than merely expanding visual access. Across all five MLLMs, EviSpec consistently improves upon the corresponding baseline on each of the three benchmarks, yielding average relative gains of \textbf{10.4%, 8.8%, and 12.4%} on V\textsuperscript{*}Bench, HR-Bench-4K, and HR-Bench-8K, respectively. Beyond high-resolution reasoning, EviSpec also achieves state-of-the-art performance on VQA and hallucination-focused benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [Detecting Phone-Induced Pedestrian Distraction via a Multimodal Fusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23507)
*Yuanzhe Li,Hounian Liu,Xiaotong Chang,Yidi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态融合Transformer（MFT），通过结合骨骼动态和视觉外观特征，利用交叉模态注意力和时序注意力模块，实现对手机诱导行人分心的高精度检测，在287个实例的数据集上达到95%的整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着手机依赖增加，行人因使用手机导致的分心行为日益普遍，成为交通事故的重要诱因。可靠的分心检测对于提升自动驾驶车辆的情境感知能力、支持及时风险评估及安全运动规划至关重要。

Method: 提出多模态融合Transformer（MFT）。该方法联合提取身体姿态关键点中的骨骼动态特征和行人图像中的视觉外观特征；引入交叉模态注意力模块，通过多头交叉注意力捕捉模态间依赖关系以实现互补信息的有效融合；随后采用基于Transformer编码器的时序注意力融合模块来捕获时序依赖。

Result: 在包含287个行人实例、共20,741张图像的标注数据集上进行训练和评估。实验表明，MFT整体准确率达到95%，比六种基线方法的性能高出6%。

Conclusion: MFT通过有效融合骨骼动态与视觉外观的多模态互补信息，显著提升了手机诱导行人分心检测的性能，为自动驾驶系统的安全决策提供了更可靠的感知支持。

Abstract: The increasing reliance on mobile phones has made phone-induced pedestrian distraction increasingly prevalent. Activities such as texting, watching videos, and making phone calls have become significant contributors to traffic accidents. Reliable detection of pedestrian distraction is essential for autonomous vehicles, as it improves situational awareness and enables timely risk assessment, thereby supporting safe motion planning and vehicle control. We propose a multimodal fusion Transformer (MFT) for detecting phone-induced pedestrian distraction. MFT jointly extracts skeletal dynamics from body pose keypoints and visual appearance features from pedestrian images, effectively leveraging the complementary information provided by the two modalities. A cross-modal attention module is proposed to capture inter-modal dependencies through multi-head cross-attention, facilitating effective fusion of complementary information across the two modalities. Then, a temporal attention fusion module, implemented with a Transformer encoder, is employed to capture temporal dependencies. MFT is trained and evaluated on a manually annotated dataset comprising 287 pedestrian instances with 20,741 images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MFT attains an overall accuracy of 95%, exceeding the performance of six baseline approaches by 6%.

</details>


### [92] [GARO: Geometry-Aware Redundancy Optimization for Real-Time and High-Fidelity Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23509)
*Huiwen Xue,Kaixing Zhao,Zuheng Ming,Tingcheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GARO框架，通过几何感知冗余优化减少高斯点数量，在保持渲染质量（PSNR稳定）的同时将动态场景重建的渲染速度提升2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可变形高斯泼溅方法虽然能实现高保真度的动态场景建模，但由于存在大量冗余高斯点，导致内存占用过高且渲染效率受限，难以满足虚拟现实等应用对高渲染速度的需求。

Method: 提出几何感知冗余优化（GARO）框架，集成于传统动态场景重建流程的自适应密度控制阶段。该方法首先通过优化活动评估策略筛选低梯度候选点，随后利用低曲率分析评估几何复杂度，从而进一步过滤并剪枝冗余点，生成紧凑且表现力强的 Gaussian 表示。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，GARO 实现了质量与速度之间的稳健权衡：峰值信噪比（PSNR）保持稳定，同时渲染速度提升了2倍，验证了其效率和有效性。

Conclusion: GARO 有效解决了动态场景重建中高斯点冗余导致的内存和效率问题，为需要高渲染速度的应用提供了高效、高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis is a key task for dynamic scene reconstruction, where high rendering speed is essential for applications such as virtual reality. Existing deformable Gaussian Splatting methods achieve high-fidelity dynamic scene modeling, but still face limitations in memory usage and rendering efficiency due to the large number of redundant Gaussians. To address these challenges, we propose Geometry-Aware Redundancy Optimization (GARO), a unified redundancy measurement framework in the adaptive density control stage of the traditional dynamic scene reconstruction pipeline. This framework first selects low-gradient candidates using an optimization activity assessment strategy, and then evaluates geometric complexity through low curvature analysis to further filter and prune redundant points, resulting in a compact and expressive Gaussian representation. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that GARO achieves robust trade-offs between quality and speed, with PSNR remaining stable and rendering speed improved by 2x, validating the efficiency and effectiveness of GARO.

</details>


### [93] [GeoBalance: Geometry-Aware Monitoring and Reconstruction with Asymmetric Optimization for Balanced Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23533)
*Zechang Xiong,Da Li,Rong Yin,Kexin Tang,Biao Yang,Pengyuan Li,Wenkang Kong,Yulan Hu,Shengyu Zhu,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoBalance框架，解决多模态分类中的“流形模态坍缩”（MMC）问题。通过监测几何特性并仅在必要时重构弱模态表示，结合Simplex-ETF支架和谱正则化恢复类间可分性，利用非对称梯度投影避免冲突，显著提升多模态基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平衡方法主要将模态不平衡视为优化问题，假设弱模态仅未充分优化但表征完整。然而研究发现，持续的模态主导会导致弱模态在表征层面发生坍缩，即“流形模态坍缩”（MMC），表现为类内方向减少和类间可分性降低，这一现象未被现有方法有效处理。

Method: 提出GeoBalance框架：1) 监测弱模态的两个几何属性以检测MMC；2) 触发时，使用固定的Simplex-ETF类支架和谱正则化重构弱模态表示，以恢复类分离并防止坍缩；3) 采用非对称梯度投影移除与重构冲突的联合梯度分量，保留非冲突优化部分，以维持联合训练中的重构效果。

Result: 在六个多模态基准数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明GeoBalance显著优于现有的竞争性平衡方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 流形模态坍缩是多模态学习中的一个关键表征问题，GeoBalance通过几何感知的重构策略和非对称梯度投影，有效解决了该问题，提升了多模态分类器的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal classifiers can converge to modality-dominant solutions in which one modality dominates the joint prediction, suppressing the learning of others. Existing balancing methods mainly adjust losses, gradients, or modality contributions, largely treating modality imbalance as an optimization problem while implicitly treating the weak modality as under-optimized but representationally intact. In this work, we find that this assumption does not always hold, as persistent modality dominance can induce a representation-level collapse of the weak modality, which we term \emph{manifold modality collapse} (MMC). MMC manifests as a coupled geometric degradation in which weak-modality representations collapse onto fewer directions within each class and become less separable across classes. Motivated by this observation, we propose \emph{GeoBalance}, a geometry-aware framework that monitors these two geometric properties and reconstructs the weak modality representation only when it exhibits signs of MMC. Once triggered, GeoBalance uses a fixed Simplex-ETF class scaffold and spectral regularization to restore class separation while preventing collapse onto a few feature directions. To preserve reconstruction during joint training, asymmetric gradient projection removes the joint-gradient component conflicting with reconstruction, leaving non-conflicting optimization unchanged. Extensive experiments across six multimodal benchmarks demonstrate great improvements over competitive balancing methods, validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [94] [PosEviLoc: Position-Conditioned Spatial Evidence for Language-Based 3D Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23534)
*Tianyi Shang,Yike Shi,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PosEviLoc框架，通过构建Query-Position Spatial Evidence Field (QSEF) 和 Multi-Level Evidence Readout (MER)，实现更精准、高效的文本到点云3D定位，显著优于现有方法MNCL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言描述的3D定位方法通常将查询和子地图压缩为全局描述符，导致物体级语义模糊及跨描述空间一致性丢失；需要一种能显式利用语义和空间证据、避免依赖全局匹配的方法。

Method: 提出PosEviLoc框架：1) 建模方向关系，由物体位置和假设查询位置共同决定；2) 构建QSEF，在每个假设位置上量化支持查询描述的空间证据比例；3) 使用MER汇总证据生成紧凑表示；4) 通过轻量级MLP计算检索得分。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，PosEviLoc的Recall@1比MNCL平均高出17个百分点；作为即插即用重排序器时，提升MNCL性能平均16个百分点；同时具有更少的参数量和更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: PosEviLoc通过显式的语义和空间证据评估机制，解决了传统全局匹配方法的局限性，实现了更高精度、更低计算开销的粗粒度文本到点云3D定位。

Abstract: Language-based 3D localization retrieves the point-cloud submap containing a target position from descriptions of nearby objects and their spatial relations. Existing methods typically compress queries and submaps into global descriptors, potentially obscuring object-level semantics and cross-description spatial coherence. We propose Position-Conditioned Evidence Localization (PosEviLoc), a query-position-aware framework for coarse text-to-point-cloud localization. Instead of relying on global matching, PosEviLoc evaluates each candidate submap using explicit semantic and spatial evidence. It models direction as a relation jointly determined by an object position and a hypothetical query position. The resulting Query-Position Spatial Evidence Field (QSEF) measures the fraction of query descriptions supported at each hypothetical position, explicitly capturing their agreement without using the ground-truth query pose to construct the evidence field. A Multi-Level Evidence Readout (MER) summarizes this evidence in a compact representation, which a lightweight MLP converts into a retrieval score. Across five benchmarks, PosEviLoc outperforms MNCL by an average of 17 percentage points in Recall@1. When used as a plug-and-play reranker, it improves MNCL by an average of 16 percentage points. Moreover, PosEviLoc introduces substantially fewer parameters and achieves faster inference speed than existing methods.

</details>


### [95] [Towards robust multimodal 3D object detection via visual foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23541)
*Ziying Song,Lin Liu,Hongyu Pan,Shaoqing Xu,Lei Yang,Mingzhe Guo,Caiyan Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoboDistill框架，利用SAM-AD视觉基础模型和KD Fusion知识蒸馏技术，解决自动驾驶多模态3D目标检测在分布外（OOD）扰动下的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在传感器噪声、恶劣天气等导致的分布外（OOD）扰动下缺乏鲁棒性，难以维持稳定的检测性能。

Method: 1. SAM-AD：在自动驾驶图像上微调Segment Anything Model以提取丰富语义特征；2. AD-FPN：构建特征金字塔网络以优化多尺度特征融合；3. DGWA模块：通过深度引导小波注意力抑制高频噪声并保留上下文信息；4. KD Fusion：利用预训练SAM-AD作为教师网络，向轻量级点云网络蒸馏高质量视觉知识。

Result: 在27种具有挑战性的OOD扰动设置下进行广泛实验，RoboDistill在检测性能和鲁棒性方面普遍优于或具备竞争力，超越了现有的代表性最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了视觉基础模型与3D目标检测之间的差距，推动了面向真实世界自动驾驶应用的鲁棒多模态感知技术的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D object detection is fundamental to robust perception in autonomous driving because it integrates complementary information from LiDAR and camera sensors. However, existing methods often fail to maintain robustness under out-of-distribution (OOD) corruptions caused by sensor noise, adverse weather, and environmental changes. To address this problem, we propose RoboDistill, a robust and generalizable multimodal 3D object detection framework that leverages visual foundation models (VFMs), such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM). First, we introduce SAM-AD, a domain-specific pretraining strategy that fine-tunes SAM on autonomous-driving imagery to extract feature representations with rich semantic information. Second, we design the AD Feature Pyramid Network (AD-FPN) to refine and upsample SAM features at multiple scales for seamless fusion with LiDAR features. Third, we develop the Depth-Guided Wavelet Attention (DGWA) module, which suppresses high-frequency sensor noise while preserving critical contextual information. Finally, we introduce KD Fusion, in which the pretrained SAM-AD serves as a teacher that distills high-quality visual knowledge into a lightweight point-cloud network, thereby improving robustness under noisy conditions. Extensive experiments across 27 challenging OOD corruption settings show that RoboDistill generally delivers stronger or competitive detection performance and robustness relative to representative state-of-the-art methods. This work bridges the gap between VFMs and 3D object detection and advances robust multimodal perception for real-world autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [96] [SewFusion: Tailored Generation of Topology and Panel-Level Geometry for Sewing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23548)
*Jiaxin Lin,Xiao Pan,Hangjie Yuan,Luyan Liang,Wan Li,Daquan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SewFusion框架，通过结合自回归拓扑预测和流匹配几何生成，解决服装纸样生成中离散拓扑与连续几何建模的割裂问题，显著提升生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾离散拓扑结构和连续几何形状的精准建模：扩散模型擅长整体几何但弱化拓扑；自回归模型保留拓扑但几何回归受限于Token级上下文，缺乏面板级（panel-level）上下文支持。

Method: 提出统一自回归框架SewFusion：1) 对离散拓扑采用Next-token prediction；2) 对面版连续几何引入Panel Geometry VAE（学习固定大小潜空间以处理变长几何）和Panel Geometry Flow进行潜变量生成；3) 提出Panel-Forcing机制以减少训练-推理不匹配并增强对拓扑预测错误的鲁棒性。

Result: 在SewFactory和GCD-MM数据集上的实验表明，SewFusion优于现有最先进方法：在图像-文本生成设置下，面板准确率提升6.36%，缝合线准确率提升11.30%，顶点L2误差降低1.90。

Conclusion: SewFusion通过解耦并针对性优化离散拓扑与连续几何的生成机制，有效弥合了两者间的鸿沟，实现了更高精度的服装纸样生成。

Abstract: Generating sewing patterns from images and text requires modeling a heterogeneous representation composed of discrete topology and continuous geometry. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: diffusion-based methods enable holistic geometry generation by converting the entire pattern into a continuous representation, but weaken discrete topology modeling; in contrast, autoregressive methods preserve discrete topology through next-token prediction, but tie continuous geometry regression to token-level hidden states with limited panel-level context. To bridge this gap, we propose SewFusion, a unified autoregressive framework that adopts tailored generation mechanisms for discrete topology and panel-level continuous geometry, using next-token prediction for the former and flow matching for the latter. To support panel-level continuous geometry generation, we introduce a Panel Geometry VAE that learns a fixed-size latent space for variable-length panel geometry, together with Panel Geometry Flow for latent generation. We further propose Panel-Forcing to reduce the training--inference mismatch in topology context and improve robustness to topology prediction errors. Extensive experiments on SewFactory and GCD-MM demonstrate that SewFusion consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods across various settings, achieving +6.36% Panel Accuracy, +11.30% Stitch Accuracy, and -1.90 Vertex L2 error in the image-text-based generation setting.

</details>


### [97] [Modeling Clinical Workflow for SYNTAX Scoring from Coronary Angiography Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23553)
*Suzhong Fu,Jingqi Dong,Xuan Ding,Rui Sun,Yiming Yang,Shuguang Cui,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层建模框架，将SYNTAX评分从直接回归重构为保持血管段身份的结构化推理问题。通过整合多源数据集构建大规模标注资源，实验显示该方法显著提升了预测精度（R²提升0.201）和稳定性（标准差降低18.4%），证明了结构与临床工作流对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化SYNTAX评分方法通常将冠状动脉造影视频直接映射为患者级分数的回归问题，缺乏对解剖结构的显式建模，导致可解释性不足且预测波动大。此外，领域特定数据稀缺限制了模型性能。

Method: 1. 提出分层建模框架：在帧间和视角间保持血管段身份，在血管段级别估计狭窄严重程度，并根据冠状动脉解剖结构进行层级证据聚合。
2. 数据构建：整合并补全多个公开冠状动脉造影数据集，构建了包含完整血管分割和衍生结构注释的大规模资源。
3. 模型优化：采用血管段级别的狭窄嵌入以增强特征表达能力。

Result: 与基线模型相比，新方法使R²分数提高了0.201，预测偏差的标准差（STD）降低了18.4%。结果表明，基于血管段级别的狭窄嵌入增强了模型的可解释性并减少了预测变异性。

Conclusion: 在自动化SYNTAX评分中，采用与解剖结构对齐的分层建模方法对于实现可靠、稳定的多视角冠状动脉造影视频分析至关重要。该研究为解决数据稀缺问题和提升临床决策支持系统的可信度提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The SYNTAX score is a clinically established tool for assessing anatomical lesion complexity in coronary artery disease and guiding subsequent treatment. However, automated SYNTAX scoring is commonly formulated as a direct regression problem from coronary angiography videos to patient-level scores. In this work, we reformulate SYNTAX scoring as a vessel segment identity-preserving anatomical reasoning problem and propose a hierarchical modeling framework that explicitly aligns learning with the clinical workflow. Our approach maintains vessel segment identity across frames and views, estimates stenosis severity at the segment level, and aggregates evidence hierarchically according to coronary anatomy. Simultaneously, to address the scarcity of domain-specific data, we integrate and complete multiple public coronary angiography datasets, constructing a large-scale resource featuring completed vessel segmentation and derived structural annotations. Experiments demonstrate that vessel segment-level stenosis embedding enhances explanatory power and reduces prediction variability compared to baseline models, with the R^2 score improving by 0.201 and dev STD decreasing by 18.4%. These results highlight the necessity of structure-aligned modeling for reliable and stable automated SYNTAX scoring from multi-view coronary angiography videos. The GitHub link is https://github.com/VersaceSu7/SYNTAX_score_777.

</details>


### [98] [Transferring Visual Explanations: How Cross-Architecture Knowledge Distillation Affects Model Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23561)
*Aleks Czufarow,Ihor Babin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过知识蒸馏（KD）将大模型的空间特征归因转移到紧凑学生模型，发现可解释性对软标签权重更敏感；细粒度归因能力受限于学生模型结构偏差，无法完全继承教师模型的像素级注意力结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境中部署高效神经网络时，紧凑模型往往牺牲了可解释性，而后者在自动驾驶和医疗等安全关键领域至关重要。现有研究缺乏对知识蒸馏如何影响模型可解释性的系统评估。

Method: 1. 使用ResNet-152作为教师网络，ResNet-34作为学生网络，在ImageNet-1K上进行知识蒸馏。
2. 系统变化蒸馏温度（temperature）和软标签损失权重（soft-label loss weight），设置五种配置。
3. 采用Relevance Mass Accuracy (RMA) 和 Relevance Rank Accuracy (RRA) 两个指标，基于Grad-CAM和Guided Grad-CAM热力图与真实物体掩码进行基准测试。
4. 评估12种卷积与Transformer混合架构的组合。

Result: 1. Top-1准确率在71.6%至74.0%之间。
2. Grad-CAM的RMA为7.7%-9.7%，RRA为7.3%-10.1%；Guided Grad-CAM的RMA为16.1%-18.6%，RRA为15.9%-21.5%。
3. 可解释性对软标签权重比温度更敏感：保留硬标签锚定能维持准确性和粗略定位，而过度依赖教师软标签会损害两者。
4. 所有配置下，细粒度归因均低于未蒸馏基线，表明Logit蒸馏主要传递注意力位置而非像素级结构。
5. 跨架构评估显示，细粒度空间推理的继承受到学生模型固有结构偏置的根本性瓶颈限制。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏虽然能转移部分注意力信息，但难以完美继承像素级的细粒度归因结构，且这种继承能力受限于学生模型本身的架构特性。该研究首次将此前用于神经网络剪枝的可解释性评估框架应用于知识蒸馏领域。

Abstract: Deploying efficient neural networks is essential in resource-constrained environments, yet compact models often sacrifice interpretability - a critical in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medicine. This study investigates whether Knowledge Distillation transfers the spatial feature attribution of a large teacher network to a compact student. To assess the influence of the KD scheme on interpretability, we distill a ResNet-152 teacher into a ResNet-34 student on ImageNet-1K across five configurations by systematically varying the distillation temperature and soft-label loss weight. Models are evaluated on top-1 accuracy, along with two interpretability metrics: Relevance Mass Accuracy and Relevance Rank Accuracy. These metrics are computed via Grad-CAM heatmaps benchmarked against ground-truth object masks. Our results show that top-1 accuracy ranges from 71.6% to 74.0%. For Grad-CAM, RMA ranges from 7.7% to 9.7% and RRA from 7.3% to 10.1%; for Guided Grad-CAM, RMA ranges from 16.1% to 18.6% and RRA from 15.9% to 21.5%. Interpretability proves far more sensitive to the soft-label weight than to the temperature: keeping the student anchored to hard labels preserves both accuracy and coarse localization, whereas weighting the teacher heavily degrades both. Fine-grained attribution, however, fell below the undistilled baseline in every configuration tested, indicating that logit distillation transmits where a model attends more readily than the pixel-level structure of that attention. We evaluate 12 cross-architecture combinations of convolutional and transformer-based models, revealing that the inheritance of fine-grained spatial reasoning is fundamentally bottlenecked by the student's intrinsic structural biases. To our knowledge, this is the first application of this interpretability-aware evaluation framework - previously used for neural network pruning - to KD.

</details>


### [99] [MaskVLA: Visual Masking Against Trajectory Overfitting of Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23565)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Jiaying Huang,Ge Wang,Shenhao Yan,Jiahao Yang,Chengsi Yao,Qi Liu,Qing Zhao,Shuguang Cui,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaskVLA，一种基于掩码的微调策略，解决VLA模型在有限数据下的轨迹过拟合问题，通过随机遮蔽主摄像头视觉信息，引导模型利用腕部摄像头信息，提升复杂任务能力和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型在有限数据集上进行微调时，表现出严重的轨迹过拟合现象，且未能有效利用腕部摄像头提供的关键视觉信息。

Method: 提出MaskVLA方法，采用基于掩码的微调策略。具体做法是随机遮蔽主摄像头（main camera）的一小部分视觉信息，迫使模型自主挖掘更细粒度、与任务相关且有效的视觉特征，从而引导其有效利用腕部摄像头（wrist camera）的信息。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0仿真环境中，相比$\pi_0$和OpenVLA-OFT基线方法，平均成功率分别提升了23.2%和16.8%。在真实世界ALOHA机器人上的实验也验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskVLA通过引入视觉掩码机制，有效缓解了VLA模型的过拟合问题，增强了其在复杂操作任务中的鲁棒性和泛化能力，并在仿真和真实场景中均取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate vision-language understanding with executable robot actions, enabling end-to-end learning for robot control. However, our empirical analysis reveals that existing models exhibit severe trajectory overfitting when finetuned on limited datasets. To guide the model in effectively utilizing wrist camera information, we propose MaskVLA, a masking-based fine-tuning strategy. By randomly masking a small portion of the main camera's visual information, the model is guided to autonomously learn more fine-grained, task-relevant, and effective visual features. This process leads to the emergence of robust policies, thereby enhancing the model's capability to tackle complex manipulation tasks and improving its generalization performance. Our method has been comprehensively evaluated on RoboTwin 2.0, achieving an average success rate improvement of 23.2% and 16.8% compared to $π_0$ and OpenVLA-OFT, respectively. Furthermore, experiments on real-world ALOHA robots also demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [100] [G6D: Geometric Learning-Free RGB-D 6D Pose Solver for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23566)
*Yixuan Liang,William Chen,Yunan Wang,Jizhou Yan,Zhao Jin,Changling Liu,Chuxiong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G6D，一种无需学习、纯几何驱动的RGB-D 6D姿态求解器。它不依赖预训练模型或目标特定训练，通过模板匹配和轮廓/深度一致性优化生成姿态假设，支持CPU部署，实现高精度与低计算资源的平衡，并在LineMOD及BOP19数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本6D姿态估计方法虽泛化性强，但依赖大规模预训练模型，导致GPU计算和内存需求高，难以在资源受限的机器人平台上部署；同时，学习的中间表示缺乏几何可解释性，不利于任务特定的适配。

Method: 提出G6D框架：输入RGB-D观测、物体实例掩码、相机内参和CAD模型；通过基于模板的几何匹配生成姿态假设，并利用轮廓和深度一致性进行细化；整个过程无需预训练视觉模型或目标特定训练，保持几何表示的可解释性，并支持可调的假设数量以平衡精度与计算量。

Result: 在LineMOD和五个BOP19数据集上实验证明其具有先进性能；真实世界的抓取放置实验验证了其在机器人操作中的适用性；支持仅CPU配置，无需GPU资源。

Conclusion: G6D提供了一种高效、可解释且易于部署的6D姿态估计方案，解决了现有方法在计算资源和可解释性方面的局限，适用于资源受限的机器人平台。

Abstract: 6D object pose estimation is fundamental to robotic manipulation and automation. Recent zero-shot methods have significantly improved generalization to unseen objects, but most still rely on large-scale pretrained models with substantial GPU computation and memory demands. These requirements complicate deployment on robotic platforms where perception, planning, and control share limited computational resources, while learned intermediate representations offer limited geometric interpretability for task-specific adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose G6D, a learning-free, geometry-driven RGB-D 6D pose solver. Given an RGB-D observation, an object instance mask, camera intrinsics, and a CAD model, G6D generates pose hypotheses through template-based geometric matching and refines them using silhouette and depth consistency, forming a purely geometry-driven pose estimation paradigm. This paradigm requires neither pretrained visual models nor target-specific training and preserves interpretable geometric representations throughout pose estimation. Moreover, adjustable hypothesis counts provide flexible accuracy-computation trade-offs, while a CPU-only configuration supports deployment without GPU resources. Experiments on LineMOD and five BOP19 datasets demonstrate advanced performance. Real-world pick-and-place experiments further demonstrate G6D's applicability to robotic manipulation. The complete project is publicly available at https://ai4control.github.io/G6D-Project-Page .

</details>


### [101] [An Efficient and Effective Watermarking Scheme for the Protection of the Intellectual Property Rights of Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23586)
*Wenhong Huang,Jianwei Fei,Benedetta Tondi,Bin Ma,Fangjun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VidMark水印方案，通过两尺度离散小波变换和全局时间注意力块增强鲁棒性，结合解码器引导微调技术，在视频生成模型中嵌入特定水印，实现合成视频验证和模型所有权验证，准确率超99%，抗攻击性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视频生成模型（VGMs）快速发展带来的知识产权问题，特别是填补合成视频验证（确定视频是否由受保护VGM生成）和模型所有权验证（确定嫌疑VGM是否为受保护VGM的未经授权的副本）这两个法医任务的空白。

Method: 1. 提出VidMark网络：结合两尺度离散小波变换（DWT）分解和全局时间注意力块（GTAB），提升水印的鲁棒性和不可感知性。2. 解码器引导微调过程：利用冻结的VidMark解码器，使VGM能够合成携带不可见、鲁棒且模型特定水印的视频。3. 建立两个验证框架进行合成视频验证和模型所有权验证。

Result: 在代表性VGM上的广泛实验表明，该方案实现了超过99%的水印提取准确率和两项任务100%的验证准确率，对视频生成质量影响可忽略不计；水印对全面的视频级和模型级攻击表现出强大的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的生成内水印方案能有效解决VGM相关的知识产权验证问题，具有高准确性、低影响和高鲁棒性，为保护视频生成模型的IP提供了有效手段。

Abstract: The rapid development of video generative models (VGMs) has enabled the generation of highly realistic synthetic videos, raising concerns about the intellectual property rights (IPR) of these models. In particular, two closely related forensic tasks remain largely unaddressed: synthetic video verification (determining whether a video was generated by a protected VGM) and model ownership verification (determining whether a suspect VGM is an unauthorized copy of a protected VGM). In this paper, we propose a new in-generation watermarking scheme that can address the two verification tasks. First, a novel video watermarking network named VidMark is presented, which incorporates a two-scale discrete wavelet transform (DWT) decomposition and a global temporal attention block (GTAB) to enhance watermark robustness and imperceptibility. Second, we present a decoder-guided fine-tuning procedure. By leveraging the frozen VidMark decoder, this process enables VGMs to synthesize videos carrying an imperceptible, robust, and model-specific watermark. Finally, two verification frameworks are established to perform synthetic video verification and model ownership verification. Extensive experiments on representative VGMs demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves over 99% watermark extraction accuracy and 100% verification accuracy on both tasks, with negligible impact on video generation quality. Furthermore, the watermarks exhibit strong robustness against a comprehensive range of video-level and model-level attacks.

</details>


### [102] [Collapse, Not Complexity: Failure-Conditioned Decomposition Repair for End-to-End Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23592)
*Xingyu Lin,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 传统端到端文档解析中的“推理模式”在复杂页面上效果不佳，反而降低质量并消耗更多Token。本文提出一种基于普通解析轨迹检测“坍塌”、通过投影分解页面并重新解析各区域的新方法，显著提升了修复效果且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端文档解析器提供的可选推理模式在处理复杂页面时，并未如预期那样提升性能，反而导致平均质量下降和计算资源浪费，且难以预测其收益。

Method: 1. 从普通解析轨迹中检测页面“坍塌”现象；2. 利用投影技术将页面分解为多个区域；3. 对每个分解后的区域分别进行重新解析以进行修复。

Result: 该方法使整体质量提升1.40（95% CI [0.68, 2.16]），Token消耗仅增加1.13倍。结果在三个检查点中均得到复现，且在所有参数冻结的情况下，在剩余的1,175个基准页面上实现了2.41的质量增益。

Conclusion: 使用推理模式并非解决复杂页面解析问题的有效途径，因为收益主要集中在已发生坍塌的页面上，而推理模式极少能成功修复这些坍塌。提出的基于检测和区域重解析的方法是一种更高效、更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers increasingly offer an optional reasoning mode for complex pages. On a 180-page entropy-stratified discovery sample with one frozen 4B checkpoint, complexity is the wrong decision variable. Reasoning lowers mean quality by 2.21 Overall at 1.54x tokens; a preregistered input-only model cannot predict its signed benefit (held-out AUROC 0.47, indistinguishable from chance). The benefit concentrates on pages whose ordinary pass has already collapsed, and they do not look complex: shared collapses have lower layout entropy than healthy ones yet consume 19x the tokens as degenerate repetition that doubling the budget does not cure. Switching modes rarely repairs them: 83% recur under reasoning. We instead detect collapse from the ordinary-pass trace, decompose the page by projection, and re-parse each region. Repair gains 1.40 Overall (95% CI [0.68, 2.16]) at 1.13x tokens, replicates across three checkpoints, and, with all parameters frozen, gains 2.41 (CI [1.64, 3.46]) on the remaining 1,175 benchmark pages.

</details>


### [103] [StyleAT: Defending Face Recognition Against Semantic Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23596)
*Ben Shapira,Roi Cohen,Shang-Tse Chen,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BoundStyle的高效语义对抗攻击方法，利用StyleGAN潜在空间加速攻击过程，并在此基础上开发了StyleAT对抗训练方案，显著提升了人脸识别模型对语义对抗攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部识别模型易受对抗性语义编辑（如轻微老化或姿态变化）的影响而误分类；虽然现有攻击手段强大，但计算成本高昂，难以用于开发有效的防御机制（如对抗训练）。

Method: 1. 提出BoundStyle：在StyleGAN丰富的潜在空间中执行强大的语义攻击，以最大化误分类率。2. 提出StyleAT：基于BoundStyle开发的高效对抗训练方案，结合低预算攻击变体，以防御更强且未见的语义攻击。

Result: BoundStyle的攻击成功率高，且速度比现有的最先进攻击快约9.5倍，适用于对抗训练。在两个未见过的数据集和七个模型上的评估显示，StyleAT增强了针对最先进攻击的鲁棒准确率，并在多种设置下优于常见防御措施。

Conclusion: 通过引入快速高效的语义攻击工具BoundStyle及其衍生的对抗训练框架StyleAT，有效解决了现有对抗攻击计算成本高导致防御开发不足的问题，显著提升了人脸识别模型的安全性。

Abstract: With face-recognition models now embedded in everyday authentication and surveillance, recent works have pinpointed a critical weakness: these models remain acutely vulnerable to adversarial semantic edits. I.e., adversarially produced semantic alterations to the input, such as slight aging or pose changes, can induce misclassifications. Certain existing attacks are powerful, but they can be computationally costly, rendering them inadequate for developing defenses (e.g., through adversarial training). To fill the gap, we introduce BoundStyle, a potent semantic attack operating in StyleGAN's rich latent space to maximize misclassification rates. Notably, BoundStyle achieves high attack success rates while being ${\sim}{\times}9.5$ faster than existing state-of-the-art attacks, making it suitable for adversarial training. Building on BoundStyle, we develop StyleAT, an efficient adversarial training scheme that incorporates low-budget attack variants yet defends against stronger and unseen semantic attacks. We evaluate on two datasets unseen during training and seven models, and find that StyleAT boosts robust accuracy against state-of-the-art attacks and outperforms common defenses in various settings.

</details>


### [104] [PETR: Prompt Ensembling with Training-free Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23600)
*Weihan Cai,Hao Tan,Xinping Gao,Shibiao Xu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PETR框架，通过双提示架构和无需训练的统计路由机制，解决视觉-语言模型在已知类上表现好但泛化到未知类差的问题，在11个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示学习（Prompt learning）虽然能高效适应下游任务，但往往在提升已知类别（seen classes）性能的同时，损害了对未知类别（unseen classes）的泛化能力。

Method: 提出“无需训练的路由提示集成”（PETR）。核心创新包括：1. 设计双提示架构，分别从不同数据和目标中学习两个互补提示，分别强调已知类的判别性和未知类的泛化性；2. 使用共享冻结的CLIP主干进行微调并收集训练集logits统计信息；3. 推理时根据测试样本与已知数据的统计相似度，将其路由至最合适的提示分支，实现无需训练的自适应路由。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，该框架在已知类和未知类上均一致优于先前方法，取得了新的最先进（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: PETR是首个基于统计相似度进行无需训练自适应路由的提示调优框架。其设计提供了可解释的路由信号，避免了混合专家（MoE）式路由常见的训练不稳定和负载不均衡问题，有效平衡了特定类别性能与跨类别泛化能力。

Abstract: Prompt learning efficiently adapts vision-language models (VLMs) to downstream tasks, but gains on seen classes often come at the expense of generalization to unseen classes. To address this limitation, we propose prompt ensembling with training-free routing (PETR), whose key innovation is a carefully designed dual-prompt architecture: two complementary prompts are learned from different data and objectives to emphasize seen class discrimination and unseen-class generalization, respectively. During training, both prompts are fine-tuned using a shared frozen CLIP backbone, and statistical information is collected from the training set logits. At inference time, we determine the similarity of each test sample to seen data, and route the sample to the most appropriate prompt branch. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first prompt tuning framework that performs training-free adaptive routing based on statistical similarity. This design provides an interpretable routing signal and avoids common MoE-style routing pathologies, such as router training instability and load imbalance. Extensive experiments on 11 benchmark datasets demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms previous methods on both seen and unseen classes, achieving new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [105] [PREM: Prefix-Steered Recurrent Memory for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23601)
*Siru Zhong,Qiongyan Wang,Xiaohui Lv,Yuzheng Zhuang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Haohuan Fu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PREM框架，通过无记忆token的递归机制，在极低显存开销下显著提升冻结VLM对长视频的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长视频理解中严格Token预算下难以捕捉瞬时视觉证据的问题，避免现有方法（如帧压缩、附加记忆Token或修改KV缓存）的局限性。

Method: 引入前缀引导的递归记忆（PREM），将视频摄入与问答分离：递归写入器将视觉流蒸馏为紧凑的多槽关联状态；问题条件读出器在非视觉提示前缀中添加基于记忆的K/V调制，实现“一次写入，多次查询”推理。

Result: 在六个长视频基准测试中持续优于冻结基线。在16帧限制下，Qwen2.5-VL-3B宏观平均准确率提升3.06%，动作反义识别提升11.0%，局部针检索提升9.9%。仅微调0.24%参数，峰值显存增加0.03 GiB。

Conclusion: PREM是一种高效、低开销的长视频理解方案，能在不改变模型主体结构的前提下，显著增强冻结VLM处理长视频的能力。

Abstract: Long-video understanding must capture transient visual evidence under strict token budgets, yet existing methods compress frames, append memory tokens, or alter internal key-value (KV) caches. We introduce Prefix-Steered Recurrent Memory (PREM), a memory-token-free framework for frozen vision-language models (VLMs).
