PREM separates video ingestion from query answering: a recurrent writer distills visual streams into a compact 256 KiB multi-slot associative state, while a question-conditioned readout adds memory-derived key/value (K/V) steering modulations to existing non-visual prompt prefixes during prefill. This enables write-once, query-many inference without extra prompt tokens or decoding recurrence. Across six long-video benchmarks in offline and streaming end-of-stream settings, PREM consistently outperforms frozen baselines at every evaluated visual budget. Under a constrained budget of 16 frames, PREM improves macro-average accuracy by 3.06% on Qwen2.5-VL-3B, with gains of 11.0% on action antonym identification and 9.9% on localized needle retrieval. These gains require tuning 0.24% of backbone parameters at 0.03 GiB of peak GPU memory overhead.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond UV Mapping: Mesh Texture Compression via Surface-Aligned Texture Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23606)
*Jianqiang Wang,Junhui Hou,Siyu Ren,Weiyao Lin,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TexF，一种基于网格表面的稀疏体素纹理场，结合3DNTC压缩技术，解决传统2D UV图集导致的编码效率低问题，在MPEG和AOM基准测试中优于现有UV方法，支持实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格纹理压缩依赖2D UV图集，其图表不连续性和映射开销限制了编码效率。

Method: 1. 引入TexF：表面对齐的纹理场，将纹理属性组织在从网格表面派生的稀疏体素中；2. 比特流压缩：复用现有3D属性编解码器，无需单独传输体素位置；3. GPU驻留压缩：开发3DNTC，结合量化哈希特征与轻量级解码器实现随机访问重建；4. 可微渲染用于图像空间 refinement。

Result: 在MPEG和AOM网格压缩基准测试中，相比代表性基于UV的方法，TexF在比特流和GPU驻留压缩方面均实现了平均率失真性能的提升；3DNTC支持实时渲染。

Conclusion: TexF通过表面对齐的稀疏体素表示和高效压缩策略，显著提升了网格纹理压缩的效率和质量，同时保持了对高分辨率纹理和实时渲染的支持。

Abstract: Mesh texture compression typically relies on 2D UV atlases, whose chart discontinuities and mapping overhead can limit coding efficiency. To tackle this challenge, we introduce TexF, a surface-aligned texture field that organizes texture attributes in sparse voxels derived from the mesh surface. This representation supports high-resolution textures while preserving local 3D correlations for compression and enabling direct surface queries. For bitstream compression, TexF reuses established 3D attribute codecs, with voxel locations reconstructed from the decoded mesh without separate transmission. For GPU-resident compression, we develop 3DNTC, which combines quantized hash features with a lightweight decoder for random-access reconstruction at surface positions. Differentiable rendering enables image-space refinement of both voxel attributes and compressed neural fields. Experiments on the MPEG and AOM mesh compression benchmarks demonstrate improved average rate-distortion performance over representative UV-based methods for both bitstream and GPU-resident compression. 3DNTC also supports real-time rendering.

</details>


### [107] [Compact Low-Cost Hyperspectral Imaging via Angular-to-Spectral Diversity Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23619)
*Kazuma Fujiwara,Takuya Funatomi,Kazuya Kitano,Yuki Fujimura,Yasuhiro Mukaigawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于角度-光谱多样性转换的快照高光谱成像系统，利用锥形万花筒和双折射滤光片实现低成本、紧凑且稳定的单帧光谱重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的快照高光谱成像系统在同时实现稳定重建、低成本和紧凑光学结构方面存在局限。

Method: 使用锥形万花筒生成具有不同入射方向的复制视场，并通过直接连接的双折射滤光片将其转换为依赖于视通道的光谱透射率，从而获得互补测量数据以改善逆问题的条件数；采用基于条件数的标准选择双折射滤光片配置，并保留简单的像素级线性模型进行非学习快速重建。

Result: 该系统仅使用现成组件，无需中继光学或级联模块，在合成数据和真实数据上均验证了有效性，实现了更稳定的单次拍摄光谱重建。

Conclusion: 该方案通过角度-光谱多样性转换机制，成功构建了兼具稳定性、低成本和紧凑性的快照高光谱成像系统。

Abstract: Snapshot hyperspectral imaging avoids sequential scanning, but systems that jointly achieve stable reconstruction, low cost, and compact optics remain limited. We present a snapshot hyperspectral imaging system based on angular-to-spectral diversity conversion. A tapered kaleidoscope creates replicated views with distinct incidence directions, and a directly attached birefringent filter converts them into view-channel-dependent spectral transmittances, yielding complementary measurements that better condition the inverse problem for more stable single-shot spectral reconstruction. The system preserves a simple pixel-wise linear model for fast non-learning-based reconstruction and uses only off-the-shelf components without relay optics or cascaded modules. We select the birefringent filter configuration using a condition-number-based criterion and validate the system on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [108] [Reassessing Global Gradient-Norm Imbalance in BLIP Fine-Tuning Across Physical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23655)
*Kiran Naseer,Samreen Azhar,Dwarikanath Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉-语言模型中梯度不平衡并非总是需要修正的缺陷；单纯降低学习率即可达到接近最优的性能，而强制平衡梯度往往导致最差结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“视觉与语言路径间梯度幅度不平衡必须被纠正”这一普遍假设，特别是针对非自适应、信号驱动类校正方案之外的固定策略。

Method: 在九个微调条件、三个随机种子及涵盖水下、航空、放射学三个不同物理域偏移的数据集上，测量归一化后的语言到视觉梯度范数比。测试了降低学习率、分阶段冻结（Staged freezing）以及强制两组梯度幅度相等三种方法，并对比其性能。

Result: 1. 梯度不平衡程度在不同域间变化显著且无规律；2. 简单降低学习率可大幅减少不平衡，且在所有数据集上BLEU得分接近最佳方法；3. 强制梯度平衡虽使参数级不平衡趋近于零，但在不同数据集上表现最差或最好，说明平衡本身不直接预测性能；4. 发现常用的LoRA配置在未正确调整时会使零个视觉参数适应，修正后提升了所有数据集的BLEU-4得分。

Conclusion: 梯度范数比的降低并不一致地预测跨域的 captioning 性能；达到特定平衡水平的方式与平衡水平本身同样重要。盲目追求梯度平衡可能适得其反，简单的超参数调整（如学习率）往往更有效。

Abstract: Imbalanced gradient magnitudes between the visual and language pathways of a vision-language model are often treated as a defect to be corrected. We test that premise for one family of correction, deliberately excluding adaptive, signal-driven schemes (e.g. BalGrad, OGM, PMR, CGGM), which are a mechanistically distinct class outside this study's scope. Measuring the language-to-visual gradient-norm ratio, reported in parameter-normalised form, across nine fine-tuning conditions, three seeds, and three captioning datasets spanning distinct physical domain shifts -- underwater, aerial, radiological -- we find imbalance magnitude varies markedly across domains with no predictable ordering. A plain learning-rate reduction cuts imbalance substantially and lands within a few BLEU points of the best method on every dataset. Staged freezing reduces the ratio on every domain yet never ranks first; a schedule-only control isolates freezing as the cause on one dataset but not the other two. Forcing the two gradient groups to equal magnitude drives per-parameter imbalance close to zero on every domain, yet is both the best result in the study and the worst placement among full fine-tuning methods, on different datasets, with identical settings. Reductions in gradient-norm ratio do not consistently predict captioning performance across domains, and how a given level of balance is reached matters as much as the level itself. As a secondary finding, a commonly reused LoRA configuration applied to BLIP silently adapts zero visual parameters; correcting it improves BLEU-4 on all three datasets.

</details>


### [109] [Why Do Video Diffusion Models Violate Physics? Unveiling the Flaws in Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23658)
*Yueyan Li,Haibo Wang,Caixia Yuan,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频扩散模型生成内容违反物理规律的问题，通过可解释性研究定位到RoPE导致的过度空间注意力衰减是根本原因，并提出一种无需训练的轻量级架构修改方案，通过缩放不同去噪步骤中RoPE的频率来改善物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管最先进的视频扩散模型具有出色的视觉质量，但其生成的内容常违反现实世界的物理定律。现有解决方案多依赖外部先验或专用数据，缺乏对模型内部机制的深入理解。

Method: 1. 进行文本到视频扩散模型“运动规划”过程的首次可解释性研究，揭示早期去噪阶段运动轨迹的形成机制。
2. 结合交叉注意力轨迹模式与因果头贡献分析，识别驱动运动规划的特定注意力头子集。
3. 通过自注意力分析发现旋转位置编码（RoPE）导致过度的空间注意力衰减。
4. 提出一种轻量级架构修改策略，在不同去噪步骤中缩放RoPE的频率，以减少过度注意力衰减。

Result: 实验证实该方法能有效减少早期候选区域锁定在物理不合理位置的情况，帮助模型探索更优候选区域以建立连贯的物理运动。训练无关和基于训练的实验均验证了其在增强生成视频物理常识方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过揭示RoPE在早期去噪阶段引发的过度空间注意力衰减是导致物理不一致性的根源，提出的频率缩放策略作为一种无训练开销的修正方法，显著提升了视频生成内容的物理合理性。

Abstract: Despite impressive visual quality, state-of-the-art video diffusion models often generate content that violates real-world physical laws. While existing solutions rely on external priors or specialized data, we investigate the root cause by exploring the internal mechanisms of these models. Specifically, we present the first interpretability study on the ''motion planning'' process of text-to-video diffusion models, revealing how motion trajectories form during early denoising stages. Building upon the ''first shape, then details'' finding, we combine cross-attention trajectory patterns with causal head contributions to identify a specific subset of attention heads driving motion planning. Further, our self-attention analysis shows that Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) induces excessive spatial attention decay. This causes early candidate regions to prematurely lock into physically implausible positions, suppressing reasonable trajectories in adjacent frames and triggering generation failure modes. To address this fundamental flaw, we propose a lightweight architectural modification that scales the frequency of RoPE across different denoising steps. This strategy reduces excessive attention decay, helping the model explore better candidate regions to establish coherent physical motion. Finally, training-free and training-based experiments confirm the effectiveness of our approach in enhancing the physical commonsense of generated videos.

</details>


### [110] [Which Terrain Is Better? Preference Learning with VLM Prototypes for Off-Road Traversability Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23673)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,E-In Son,Dong-Wook Kim,Jung-Taak Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TravPro方法，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）将稀疏的成对比较标注转化为密集的地形偏好伪标签，实现无需像素级标注的视觉可通行性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的越野导航中，传统的可通行性评分缺乏通用地面真值，且仅反映区域属性而非机器人偏好；需要一种能根据地形表面条件进行排序的方法。

Method: 构建TravPro框架：1) 将标准标注转换为有序区域对；2) 在冻结的VLM patch tokens上拟合小型读出器以学习原型偏好分数；3) 生成稠密偏好伪标签作为教师信号；4) RGB学生网络蒸馏这些地图，生成包含非地面掩码的稠密地形偏好图。

Result: 在五个未见域上，TravPro的平均成对准确率达到0.915，显著优于最强基线的0.783；该方法生成的排序对表面条件敏感，超越了每类固定值的表达能力。

Conclusion: 通过特定的使用方式（成对比较与原型聚类），相同的VLM和监督信号能有效生成地形偏好排序，证明了方法论设计比单纯提示或密集目标更关键。

Abstract: In vision-based off-road navigation, a robot needs to know not only which obstacles to avoid but also which terrain is better. The first is handled by freespace detection or semantic segmentation. The second is usually answered with a traversability score, but no universal ground truth exists for such a score, so perception falls back on a predefined value per semantic class or a freespace confidence. These scores say what a region is, not which region a robot should prefer. We therefore formulate this preference as visual traversability ranking, an ordering of visible terrain that can be supervised by comparisons between two regions. Standard annotations do not label preference, but they imply its direction. We present TravPro, which converts these annotations into ordered region pairs and fits a small readout on frozen vision--language model (VLM) patch tokens to these pairs. The tokens are clustered once into a fixed prototype bank, and the readout learns a preference score per prototype. The readout is then applied to every patch and serves as a teacher that turns sparse comparisons into dense preference pseudo-labels without pixel-wise annotation. An RGB student distills these maps into a dense terrain-preference map together with a non-ground mask that excludes obstacles and background from the ranking. On five unseen domains, TravPro reaches a mean pairwise accuracy of 0.915 against 0.783 for the strongest baseline, producing an ordering sensitive to surface condition that a per-class value cannot represent. The same VLM and the same supervision yield no such ordering when the VLM is prompted and the supervision is used as dense targets; what matters is how they are used.

</details>


### [111] [Infectious Bovine Pinkeye Detection Using Computer Vision and Imbalance-Aware Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23714)
*Michael Abalo,Jameson Brennan,Hossein Moradi Rekabdarkolaee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估并比较了YOLOv11和YOLOv26在牛传染性结膜炎自动分类中的性能，重点分析了类平衡策略（RMO与SMOTE）的影响。结果显示两种模型均表现优异，其中RMO策略显著提升了少数类的检测能力，部分变体甚至实现了零误报和完美召回，证明了深度学习在畜牧健康监测中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的牛传染性结膜炎诊断主要依赖临床观察，存在主观性强、耗时长且难以在大型牧场或偏远地区高效实施的问题。因此，需要开发自动化、客观且高效的诊断工具以提升畜牧业健康管理和生产力。

Method: 研究训练并评估了YOLOv11和YOLOv26的五种变体（n, s, m, l, x）。使用原始不平衡数据集以及两种类平衡策略：随机少数类过采样（RMO）和调整后的合成少数类过采样技术（SMOTE），对模型进行分类性能对比分析。

Result: YOLOv11和YOLOv26均展现出强大的分类性能。YOLOv11的RMO-s变体达到0.99准确率、0.98宏观F1分数及1.00真正例率（TPR），无假阴性；YOLOv26的原始l变体及RMO-m/l变体也取得了相似的高分。总体而言，RMO在提升少数类检测方面优于SMOTE，但YOLOv26-l等变体无需过采样即可表现强劲。

Conclusion: YOLOv11和YOLOv26具有用于牛传染性结膜炎自动检测的巨大潜力。RMO策略能有效改善少数类识别，但特定模型架构本身已具备高性能。研究结果支持将这些深度学习模型进一步应用于牲畜健康监测系统中。

Abstract: Infectious bovine pinkeye is a contagious ocular disease that adversely affects cattle health, welfare, and agricultural productivity. Conventional diagnosis relies primarily on clinical observation, which can be subjective, time-consuming, and difficult to implement efficiently in large herds or remote settings. This study evaluated and compared You Only Look Once (YOLO) v11 and YOLOv26 for automated bovine pinkeye classification and investigated the effects of class-balancing strategies on model performance. Five variants (n, s, m, l, and x) of each architecture were trained and evaluated using the original imbalanced dataset, Random Minority Oversampling (RMO), and an adapted Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE). Both YOLOv11 and YOLOv26 demonstrated strong classification performance, although the effects of class balancing varied across model variants. For YOLOv11, RMO-s achieved an accuracy of 0.99, a macro F1-score of 0.98, and a true positive rate (TPR) of 1.00, with no false-negative classifications. RMO-m also achieved a TPR of 1.00 with no false negatives. For YOLOv26, the original l, RMO-m, and RMO-l variants each achieved an accuracy of 0.99 and a macro F1-score of 0.98, with RMO-l attaining a TPR of 1.00 and no false negatives. Overall, RMO generally provided greater improvements in minority-class detection than adapted SMOTE, whereas the strong performance of the original YOLOv26-l demonstrates that oversampling was not necessary for all model variants. These findings demonstrate the potential of YOLOv11 and YOLOv26 for automated detection of bovine pinkeye and support further evaluation for livestock health monitoring.

</details>


### [112] [Layer-Aware Position Embeddings for Visual Token Pruning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23715)
*Yahong Wang,Zhangkai Ni,Juncheng Wu,Yuyin Zhou,Ying Wen,Lianghua He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层感知的位置嵌入策略，通过在关键层使用稀疏位置嵌入、其他层使用连续位置嵌入，解决了现有Token剪枝方法中因位置嵌入方式不当导致的感知或接地能力下降问题，从而提升了多模态大语言模型的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）因依赖大量视觉Token导致计算开销巨大。现有的Token剪枝方法在重新分配位置嵌入时存在局限：稀疏位置嵌入会降低注意力值，削弱模型的感知能力；而连续位置嵌入会破坏原始空间对应关系，削弱模型的接地（grounding）能力。

Method: 通过对语言解码器进行逐层分析，发现中间层在维持Token剪枝下的接地能力方面起关键作用。基于此，提出一种分层感知位置嵌入策略：在接地敏感层切换到稀疏位置嵌入，而在其他层保持连续位置嵌入。

Result: 在多种代表性剪枝方法和多样化基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法相比标准的稀疏和连续位置嵌入，显著提高了剪枝后MLLMs的综合多模态性能。

Conclusion: 通过结合稀疏和连续位置嵌入的优势，并针对特定层级进行差异化处理，可以有效缓解Token剪枝带来的性能退化问题，实现更高效且高性能的多模态推理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial computational overhead due to the reliance on hundreds of visual tokens to represent images. While token pruning has emerged as a promising approach to reduce the inference cost of MLLMs, existing methods typically reassign position embeddings to the retained tokens using either sparse or continuous position embeddings, each introducing distinct limitations. Sparse position embeddings tend to decrease the attention value allocated to visual tokens, thereby degrading the perception capability of MLLMs, whereas continuous position embeddings disrupt the original spatial correspondence of visual tokens, leading to weakened grounding capability. To mitigate this issue, we perform layer-wise analysis of the language decoder and observe that intermediate layers play a critical role for maintaining the grounding capability of MLLMs under token pruning. Based on this observation, we propose a layer-aware position embedding strategy, which switches to sparse position embeddings at grounding-sensitive layers while maintaining continuous position embeddings elsewhere. Extensive experiments across representative pruning methods and diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach improves the comprehensive multimodal performance of pruned MLLMs compared with standard sparse and continuous position embeddings.

</details>


### [113] [BindCLIP: One Balanced Coupling For Compositional Vision Language Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23717)
*Liuyang Song,Yi Zhang,Zhongyi Deng,Daqian Yang,Hongbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BindCLIP，一种基于最优传输耦合的对齐评分器，通过冻结编码器保留图像-文本的关联结构，无需额外标注或检测器即可提升细粒度视觉语言对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决全局视觉-语言相似度模型无法捕捉词汇与区域对应关系及空间排列的问题，避免模型识别所有单词对象却偏好组合错误的描述。

Method: 构建基于单个潜在对象的配对评分器，采用平衡的token-patch-深度最优传输耦合，将候选描述和多个视觉深度置于同一平面；读取语义、实体、顺序和空间证据作为能量，并通过几何 refinement 收缩不支持的移动；不使用任务标签、解析器、关系清单或检测器。

Result: 在What'sUp、ARO和SugarCrepe基准测试中，相比冻结的全局CLIP提升了性能，特别是在关系分割上实现了最强的迁移效果；控制实验排除了补丁访问和描述长度捷径的影响。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输耦合读取冻结编码器的关联结构，能有效捕捉空间排列信息，显著提升视觉语言模型的细粒度理解能力。

Abstract: Global vision--language similarities compress an image and a caption into one vector, preserving semantics but not which word corresponds to which region or how those regions are arranged; a model can recognize every word and object yet prefer a compositionally incorrect caption. We argue that a frozen encoder retains this association structure, so the problem is to read it rather than to rebuild it beside the pretrained similarity. We introduce BindCLIP, a pairwise scorer built on one latent object: a balanced token--patch--depth optimal-transport coupling that places both candidate captions and several visual depths in a single plan. Semantic, entity, order, and spatial evidence are read as energies of this state, and exchanging the candidates permutes the plan, making the score exactly antisymmetric. A geometric refinement inside the coupling contracts moves that the candidates and the visual depths do not support. No task label, parser, relation inventory, or detector is used. One checkpoint and one inference path improve the official What'sUp, ARO, and SugarCrepe benchmarks over frozen global CLIP, with the strongest transfer on the relation splits. Controls rule out patch access and caption-length shortcuts, and an inference-time lesion localizes spatial arrangement to the coupling.

</details>


### [114] [VGGT-Prime: Compute-Adaptive Mixture-of-Heads for Efficient Visual Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23733)
*Abteen Arab,Guile Wu,Chengjie Huang,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGGT-Prime，通过识别并消除视觉几何Transformer中的架构冗余（即部分注意力头携带关键几何信息），实现8倍推理加速，且与现有方法互补可达14倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Feed-forward视觉几何模型（如VGGT）因全局注意力机制导致的计算复杂度随输入视图数量二次增长的问题，旨在降低长序列输入的延迟。

Method: 提出VGGT-Prime，一种计算自适应的混合头（mixture-of-heads）模型。通过轻量级路由器评估每个全局注意力头的适当计算水平，并动态分配不同的计算模式，以消除多头部注意力模块中的架构冗余。

Result: 在多个数据集上，VGGT-Prime相比原VGGT实现了8倍的推理速度提升，同时保持具有竞争力的相机位姿、深度和点云预测性能；结合Token合并等现有加速方法，总加速比可达14倍。

Conclusion: 从架构冗余视角而非仅Token冗余视角解决视觉几何Transformer的效率瓶颈是可行的，提出的动态计算分配机制能显著加速模型而不牺牲重建质量。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry models such as the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) have recently enabled direct 3D reconstruction from multi-view images. Despite their promising performance, these models scale quadratically with the number of input views due to their global attention mechanism, resulting in substantial latency for long sequence inputs. There have been some recent efforts to accelerate VGGT, but they primarily focus on reducing \emph{token redundancy} through token merging or key/value sparsification. Our work resolves this bottleneck from a different perspective by investigating \emph{architectural redundancy} in visual geometry transformers. We show that the multi-head attention modules in VGGT's global-attention layers contain substantial architectural redundancy, with only a subset of heads carrying critical geometric information. In light of this observation, we propose VGGT-Prime, a compute-adaptive mixture-of-heads model that resolves this redundancy to accelerate visual geometry transformers while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality. The key idea of VGGT-Prime is to estimate the appropriate computation level for each global-attention head using a lightweight router and then dynamically assign each head to different computation modes. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that VGGT-Prime can achieve an {$8\times$} inference speedup over VGGT while maintaining competitive performance on camera pose, depth, and point-cloud predictions. We further show that VGGT-Prime is complementary to existing acceleration methods, such as token merging, further improving inference speed by up to $14{\times}$ over VGGT. An overview of our work is available on our \href{https://vggt-prime.github.io}{project page}.

</details>


### [115] [OnlineWM: Causality-Aware Active Online Learning for Effective World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23753)
*Yikun Miao,Fangqi Zhu,Quanxin Shou,Xiaoyi Pang,Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Haodong Wang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OnlineWM框架，通过主动在线学习和因果感知微调解决生成式世界模型在动作可控性上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练管线存在两大缺陷：1. 静态离线数据导致分布错位，无法覆盖长尾场景；2. 最小化观测差异的目标易使模型利用虚假相关性而非捕捉因果机制。

Method: 构建闭环优化流程：(1) 主动在线学习：自适应查询模拟器以获取针对当前预测弱点的交互序列；(2) 因果感知微调：采用反事实学习策略，对比相同状态下不同动作的结果，强制模型将状态转移归因于特定动作。

Result: 显著提升了动作可控性，并有效泛化至未见领域。

Conclusion: 结合主动数据获取与因果优化，OnlineWM建立了闭环精炼过程，确保模型在多样场景中鲁棒且因果归因精确。

Abstract: Generative world models aim to predict future states conditioned on actions, where action controllability is fundamental for reliable dynamics modeling. While recent efforts leverage simulator-generated data to enhance this capability, existing training pipelines face two fundamental limitations. First, static offline data collection leads to a distribution misalignment between training sets and the model's evolving error patterns, failing to resolve critical long-tail scenarios where dynamics predictions remain unreliable. Second, the standard objective of minimizing observational discrepancy often encourages the model to exploit spurious correlations instead of capturing the underlying action-effect causality. To address these limitations, we propose OnlineWM, an online training framework that continuously improves world modeling through active simulator interaction and causality-aware optimization. OnlineWM introduces two key innovations: (1) Active Online Learning: Instead of using fixed datasets, OnlineWM adaptively queries the simulator for new interaction sequences that target the model's current predictive weaknesses, ensuring high-utility data acquisition. (2) Causality-Aware Fine-Tuning: We propose a counterfactual learning strategy that contrasts the outcomes of different actions from identical states, forcing the model to attribute state transitions to specific actions rather than ambient environmental evolution, thereby grounding its predictions in reliable causal mechanisms. By integrating active data acquisition with causal optimization, OnlineWM establishes a closed-loop refinement process that ensures the model is both robust to diverse scenarios and precise in its causal attribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OnlineWM significantly enhances action controllability and generalizes effectively to unseen domains.

</details>


### [116] [Training-Free Spectral Transductive Refinement for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23758)
*Fahim Rahman,S. M. Tanjeeb Meheran Rohan,Md. Taimum Ibne Sayed,Asaduzzaman Herok,Md. Bakhtiar Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Spectral Transductive Refinement (STR)的免训练转导推理方法，通过在测试时利用支持集和查询集的几何结构进行谱坐标下的迭代优化，显著提升了少样本识别在域偏移和单样本监督下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决冻结视觉特征在域偏移（domain shift）和单样本监督（one-shot supervision）场景下的脆弱性问题，特别是当仅有一个标记图像作为类别估计不可靠时。旨在不重新训练编码器或增强源域数据的前提下，仅通过测试时的处理来降低这种脆弱性。

Method: 提出Spectral Transductive Refinement (STR)，一种无需训练的转导推理规则。该方法构建联合k近邻图，将 episode 映射到归一化拉普拉斯谱坐标系中，从标记的支持集初始化类代表元，并利用伪标签查询数据进行迭代 refinement。

Result: 1. 在ResNet-18冻结特征的受控研究中，STR在五个性偏移域上均优于单原型谱初始化，尤其在支持估计最弱的单样本场景中提升最大。2. 在ResNet-10骨干网络的基准测试中，STR在8个目标域上实现了最高的1-shot平均准确率，且在5-shot设置下与依赖重源域元训练增强方法的性能相当。

Conclusion: STR作为一种纯推理阶段的转导方法，能有效提升跨域少样本学习的性能，特别是在极端少样本情况下。其性能增益主要来源于谱坐标下的迭代细化过程，而非增加原型容量。

Abstract: Few-shot recognition with frozen visual features is especially fragile under domain shift and one-shot supervision, where a single labelled image is an unreliable estimate of its class. We ask how far this fragility can be reduced purely at test time, without retraining the encoder or augmenting the source domain. We present Spectral Transductive Refinement (STR), a training-free transductive inference rule that exploits the geometry of the complete support-query episode. Given frozen embeddings, STR builds a joint k-nearest-neighbour graph, maps the episode into a normalized-Laplacian spectral coordinate system, initializes class representatives from the labelled support, and iteratively refines them using pseudo-labelled queries. We evaluate STR under two protocols. A controlled component study with frozen ResNet-18 features shows that spectral refinement consistently improves over single-prototype spectral initialization across five shifted domains, with the largest gains in the one-shot regime where support estimates are weakest. We then benchmark STR against recent Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning (CD-FSL) methods using the standard miniImageNet-pretrained ResNet-10 backbone over eight established target domains. Operating entirely at inference time, STR attains the highest 1-shot average among compared methods and remains competitive at 5-shot, rivalling approaches relying on heavy source-domain meta-training augmentations. Because STR is transductive, we report its setting explicitly. Diagnostics attribute its gains to iterative refinement in spectral coordinates rather than added prototype capacity, which remains inactive in our configuration.

</details>


### [117] [PRISM-RAG: Multimodal Hypergraph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Tobacco Product and Legislative Policy Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23769)
*Manuel Serna-Aguilera,Raegan Anderes,Page Dobbs,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对美国各司法管辖区烟草法规文本相似导致的检索冲突问题，提出NicoPRISM数据集和PRISM-RAG多模态超图检索增强生成框架。该框架通过无LLM索引的管辖权感知机制，显著提升了政策合规问答中法源定位的准确性（93.9%），优于传统RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法无法解决跨司法管辖区语义相似但法律语境不同的条文消歧问题。这种“上下文冲突”会导致生成模型基于相关但错误的司法管辖区来源自信地回答问题。特别是在电子烟等新兴产品监管中，需准确识别适用哪一地区的法律，而不仅仅是检索相关文本。

Method: 1. 构建NicoPRISM数据集：包含161,563张图像、属性描述、覆盖13个美国司法管辖区的产品/健康/立法文档知识库，以及1,495对经过验证的问题-答案对。
2. 提出PRISM-RAG框架：一种多模态超图检索增强生成架构。特点包括：索引阶段零LLM调用；基于图像、标题和实体构建超图；通过“管辖权感知上下文组装机制”确保检索到的法条来自查询指定的司法管辖区。

Result: 在政策合规查询中，PRISM-RAG能以93.9%的概率从正确的司法管辖区检索到段落，比标准RAG高出48.6个百分点（p<0.001）。该方法在关键词匹配、语义相关性、管辖权准确性和合规性准确率等多个指标上具有竞争力或优于最先进的RAG框架，且仅需一次查询时的LLM调用。

Conclusion: PRISM-RAG通过引入管辖权感知的多模态超图结构，有效解决了跨司法管辖区法规检索中的上下文冲突问题，实现了高精度的法源定位和政策合规性判断，为复杂监管领域的AI应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The disambiguation of semantically similar statutory text across jurisdictions is a retrieval problem that existing methods do not solve. This inter-context conflict can steer generative models toward confidently produced answers grounded in topically relevant but jurisdictionally incorrect sources. Tobacco and nicotine regulations vary by US jurisdiction, often sharing similar language, thus, robust reasoning requires identifying which jurisdiction's law governs a given product, not merely retrieving relevant text. Emerging products (e.g., pouches) exploit ambiguous definitions to evade regulation. State-of-the-art (SOTA) document retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods struggle to address this inter-context conflict, and thus struggle to connect image attributes (e.g., rich attribute captions) to the set of similar legislation texts. We introduce NicoPRISM (Nicotine Product and Regulation Image-and-Text Surveillance Multimodal), comprising 161,563 images, attribute captions, a knowledge base of product, health, and legislative documents spanning 13 US jurisdictions, and 1,495 validated question-answer pairs across two tasks: policy compliance QA and product knowledge QA. We also propose PRISM-RAG, a multimodal hypergraph RAG framework built over images, captions, and entities without any LLM calls at index time, grounding every query in a product image and routes retrieval through a jurisdiction-aware context assembly mechanism guaranteeing that statutory text from the queried jurisdiction reaches the language model by construction. PRISM-RAG retrieves passages from the correct jurisdiction in 93.9% of policy compliance queries, a 48.6 percentage point advantage over standard RAG (p<0.001), using zero LLM calls at index time and one at query time, and is competitive with or outperforms SOTA RAG frameworks across keyword, semantic, jurisdiction-, and compliance-accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [118] [Mira-Scene: Pixel-Aligned Layouts for Generative 3D Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23796)
*Yang-Tian Sun,Tianjia Liu,Zehuan Huang,Yi-Hua Huang,Xiaoyang Lyu,Ziyi Yang,Zi-Xin Zou,Yuan-Chen Guo,Yan-Pei Cao,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mira-Scene通过引入规范坐标图（CCM）和点云图（PCM），将稀疏姿态回归转化为稠密有界对应关系恢复，实现了从单张图片到高质量3D场景布局的高效重建，显著提升了布局准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单图像3D物体生成后，将其准确放置到连贯场景布局中的挑战。现有整体方法牺牲物体细节，而组合方法因使用稀疏无界姿态变量，导致学习困难且在场景级监督稀缺时泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Mira-Scene框架：1. 核心为规范坐标图（CCM），将可见物体像素映射到物体有界规范空间的表面坐标；2. 结合单目几何估计产生的场景空间点云图（PCM），通过鲁棒几何对齐恢复对象变换；3. 利用多模态扩散Transformer联合生成物体几何和CCMs，采用共享注意力和位置编码的特定模态专家流以促进几何-布局一致性。

Result: 在室内、室外、合成及真实世界场景上实验表明，Mira-Scene在布局准确性上大幅优于强基线模型。相比SAM3D，在有限开源训练数据下，3D-IoU相对提升39.8%，2D-IoU相对提升16.5%。

Conclusion: Mira-Scene通过有界规范空间的稠密对应恢复，解决了场景布局参数化的难题，无需场景级布局标注即可利用可扩展的物体级3D数据进行训练，实现了高保真且布局准确的3D场景重建。

Abstract: Single-image 3D object generation can now produce high-fidelity assets, yet accurately placing them into a coherent scene layout remains an open challenge. A central difficulty lies in how object layout is represented. Holistic methods absorb placement into a scene-level generation process, sacrificing object-level detail. Compositional methods preserve object fidelity by decoupling geometry from layout, but typically parameterize layout as sparse, unbounded pose variables that are difficult to learn and generalize poorly under scarce scene-level supervision.We present Mira-Scene, a compositional 3D scene reconstruction framework that replaces sparse pose regression with dense, bounded correspondence recovery. At its core is the Canonical Coordinate Map (CCM), a pixel-aligned field that maps each visible object pixel to a surface coordinate in the object's bounded canonical space. When paired with a scene-space Point Cloud Map (PCM) from monocular geometry estimation, CCM induces dense canonical-to-scene correspondences from which object transformations are recovered through robust geometric alignment. Because CCM operates in bounded canonical space, it provides a stable prediction target that can be trained from scalable object-level 3D data without requiring scene-level layout annotations. Mira-Scene further introduces a multimodal diffusion transformer that jointly generates object geometry and CCMs, using modality-specific expert streams with shared attention and positional encoding to promote geometry-layout consistency. Experiments on indoor, outdoor, synthetic, and in-the-wild scenes show that Mira-Scene substantially outperforms strong baselines in layout accuracy, achieving relative gains of 39.8% in 3D-IoU and 16.5% in 2D-IoU over SAM3D, using limited open-source training data.

</details>


### [119] [Confidence-Aware Teacher-Student Distillation for 3D Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23815)
*Georgios Triantafyllou,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅需单张2D切片上少量点提示的3D医学图像分割框架，通过离线教师模型生成带置信度的伪标签，并利用置信度感知优化策略训练学生网络，显著降低标注成本并提升分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有医学图像分割模型依赖大量密集注释数据、难以跨任务和成像模扩展的问题，旨在应对极端稀疏注释下的3D解剖结构预测挑战。

Method: 构建高效的师生框架：1. 基础模型作为离线教师，利用单切片上的点提示生成全体积伪注释及空间置信度；2. 针对特定任务的3D学生网络采用置信度感知优化策略进行训练，该策略在损失计算中排除静态不确定区域并强调高置信度区域，以减轻噪声伪标签导致的误差传播。

Result: 在3D心脏MRI数据集上评估，该方法优于最先进的半监督方法，分割性能提升高达43.6%；手动标注负担大幅减少（每卷仅需几个正点提示）；表面边界精度比教师模型提高14.7%；成功恢复了向完全监督上限差距的34.1%。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了在极端稀疏注释下实现高效、高精度的自动3D医学图像分割的可行性，为降低标注成本和提升模型可扩展性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models typically rely on large amounts of densely annotated volumetric data, limiting their scalability across tasks and imaging modalities. This work addresses the challenge of predicting entire 3D anatomical structures from extreme annotation sparsity. An annotation-efficient student-teacher framework is proposed for automatic 3D medical segmentation that requires only a set of point prompts on a single 2D slice per volume, as input. A foundation model serves as an offline teacher, utilizing the provided point prompts from the selected slice to full-volume pseudo-annotations alongside their corresponding spatial confidence scores prior to student training. To mitigate the error propagation of noisy pseudo-annotations, a task-specific 3D student network is trained using a confidence-aware optimization strategy. By leveraging the teacher's pre-computed confidence scores, this strategy explicitly excludes statically uncertain regions of the pseudo-annotations from the loss calculation, while simultaneously emphasizing regions with higher confidence. Evaluated on 3D cardiac MRI datasets, our framework outperforms state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, improving segmentation performance by up to 43.6%. Furthermore, it drastically reduces the manual annotation burden to just a few positive point prompts per volume, while improving surface boundary precision by up to 14.7% over the teacher and successfully recovering up to 34.1% of the performance gap toward the fully supervised upper bound.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Recognition, Simulation, and Refusal: A Contamination-Aware Study of Classic Psychological Effects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22090)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出PsyAgentBench基准，通过因子设计区分LLM是否真正具备人类心理偏差。结果显示，LLM表现出的类人效应源于多种不同机制（如标签门控、知识依赖、安全过滤等），而非单一的心理易感性；简单的角色设定即可消除或逆转这些效应，证明不能简单用标量偏差分数来概括LLM行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清“LLM产生与人类心理效应相关的响应模式”不等于“LLM拥有该心理偏差”，并解决现有评估中因混淆实验条件而导致的归因模糊问题。

Method: 构建PsyAgentBench基准，在5个经典心理学范式上对开源LLM进行41,904次试验。采用因子设计，交叉操纵三个变量：任务呈现方式（显式标注名称 vs. 盲法常规任务）、任务版本（标准教科书版 vs. 减少训练数据重叠的反事实版）以及人格操控。

Result: 发现类人效应通过五种定性不同的路径产生：Asch从众效应受标签门控主导；锚定效应依赖可用信号（理性使用）；框架效应在新内容下被放大；沉没成本谬误表现为稳健缺失；最小群体分配涉及安全拒绝。单人设改变可消除、减弱或逆转效应。

Conclusion: LLM中的心理现象并非由单一响应偏差账户解释，而是由 persona dominance（人格主导）、population collapse（种群崩溃）和 safety selection（安全选择）三种机制导致。建议放弃标量偏差易感性评分，转而报告详细的复制配置文件以准确描述LLM行为。

Abstract: An LLM producing the response pattern associated with a human psychological effect is not the same claim as the LLM possessing that bias. We present PsyAgentBench, a benchmark that re-runs classic psychology experiments on LLM agents under a factorial design built to separate these: each paradigm is run with the paradigm explicitly labeled in the prompt (named) or framed as a routine task (blind), and on the literal textbook version of the task (canonical) or a structurally matched variant written to reduce lexical and scenario overlap with likely training data (counterfactual), crossed with a persona manipulation. Across five completed paradigms, evaluated on up to three open-weight model families with 41,904 trials released, apparently human-like effects arise through qualitatively different routes rather than one susceptibility: paradigm-label gating with explicit override (Asch conformity, 0 percent blind to 83.3 percent named on gpt-oss-120B), knowledge-dependent signal reliance (anchoring, exactly zero on grounded facts versus near total on invented quantities, a pattern equally consistent with rational use of the only available signal), amplification on novel content under labeling (framing), robust absence (sunk cost), and safety-mediated selection where refusal itself is the primary finding (minimal-group allocation). A one-sentence persona change (agreeableness, framed as an instruction rather than a verified trait manipulation) eliminates, dampens, or reverses these effects depending on which effect it is, arguing against any single response-bias account. We further formalize, and in two cases document empirically, three ways a psychology paradigm can fail to port to LLM agents: persona dominance, population collapse, and safety selection. We argue scalar bias-susceptibility scores obscure this structure and report replication profiles instead.

</details>


### [121] [Memory That Looks Forward: A Zero-Inference Prospective Term for Personal Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22091)
*Jonathan Groff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需推理成本的“前瞻性”记忆检索机制，通过显式账本中的承诺条目为相关记忆项提供显著性提升，在合成任务中将召回率从0提升至1.0，且无错误提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个人记忆库检索是回顾性的，仅基于相似度匹配，无法处理用户已承诺执行的未来任务（即缺乏前瞻性）。

Method: 构建显式账本存储带日期或触发条件的承诺；将链接到触发条目的记忆项嵌入与基础检索结果进行乘法混合，使相关性保持主导，同时实现零推理成本的前瞻性增强。

Result: 在模拟TriggerBench结构的合成任务中，默认混合权重下硬子集的recall@5从0.000提升至0.955，地板变体下达到1.000；在53个已解决承诺任务中零假阳性；发现仅17-29%的自然语言承诺触发对需要此机制，其余情况无负面影响。

Conclusion: 预计算承诺链接可作为分层设计的底层支持，上层为查询时前瞻扩展；当前结果为初步验证，需在TriggerBench正式数据发布后进行后续评估。

Abstract: Retrieval over a personal memory store is retrospective: it surfaces what resembles the query, and it is blind to what the user has committed to do. We describe a prospective term for memory retrieval that costs no inference at query time. Commitments are held in an explicit ledger as dated or trigger-conditioned entries; memory items linked to a firing entry receive a salience boost, blended multiplicatively into embedding-based retrieval so that relevance remains sovereign. On a synthetic prospective-memory task set modeled on TriggerBench's published structure (48 blind-authored dialogues, 175 tasks), the term raised recall@5 on the hard stratum from 0.000 to 0.955 at the default blend weight and to 1.000 under a floor variant, with zero false boosts across 53 resolved-commitment tasks. Blind authorship also produced a scope finding: only 17-29% of naturally phrased commitment-trigger pairs defeat embedding similarity, so the term matters on a real minority of cases and must do no harm on the rest, which it does not. We position precomputed commitment linkage as the always-on floor of a layered design whose expansion layer is query-time prospection. Results are preliminary: the evaluation set is author-constructed, and evaluation on TriggerBench proper is committed follow-up work once its data is released.

</details>


### [122] [Summarize, Judge, Refine: Decoupled Content Understanding and Policy Learning for Multimodal Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22094)
*Zeeshan Ahmed,Yang Qin,Hanqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SJR架构，解耦多模态理解与策略分类，通过文本摘要实现可解释性、数据增强及零样本策略启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核系统混淆了多模态理解与特定策略分类，导致每次策略变更需全管线重训练，且因多媒体难以有效增强而面临标签稀缺问题。

Method: 提出“总结-判断-优化”(SJR)双模型架构：多模态Content Model生成结构化文本摘要，纯文本Policy Model依据策略定义进行分类；通过GRPO迭代协同训练优化Content Model，并利用文本空间对抗性摘要变体进行数据增强，实现少样本策略引导。

Result: 在误导性广告检测任务中，SJR相比零-shot思维链基线，非误导性F1相对提升23.6%，优于端到端SFT、STaR/RFT及RLFT。值得注意的是，仅使用合成正类数据（零真实违规样本）训练的变体，其违规F1与全数据模型差距不足0.2%。

Conclusion: SJR架构不仅提供了基于人类可读摘要的结构化可解释性，还证明了新策略可在无任何真实违规数据的情况下快速启动，解决了传统方法的数据稀缺和重训练瓶颈。

Abstract: Content moderation systems traditionally entangle multimodal understanding with policy-specific classification, requiring full pipeline retraining for every policy change and suffering from label scarcity since multimedia cannot be meaningfully augmented. We propose Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR), a two-model architecture that decouples these concerns via a natural language interface: a multimodal Content Model produces structured text summaries, and a text-only Policy Model classifies them against policy definitions. An iterative co-training loop refines the Content Model via GRPO to produce policy-relevant summaries, while text-space augmentation generates adversarial summary variants---an augmentation pathway impossible on raw multimedia---enabling few-shot policy bootstrap. Every decision is grounded in a human-readable summary, providing interpretability as a structural byproduct. On misleading advertisement detection, SJR achieves +23.6\% relative non-misleading F1 over a zero-shot chain-of-thought baseline, outperforming end-to-end SFT, STaR/RFT, and RLFT. Notably, a variant trained on zero real violating examples---with all positive-class data synthetically generated---matches the full-data model within 0.2\% relative on violating F1, demonstrating that new policies can launch without any real violation data.

</details>


### [123] [AI-inferred expressed well-being and collective-action discourse in climate-change campaigns on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22096)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现气候宣传活动期间，公众情绪中的“幸福感”显著上升，但指向具体行动的“行动语言”却显著下降，揭示了“幸福-行动背离”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管气候活动常通过关注度和动员力来评估，但其伴随的福祉语言（如积极情绪、希望）尚不明确。本研究旨在解决两个核心问题：活动期间是否改变积极情感和希望？幸福感是否与行动语言一致？

Method: 分析了19个年份中地球日、地球一小时等4个气候节日期间的364,118条Twitter/X帖子。采用30天预事件、事件及后事件窗口，使用版本化加权词汇模型估算幸福感、未来导向的希望、集体能力、痛苦和行动语言。

Result: 1. 事件期间的幸福感 prevalence 比基线高出9.02个百分点；2. 配对对比显示行动语言下降了10.75个百分点，证实了“幸福-行动背离”；3. 幸福感估计在组成和文本去重检查中保持正向，但在19聚类野Bootstrap下精度较低；4. 来源帖子越快乐，观察到匹配转发级联的概率越低。

Conclusion: 气候宣传提高了公众的幸福感，但这种情感提升并未转化为更多的行动语言或传播行为，反而显示出幸福感与实际行动意愿之间的背离。

Abstract: Climate campaigns are often evaluated through attention and mobilization, but less is known about the well-being language that accompanies them. Whether campaign periods alter positive affect and hope, and whether happiness aligns with action language, remains unresolved. We analysed 364,118 public Twitter/X posts from Earth Day, Earth Hour, Global Climate Action Day and World Environment Day in 19 occurrence-years, using 30-day pre-event, event and post-event windows. A versioned weighted lexical model estimated happiness, future-oriented hope, collective capability, distress and action language. Event-period happiness prevalence was 9.02 percentage points higher than the pre-event baseline , whereas paired occurrence contrasts showed a 10.75-point decline in action language, indicating a happiness--action divergence. The happiness estimate remained positive across composition and text-deduplication checks, but was less precise under a 19-cluster wild bootstrap. Happier source posts had lower odds of an observed matched retweet cascade.

</details>


### [124] [Token Signatures of Code: Comparing Coding Behaviors Across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22097)
*Junpeng Wang,Yuzhong Chen,Menghai Pan,Uday Singh Saini,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLIC方法，通过Token频率分析和可视化系统比较不同大语言模型(LLM)的代码行为差异，而非仅评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码评估主要依赖pass@k等性能指标，随着模型能力提升，这些指标的区分度降低，且缺乏对LLM编码行为差异的深入探索。

Method: 提出CLIC（Code Learning for Identification and Comparison），将代码样本表示为Token频率特征向量，训练可解释决策树分离两个LLM的代码集；定义鲁棒性（robustness）和集中度（concentration）新指标；开发交互式可视分析系统进行多尺度假设驱动探索。

Result: 通过对10个LLM在22个Kaggle机器学习任务上的案例研究，揭示了LLM选择提示工程的可操作见解，验证了该方法在多模型、多任务对比中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于行为的比较分析能提供更细致的LLM差异化洞察，补充传统性能评估的不足，有助于优化LLM选择和提示工程策略。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on coding tasks has primarily focused on performance metrics such as pass@k. As LLMs continue to advance, many models now meet baseline performance requirements, reducing the discriminative power of performance-based evaluation alone. Yet a key question remains largely unexplored: how do LLMs differ in their coding behavior? We propose CLIC (Code Learning for Identification and Comparison), a visual analytics approach that characterizes LLM coding behavior through token-frequency analysis. CLIC represents each code sample as a feature vector of token frequencies and trains an interpretable decision tree to separate two LLMs' code sets. Beyond classification accuracy, we define two new metrics: robustness, which measures whether the two LLMs remain distinguishable as their most-discriminative tokens are progressively removed, and concentration, which measures whether the difference is driven by a few dominant tokens or spread across many. Interpreting numerous pairwise comparisons (across LLM pairs, tasks, and tokenization levels) and tracing the full analytical chain form an inherently multi-scale, hypothesis-driven exploration task. We therefore develop an interactive visual analytics system to navigate the comparison landscape, identify pairs of interest, and drill down into discriminative tokens and their code contexts. Case studies comparing 10 LLMs across 22 Kaggle ML tasks reveal actionable insights for LLM selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [125] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TreeSpark，一种基于自适应树结构的推测解码方法，通过利用起草者模型的马尔可夫头信息校准边缘接受估计，实现最佳优先扩展和负载自适应策略，显著提升推理速度和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块起草器虽高效但缺乏多样性；现有的树结构推测解码因忽略父节点依赖关系导致候选项排序错误，且固定大小的树无法适应不同解码轮次和服务负载的变化，限制了性能增益。

Method: 引入TreeSpark：1) 从起草者现有的马尔可夫头读取父条件分布，以极低成本校准为边缘接受估计；2) 由路径存活率驱动最佳优先扩展、每轮停止和负载自适应服务策略；3) 采用无放回采样兄弟节点及递归拒绝中的匹配残差，确保任意温度下的无损解码。

Result: 在相同温度下，相比匹配的固定预算方案表现更优；与调优后的链式起草相比，每轮多接受15-25%的草稿令牌，单请求墙钟时间加速8-14%；在高负载下能自动将树收缩回链式结构，保持稳定性。

Conclusion: TreeSpark通过自适应树结构和精确的边缘接受估计，有效解决了现有推测解码方法的排序错误和负载不敏感问题，实现了更高的解码效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [126] [Which Part of the Context Layer Does the Work? Separating Semantic Content from Retrieval Scaffolding in Text-to-SQL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22259)
*Qing Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过四臂消融实验证明，在文本到SQL任务中，数据契约（Data Contract）中的语义内容对准确率提升起主导作用，检索脚手架贡献极小，预计算视图仅作为上限参考。遵循“语义优先、脚手架次之、预计算仅在必要时使用”的原则可显著提升性能并降低错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出上下文层能大幅提升文本到SQL基准测试的准确率，但缺乏明确区分其中语义内容、检索脚手架和预计算视图各自具体贡献的方法，无法确定哪一部分真正起作用。

Method: 基于DABStep框架，对四个模型进行四臂消融实验。使用“数据契约”（YAML格式）作为工具，分离并独立测试语义内容、检索指令/规则以及预计算视图的影响，对比其与直接将知识嵌入提示词的效果。

Result: 1. 语义内容起决定性作用，使四个模型的难任务准确率分别从13.9%、22.6%、22.9%和37.0%提升至55.1%、56.6%、68.4%和77.4%，全面优于直接嵌入提示词。
2. 仅有脚手架而无内容的增益微乎其微（Flash模型0-5分，前沿模型14-15分）。
3. 数据契约比提示词更高效，因其SQL语义查找成功率高达98%（提示词仅为4%），且成本更低。
4. 受控臂未提交任何变异语句，而未受控臂提交了166条，证明了治理的有效性。

Conclusion: 在构建分析代理时，应优先确保领域语义的准确性，其次考虑检索脚手架，预计算宏仅应在代理确实无法推导时使用。该方法不仅提升了准确率，还显著降低了错误操作风险，尽管其增益局限于契约定义的领域内。

Abstract: Context layers, curated documentation that an analytics agent fetches at query time, produce large accuracy gains on text-to-SQL benchmarks. A with/without comparison cannot say which part of the layer does the work: the semantic content, the retrieval scaffolding that delivers it, or the pre-computed views that usually accompany it. We report a four-arm ablation on DABStep on four models that separates the three. The instrument is a data contract: a YAML artifact that carries a domain's semantics and the rules an agent's tools enforce. One arm empties every field of prose in the frozen contract while holding the tool surface, retrieval instruction, table allow-list and operation rules byte-for-byte fixed. Compiling the contract's own SQL expressions into views gives the ceiling a pre-computed layer would reach: gold on all 176 tasks it covers. Content dominates. It raises hard-task accuracy from 13.9% to 55.1%, 22.6% to 56.6%, 22.9% to 68.4% and 37.0% to 77.4%, beating the same knowledge pasted into the prompt on every model. Scaffolding without content is worth 0 to 5 points on two flash models and 14 to 15 on two frontier models. Against the compiled ceiling the contract arm's shortfall is a failure to derive, and it falls from 39 points to 5 with model capability. The contract beats the prompt because the rule it needs is one lookup away rather than buried in a long prompt: its SQL carries the fee semantics up to 98% of the time against the prompt arm's 4%, and at the lowest cost per correct answer on three of four models. For practitioners: semantics first, scaffolding second, pre-computed macros only where an agent demonstrably fails to derive. Ungoverned arms submitted 166 mutating statements; governed arms none. The gain is confined to the contract's domain. On one model the benchmark's own withheld golds grade the contract arm at 51.9% against 18.8% for the prompt baseline.

</details>


### [127] [A framework for recipe data structure with applications for culinary and nutritional insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22099)
*Mansi Goel,Sumit Bhagat,Saloni Srivastava,Malav Patel,Shlok Vinodkumar Mehroliya,Ganesh Bagler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RecipeDB2框架，将非结构化食谱文本转化为可计算的结构化数据，包含128,942条食谱和35,474种食材，实现营养、地域和文化属性的统一查询与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食谱数据多为自由文本，缺乏统一的共享表示形式，无法同时关联结构化的成分组成、地缘文化背景及营养概况，导致难以进行量化分析。

Method: 构建结构化框架，利用基于Transformer的命名实体识别模型解析七种烹饪属性；通过BERT嵌入策略将食材链接至USDA参考表以获取148项营养参数；使用随机森林分类器传播34类食材类别；并应用确定性规则集分配饮食风格。

Result: 建立了包含来自32个地区、99个国家128,942条食谱和35,474种食材的RecipeDB2数据库。在200个最频繁食材的手动审核集中，链接准确率达到F1=87.90，实现了从艺术性描述到数据驱动分析的转化。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了食谱数据表示的空白，提供了一个可扩展的框架，使食谱变得可计算，从而将文化遗产转化为可量化的研究对象，支持后续的数据驱动分析。

Abstract: Cooking is a complex process that transforms raw ingredients into delicious and nutritious dishes, yet the recipes that encode this process remain largely free text; readable by people but not directly computable. Existing recipe collections capture fragments of this information, but no shared representation links a recipe's structured ingredient composition, its geo-cultural provenance, and its nutritional profile within a single queryable schema. We address this representation gap by formalizing a framework for recipe data structure that decomposes each recipe into typed ingredient entities, grounds those entities in a reference nutritional database, and annotates them with geo-cultural and dietary context. We present RecipeDB2, a structured compilation of 128,942 recipes with 35,474 ingredients from 32 regions and 99 countries. Ingredient phrases are parsed into seven culinary attributes using a transformer-based named-entity model; ingredients are linked to the USDA reference tables through a BERT embedding strategy (F1 = 87.90 on a manually adjudicated set of the 200 most frequent ingredients), yielding 148 nutritional parameters per mapped ingredient; a Random Forest classifier propagates 34 ingredient categories across the full vocabulary; and a deterministic, conservative rule set assigns each recipe a dietary style. Through RecipeDB2 (https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/), we demonstrate a scalable framework for making recipes computable, turning culinary heritage (long treated as an artistic rather than a quantitative object) into a data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [128] [AdaMem: Adaptive Memory Token Allocation for Soft Compression in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22100)
*Artem Sakhno,Grigorii Davydenko,Omar Zoloev,Julia Belikova,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaMem，一种通过相关性引导的软压缩框架，动态分配记忆token以优化长文本检索增强生成（RAG）的效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软压缩方法通常对每个保留段落分配相同数量的记忆嵌入，忽略了其与查询的相关性差异，导致在长段落处理中成本高且可能引入干扰信息。

Method: AdaMem框架利用共享的查询条件压缩器，在一次传递中同时生成连续段落记忆和相关性分数；通过确定性分配规则，将固定记忆预算优先分配给高相关性段落，省略低相关性段落。

Result: 在六个开放域QA基准上，AdaMem优于均匀分配的OSCAR及其他软压缩方法。在16x压缩下平均相对提升3.4%，64x压缩下平均提升14.6%（PopQA最高提升19.7%）。在达到未压缩答案质量时，推理延迟降低至全上下文基线的1/4。

Conclusion: 相关性引导的记忆分配在检索池大且记忆预算紧张的情况下尤为有效，能在保持高效能的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models with retrieved evidence, but processing many long passages is costly and can introduce distracting information. Soft compression addresses this challenge by encoding passages as compact sequences of continuous memory embeddings before generation. However, existing methods typically assign each retained passage an identical number of memory embeddings, irrespective of its query-specific relevance. To address this, we propose AdaMem, a relevance-guided soft-compression framework that maps learned passage-relevance estimates to a query-dependent allocation of a fixed memory-token budget. A shared query-conditioned compressor produces both continuous passage memories and relevance scores in a single pass; a deterministic allocation rule assigns more memory tokens to higher-scoring passages and can omit low-scoring ones. Across six open-domain QA benchmarks, AdaMem consistently outperforms OSCAR (the closely matched soft-compression baseline that uses uniform allocation) as well as other soft-compression methods at matched memory budgets. Under standard 16$\times$ compression, AdaMem improves sub-string match by up to 3.2 points (5.5%) over uniform allocation baseline, with an average relative gain of 3.4%; under aggressive 64$\times$ compression the average relative gain grows to 14.6%, with a maximum of 9.8 points (19.7%) on PopQA. AdaMem matches the answer quality of the uncompressed at up to 4$\times$ lower inference latency than full context baseline. AdaMem retains an efficiency profile comparable to the uniform-compression baseline, while achieving up to $4\times$ lower inference latency than full-context inference. Thus, relevance-guided memory allocation is particularly effective when retrieval pools are large and the available memory budget is tight.

</details>


### [129] [An Iterative LangGraph Agent for Text-to-SQL: Natural Language Access to the Chicago Crime Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22917)
*Vigneshwar Ravi Rao,Rupesh Swarnakar,Fayeq Jeelani Syed†*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个基于LangGraph的Text-to-SQL智能体，通过纯提示工程（无微调）解决非技术人员无法编写SQL的问题。在芝加哥犯罪数据集上的测试显示，优化后的系统V2版本实现了93%的有效SQL率和60%的执行准确率，主要改进源于移除限制返回行数的指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非技术利益相关者通常无法编写从操作数据库中提取洞察所需的SQL代码，存在技术鸿沟。

Method: 构建了一个六节点的LangGraph StateGraph智能体，流程包括：检查问题相关性、获取实时模式、生成PostgreSQL查询、干运行验证、失败重试、执行查询及用自然语言叙述结果。仅使用提示工程，未对模型进行微调。在包含100个自然语言问题的基准测试上评估，数据来自约850万条记录的芝加哥犯罪数据集。

Result: 对比两个提示修订版本，V2版本将有效SQL率从87%提升至93%，执行准确率从47%提升至60%（混合关系等价性指标），合成质量平均分从3.91提升至4.34/5。最大改进驱动因素是移除系统提示中的LIMIT 10指令，避免了多行答案被截断。剩余错误归因于相关性检查器的误拒、问题语义歧义及API速率限制。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了通过精心设计的提示工程和状态机工作流，可以在不进行模型微调的情况下显著提升Text-to-SQL的性能和实用性，尽管其局限性在于缺乏外部基线对比和公共基准测试。

Abstract: Non-technical stakeholders frequently cannot write the SQL needed to extract insights from operational databases. We built and evaluated a Text-to-SQL agent that closes this gap end to end: a six-node LangGraph StateGraph checks question relevance, fetches the live schema, generates PostgreSQL, validates it with a dry run, retries on failure, executes the query, and narrates the result set in plain English. The agent uses prompt engineering only; no model was fine-tuned. We evaluated it on the Chicago Crime dataset (approximately 8.5 million records, 22 attributes) against a hand-built benchmark of 100 natural language questions with ground-truth SQL, stratified into 30 Easy, 40 Medium and 30 Hard items. Comparing two prompt revisions of the same agent, the revised system (V2) reached a Valid SQL Rate of 93% (from 87%), an Execution Accuracy of 60% under a hybrid relational equivalence metric (from 47%; 19% from 12% under strict JSON matching), and a mean Synthesis Quality of 4.34 out of 5 (from 3.91). The single largest driver was removing a LIMIT 10 instruction from the system prompt, which had been truncating multi-row answers. Error analysis attributes the residual failures to relevance-checker false rejections, ambiguous question semantics, and free-tier API rate limits rather than to the language generation step. We report no comparison against an external baseline system or a public benchmark; the study is a single-model engineering evaluation.

</details>


### [130] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Bhaskar Sen,Nasim Sabetpour,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在处理长文本时出现的“上下文中毒”现象，即随着无关或混淆信息增加，模型定位关键证据的能力下降。研究通过理论推导和实验验证了该现象的成因及缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能处理越来越长的提示词，但在加入无关或易混淆的上下文后，其定位和使用决定性证据的能力会退化。本研究旨在形式化这一现象（称为“上下文中毒”），并探究其背后的机制及对检索准确性的影响。

Method: 1. 理论建模：将上下文中毒表述为注意力机制中的极值干扰，在Softmax检索抽象下推导有限样本上界，证明保持固定准确率需证据边际随有效干扰项数量N按Ω(√log N)缩放。
2. 关联分析：将长上下文性能退化与分数别名(aliasing)、位置别名和Softmax稀释联系起来。
3. 控制实验：测试嵌入硬负例、同格式干扰等不同构造方式对检索准确率的影响，并评估检索门控机制的效果。

Result: 1. 理论结果：维持固定准确率要求证据边际随有效干扰项数量增加而扩大。
2. 实验发现：
   - 存在嵌入式硬负例时，总上下文增长导致检索准确率下降。
   - 同格式条件导致的准确率下降幅度最大。
   - 检索门控可改善证据使用，但其净收益取决于是否保留证据召回率。
3. 归因：性能退化源于分数别名、位置别名和Softmax稀释。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持提出以下改进方向：证据瓶颈设计、抗别名的表示方法、“检索-推理”架构、验证器介导的记忆机制以及对比式抗中毒训练。这些方法有助于提升长上下文场景下的证据定位能力。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [131] [DeepInstructor: An Agentic AI Instructor for Experience-Driven Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22104)
*Rongcan Pei,Fang Guo,Qinglin Qi,Qi Zhu,Yun Luo,Jianhao Yan,Minjun Zhu,Qiujie Xie,Dehong Zheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DeepInstructor框架，通过将想法评估转化为基于结构化学术经验的推理，解决LLM生成研究想法后的评估瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估器依赖参数化知识或非结构化检索，缺乏人类导师式的经验驱动推理，导致判断质量不足。

Method: 构建包含58,607条同行评审的“经验图谱”（Experience Graph），利用基于ReAct的智能体检索特定维度的证据进行可追溯评估；同时引入DeepInstruct数据集，涵盖新颖性、重要性和可行性三个维度的成对比较。

Result: DeepInstructor显著优于现有基线，在Hit@1和Hit@2指标上分别比基线提高24.4%和29.7%，更贴合人类判断。

Conclusion: 科学想法评估可以通过对结构化学术经验进行显式推理来实现，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: As automated scientific discovery advances, Large Language Models (LLMs) can now generate research ideas at an unprecedented scale, shifting the bottleneck from idea generation to idea evaluation. Existing evaluators mainly rely on parametric LLM knowledge or unstructured retrieval, producing judgments that lack the experience-grounded reasoning used by human instructors. To address this, we propose DeepInstructor, an agentic framework that formulates idea evaluation as reasoning over structured scholarly experience. DeepInstructor constructs an Experience Graph from 58,607 peer reviews and employs a ReAct-based agent to retrieve dimension-specific evidence for traceable evaluation. We further introduce DeepInstruct, a dataset with controlled pairwise comparisons across novelty, significance, and feasibility. Experiments show that DeepInstructor substantially outperforms existing baselines, improving Hit@1 and Hit@2 alignment with human judgments by 24.4% and 29.7%, respectively. Our findings suggest that scientific idea evaluation can be grounded in explicit reasoning over structured scholarly experience

</details>


### [132] [Evaluating Fine-Tuned and Base Language Models in Maternal and Vaccination Healthcare for African Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22110)
*Abdulquddus Ajibade,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Iyinoluwa Animasaun,Chioma Nwakanma-Akanno,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Oluwafunke Akinbuwa,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了针对尼日利亚母婴健康和疫苗接种领域微调的大语言模型（LLM）效果。结果显示，基于高质量数据的母婴健康模型（MamaBot-Llama）显著优于基础模型，而疫苗接种模型（Vax-Llama）性能下降，表明数据质量对领域微调至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在资源匮乏地区改善医疗信息传递方面具有潜力，但可能产生不准确或文化不适宜的建议。本研究旨在评估针对特定领域（母婴健康和疫苗接种）的微调是否能提高模型在准确性、安全性及语境适宜性等方面的表现。

Method: 研究对比了HelpMum的MamaBot-Llama和Vax-Llama与Meta Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct基础模型。通过低秩自适应（LoRA）技术，分别使用超过36,000对和9,000对问答数据对模型进行微调。由两名尼日利亚注册医师依据5点Likert量表独立评估200个医疗问题的回答，并使用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行配对比较。

Result: MamaBot-Llama在所有评估标准上均显著优于基础模型，整体提升4.9%（p < .001），其中临床可信度提升7%，医学准确性提升5%，关键问题减少50%，且医生在78%的情况下更偏好该模型。相反，Vax-Llama整体性能下降5.2%（p < .001），关键问题增加192%，安全隐患增加400%。

Conclusion: 基于高质量、经临床专家筛选的数据进行领域特定微调可以提升医疗LLM的性能；然而，若数据集质量不足，则可能导致性能退化。在临床部署前必须进行严格的领域特定验证。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) can improve healthcare information delivery in low-resource settings but may produce inaccurate or culturally inappropriate advice. This study evaluated domain-specific fine-tuning for maternal health and vaccination in Nigeria. Objective: To compare HelpMum's MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama with Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct for accuracy, safety, clarity, contextual appropriateness, and trustworthiness. Methods: We evaluated 200 healthcare questions, 100 each for maternal health and vaccination, across five subdomains per domain. MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation on over 36,000 maternal health and 9,000 vaccination question-answer pairs, respectively. Two Nigerian licensed physicians independently rated responses using a 5-point Likert scale. Paired comparisons used Wilcoxon signed-rank tests. Results: Performance varied by domain. MamaBot-Llama significantly outperformed the base model across all criteria, with a 4.9% overall improvement (p < .001), including gains in clinical trustworthiness (+7%) and medical accuracy (+5%). Critical issues decreased by 50%, and clinicians preferred it in 78% of cases. In contrast, Vax-Llama showed a 5.2% overall decline (p < .001), with critical issues increasing by 192% and safety concerns by 400%. Conclusions: Domain-specific fine-tuning can improve healthcare LLM performance when based on high-quality, clinician-curated data, but may also degrade performance when dataset quality is inadequate. Rigorous domain-specific validation is essential before clinical deployment. Physician evaluators provided informed consent, and chatbot logs were anonymized. Keywords: Large language models; Fine-tuning; Maternal health; Vaccination; Healthcare AI; Low-resource settings; Nigeria; Model evaluation; LoRA; Medical accuracy

</details>


### [133] [Beyond the Text: Verifying That Agent-Written Papers Are Backed by Their Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22111)
*Qiuhong Shen,Benlong Wu,Hanjin Liu,Yuang Qi,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReAgent框架，通过静态和动态审计自动化检测大语言模型代理生成的研究文档与代码仓库之间的一致性，解决现有评审无法识别硬编码指标或未实现方法等不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审查实践主要评估文本质量，无法可靠识别大语言模型代理生成研究中存在的严重不一致性（如硬编码指标、未实现的方法或不受支持的实验结果），亟需一种能验证报告发现是否由实际实现和证据支持的方法。

Method: 构建结构化科学声明以指导仓库分析和证据收集；结合静态审计（检查方法论、实现和配置在仓库中的一致性）和动态审计（执行实验并收集执行证据）；最终生成结构化的仓库级审计报告以实现透明的证据追溯。

Result: 在人工策划的基准测试上评估表明，ReAgent能有效识别报告的研究发现与支持性仓库证据之间的不一致性，且优于单一的静态或基于复现的基线方法。

Conclusion: ReAgent提供了一种有效的自动化审计机制，能够揭示单一视角下隐藏的不一致性，确保AI生成研究的透明度和可信度。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly capable of conducting research autonomously, producing research documents alongside the code and experiments that ostensibly support them. Yet whether the reported findings are consistently supported by corresponding implementations and execution evidence remains largely unexplored: existing review practices primarily assess textual quality and cannot reliably identify inconsistencies such as hard-coded metrics, unimplemented methods, or unsupported experimental results. We present ReAgent, an automated auditing framework for assessing the consistency between agent-generated research documents and their associated repositories. ReAgent constructs structured representations of scientific claims from research documents and uses them to guide repository analysis and evidence collection. Static auditing examines whether claimed methodologies, implementations, and experimental configurations are consistently reflected in the repository, while dynamic auditing executes relevant experiments and collects execution evidence to assess empirical findings. By combining static analysis with dynamic evidence, ReAgent identifies inconsistencies that may remain hidden under either perspective alone, such as experiments that reproduce reported numbers while deviating from the claimed methodology. The collected evidence and audit decisions are organized into a structured repository-level audit report, enabling transparent evidence traceability. We evaluate ReAgent on a manually curated benchmark of agent-generated research document--repository pairs and compare it against representative static and reproduction-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that ReAgent effectively identifies inconsistencies between reported research findings and their supporting repository evidence.

</details>


### [134] [Privacy Personalization Trade offs in LLMs: The Impact of Stylometric Signal Reduction on User-Specific Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22112)
*Muhammed Nazmul Arefin,Omar Jamal Hammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在文本个性化生成中，去除可识别的风格特征（如人口统计、文化引用等）对个性化效果的影响。研究发现，虽然去除这些信号能保护隐私，但会显著降低模型模仿作者写作风格的能力，揭示了隐私与个性化之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能高保真地生成用户特定的文本，但这种个性化往往依赖于嵌入 demographic、文化和风格标记的个人数据，引发了关于“风格重识别”（stylometric re-identification）的隐私担忧。因此，需要探究减少这些可识别的风格信号是否会影响个性化生成的质量。

Method: 1. 构建受控框架，使用 LaMP-7 Twitter 基准测试隔离 LLM 个性化中的风格信号。
2. 选取250名采样用户，对比两种设置：基于原始个人资料的改写 vs. 基于匿名化转换个人资料（系统中和了人口统计标识、文化参考、个人细节和非正式语言线索）的改写。
3. 通过两个独立的 LLM 裁判和补充的人类评估来评估输出结果。

Result: 1. 基于原始资料的条件输出几乎无法与人类 authored 的真实数据区分开，表明现代 LLM 能以高保真度重现作者风格。
2. 基于匿名化资料的条件输出，其偏好率平均降至 13.0%，尽管语义上下文保持率仍高达 94.8%。
3. 人类评估者确认了相同模式，即匿名化处理显著降低了输出的个性化风格匹配度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了明显的隐私-个性化权衡（privacy-personalization trade-off）。为了平衡两者，需要开发隐私感知的个性化方法，这些方法应在抑制识别性风格信号的同时保留语义意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to generate user-specific text with high stylistic fidelity. However, the personal data that enables such personalization frequently embeds demographic, cultural, and stylistic markers that raises concerns about stylometric re- identification. This paper investigates whether reducing identifiable stylistic signals affects personalization in text generation by LLMs. We introduce a controlled framework to isolate stylometric signals in LLM personalization using the LaMP-7 Twitter benchmark. Experiments on 250 sampled users compare two settings: paraphrasing conditioned on the original profile and paraphrasing conditioned on an anonymized converted profile in which demographic identifiers, cultural references, personal details, and informal linguistic cues have been systematically neutralized. Outputs are assessed by two independent LLM judges and a complementary human evaluation. Our pairwise evaluation shows that outputs conditioned on original profiles are nearly indistinguishable from human-authored ground truth, indicating that modern LLMs can closely reproduce an author's writing style with sufficient fidelity. In contrast, preference for model outputs with anonymized profiles drops to 13.0% on average, while semantic context preservation remains high at 94.8%. A study with human evaluators confirms the same pattern. These findings reveal a clear privacy-personalization trade-off and highlight the need for privacy-aware personalization methods that retain meaning while suppressing identifying stylistic signals.

</details>


### [135] [An Empirical Cost Attribution of Context-Compression Gateways in Multi-Turn Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22114)
*Luzhuo Chen,Jiayu Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在生产环境中部署压缩网关，分析了LLM编程代理在多轮对话中的Token成本构成。研究发现：工具模式过滤（固定节省）和文件读取内容压缩（累积二次方增长节省）是主要杠杆，而历史摘要效果有限；单轮基准测试的高压缩率不能直接等同于多轮实际场景的成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管上下文压缩被广泛认为是降低LLM编程代理Token成本的手段，且公开基准显示其能保持任务质量，但业界普遍假设“压缩文件读取能在真实多轮代理中省钱”缺乏实证支持。本文旨在通过实测分解真实会话的Token账单，验证各压缩策略的实际经济效应。

Method: 在编码代理（Claude Code, Codex）与前沿LLM之间部署生产级压缩网关（Paritok），在受控A/B测试环境下隔离测量三种独立杠杆的效果：1. 工具模式过滤（Tool-schema filtering）；2. 文件读取及工具输出的内容压缩；3. 历史摘要（History summarization）。同时评估非破坏性网关的回取机制对成本的影响。

Result: 1. 工具模式过滤每轮固定节省21K-57K Token，随轮数N线性增长，是唯一稳定有效的节省手段。2. 内容压缩每轮仅节省缓存前缀约2%，但因压缩后的片段在多轮历史中重复发送，累计节省呈二次方增长（约3350*N^2），在第6轮左右超过工具过滤，直至达到上下文窗口上限。3. 非破坏性网关允许按需回取原始数据，每次回取成本固定且有界，不会导致成本爆炸，但大量回取会抵消累积节省。4. Paritok-4B模型在SWE-bench上以25.7%压缩率保留86.5%质量，但该单轮指标与多轮代理成本节约正交，不可混为一谈。

Conclusion: 单轮压缩基准测试结果不能直接作为多轮代理成本节约的依据。在实际应用中，应优先利用工具模式过滤进行线性成本削减，并利用内容压缩的累积效应，同时警惕高频回取导致的收益抵消。研究为优化Token成本提供了可操作的策略建议。

Abstract: Context compression is widely proposed as a way to cut the token bill of LLM coding agents, and public benchmarks report that aggressive compression preserves task-solving quality. These two facts do not imply the third one commonly assumed: that compressing file reads saves money in a real multi-turn agent. We instrument a production compression gateway (Paritok) between coding agents (Claude Code, Codex) and frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet, GPT-5), and decompose the token bill of real sessions into three independent levers: tool-schema filtering, content compression of file reads and tool output, and history summarization. Measured in isolation under controlled A/B runs, the three save at fundamentally different rates. Tool-schema filtering removes a fixed block every turn, roughly 21K-57K tokens on a typical turn; it is linear in the turn count N and the only unambiguously and reproducibly positive lever. Content compression saves only about 2% of the cache-priced prefix per turn, but compressed reads accumulate in history and are re-sent on every later turn, so its cumulative saving grows quadratically, about 3350*N^2 tokens (measured), overtaking the fixed tool-filter saving within roughly 6 turns until the context window caps it. A non-destructive gateway lets the agent pull original bytes back on demand; each recall re-sends exactly the one segment just compressed away, so its cost is fixed and bounded rather than a multiplicative blowup, and heavy recall spends the accumulated saving back one segment at a time. Finally, a strong single-shot compression benchmark - 86.5% of SWE-bench quality retained at a 25.7% compression rate, achieved by the model this gateway deploys (Paritok-4B, reported separately) - is orthogonal to multi-turn agent cost and must not be cited as a cost-saving argument. We distill the results into an actionable recipe for where token-saving effort pays off.

</details>


### [136] [Evaluation Awareness Shifts from Format to Context with Model Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22119)
*Navraj Singh,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了小模型和大模型检测“评估意识”（Evaluation Awareness）的机制差异，并提出了一种结合提示清洗和激活反制的双路径干预方法，有效抑制了模型的评估感知行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估意识对模型评估构成严重威胁，但现有研究尚不清楚模型检测该意识的内在机制，尤其是不同规模模型间的差异。

Method: 1. 使用Gemma 3、Phi-3和Llama-3等模型进行实验；2. 采用思维链分析、表示探测和集成梯度归因技术探究检测机制；3. 提出并验证一种结合提示 sanitization 和 activation counter-steering 的双路径干预方法。

Result: 发现小模型依赖提示格式敏感性，大模型依赖高阶推理来检测评估意识。提出的双路径干预方法在200个高评估意识提示上实现了70.58%的行为翻转率，优于单一干预手段。

Conclusion: 评估意识的发展机制在不同规模模型中有所不同，有效的缓解策略需要同时解决提示层面和表示层面的信号。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness poses an unprecedented threat to model evaluation, but the mechanisms by which models detect it remain unknown. This study focuses on determining this and identifying contrasting mechanisms between smaller and larger models. While smaller models use the prompt's format sensitivity to detect evaluation, larger models often rely on higher-order reasoning to detect it. We evaluated Gemma 3 (1B, 4B, and 12B), Phi-3 (Mini and Medium), and Llama-3 8B using Chain-of-Thought analysis, representation probing, and Integrated Gradients attribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose a dual-pathway intervention that combines prompt sanitization with activation counter-steering to suppress both external evaluation triggers and their internal representations. Across 200 highly evaluation-aware prompts, our method achieves an average behavioral flip rate of 70.58\%, consistently outperforming either intervention alone. These results provide new insights into how evaluation awareness develops in compact language models and suggest that effective mitigation requires jointly addressing both prompt-level and representation-level signals.Datasets and codebase can be found in this \href{https://github.com/chahal-navi/Evaluation-Awareness-Compact-LLMs/tree/main}{Github Repository.}

</details>


### [137] [Balancing Reasoning and Hardware Constraints in RAG Pipelines for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22124)
*Illya Havrylov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了参加UNLP 2026多领域文档理解共享任务的系统。针对OCR耗时过长导致LLM推理时间受限的问题，团队开发了一种资源高效的混合检索增强生成（RAG）管道，采用BM25、BGE-M3和Cross-Encoder重排序，并使用4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B模型在NVIDIA T4 GPU上运行。最终系统在私有测试集上获得0.8095分，排名第10/15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在UNLP 2026共享任务中，从乌克兰语PDF文档中提取精确答案、文档ID和页码。由于扫描文档的OCR处理耗时巨大（5-7小时），严重挤压了大语言模型（LLM）的推理时间（仅剩约2小时处理500个问题），因此需要一种能在严格时间限制内稳定完成且高效的方法。

Method: 1. 构建混合RAG管道：结合BM25（稀疏检索）、BGE-M3（稠密检索）和Cross-Encoder进行重排序。
2. 模型选择与部署：放弃易超时的重型推理模型（如DeepSeek R1），选用4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B模型，通过llama.cpp在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上部署，以平衡性能与速度。
3. 策略优先：将管道稳定性置于多步推理能力之上，确保在9小时离线执行限制内完成任务。

Result: 系统在隐藏私有测试集上取得了0.8095的Private Score，在所有15个活跃参赛队伍中排名第10位。

Conclusion: 在计算资源和时间严格受限的文档理解任务中，通过优化检索组件和使用量化轻量级LLM，而非追求复杂的推理模型，可以确保系统的稳定性和可交付性，从而取得具有竞争力的成绩。

Abstract: This paper describes the system submitted to the UNLP 2026 Shared Task on Multi-Domain Document Understanding. The challenge required extracting precise answers, document IDs, and page numbers from a diverse corpus of Ukrainian PDF documents within a strict 9-hour offline Kaggle execution limit. During evaluation on the hidden private test set, optical character recognition (OCR) of scanned documents emerged as a severe bottleneck, consuming 5-7 hours of the total time budget due to sequential single-threaded execution. This overhead strictly limited the remaining time for Large Language Model (LLM) inference to approximately two hours for 500 questions. To guarantee pipeline completion without timeouts, we developed a resource-efficient Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing BM25, BGE-M3, and Cross-Encoder reranking. Rather than deploying parameter-heavy reasoning models (e.g., DeepSeek R1) which consistently timed out, we utilized a 4-bit quantized LapaLLM 12B model via llama.cpp on dual NVIDIA T4 GPUs. Prioritizing pipeline stability over multi-step reasoning, our system achieved a Private Score of 0.8095, placing 10th out of 15 active teams.

</details>


### [138] [Type-Driven Tokenization for Brahmic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22125)
*Sai Hemanth Kapila,Rakshika Bagavathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型中标准词元器在处理婆罗米系文字（如天城文、泰米尔文等）时产生格式错误文本的问题，本研究通过形式化验证方法识别其正字法约束差异，开发了`fixToken`函数及SentencePiece补丁和Rust预词元库，有效消除了跨印度语言的编码错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型中使用的标准词元器在应用于婆罗米系文字（abugidas）时产生畸形/错误文本的问题，这些文字系统的辅音带有固有元音且受依赖标记修改，现有词元器违反了不适用于英语等拼音文字的拼写约束。

Method: 1. 理论分析：指出英语正字法构成“半群”（任意两个有效词元可自由连接），而婆罗米正字法构成“偏半群”（并非所有连接都生成有效字符串）。2. 形式化验证：使用Agda语言对该区别进行形式化，将有效的婆罗米词元建模为转换系统中的链。3. 算法推导：推导出一个能证明正确的`fixToken`函数，用于扩展候选词元以符合正字法边界。4. 工程实现：将该形式化推导转化为SentencePiece的实际补丁以及独立的基于Rust的预词元库。

Result: 成功消除了 across Indic scripts（跨印度语言）中观察到的词元化错误，证明了形式化方法在改进多语言NLP预处理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 婆罗米系文字的正字法特性要求特殊的词元处理机制，通过形式化验证推导出的修复函数和相应的软件实现能够显著改善大语言模型在这些语言上的处理能力。

Abstract: Standard tokenizers used in large language models produce malformed text when applied to Brahmic scripts. They are a family of abugidas, writing systems whose consonants carry an inherent vowel that dependent marks can modify. They include Devanagari, Telugu, Tamil, Kannada, and others. The underlying issue is that these tokenizers violate orthographic constraints that do not arise in alphabetic scripts like English. We observe that while English orthography forms a \emph{semigroup} (any two valid tokens can be freely concatenated), Brahmic orthography forms a \emph{partial semigroup}: not every concatenation yields a valid string. We formalise this distinction in Agda, model valid Brahmic tokens as chains in a transition system, and derive a provably correct \texttt{fixToken} function that extends any candidate token to respect orthographic boundaries. We then show how this formal derivation translates into a practical patch for SentencePiece as well as a standalone Rust-based pre-tokenizer library, eliminating the observed errors across Indic scripts.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Accuracy and Surface Fluency: Risk-Sensitive Evaluation of LLMs for Legal Clause Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22127)
*Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对LLM生成合同条款的评估框架，结合CLAUSE和LENS-CRAFT两大体系，采用“最大严重性原则”而非平均分，旨在通过22类条款和34种法律失败模式进行风险敏感型评估，推动法律AI评价从聚合准确率转向具体失败模式驱动的风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于准确性或偏好的评估方法不适用于法律起草，因为条款可能语言流畅但存在遗漏关键例外、风险分配不可执行、管辖权适用错误或暴露监管责任等严重法律缺陷。

Method: 设计并实施实证研究框架，评估Claude Haiku 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、GPT 5.4 Nano和Qwen 3.5 Flash四个模型；使用CLAUSE分类提示词的法律功能和失败目标，使用LENS-CRAFT在九个法律质量维度上评分；应用“最大严重性原则”确保单一决定性法律缺陷不被平均分数掩盖。

Result: 提供了完整的评估协议、分类法、分析计划和结果报告结构；通过对22类合同条款和34种法律动机失败模式的测试，揭示了现有评估方法的局限性及新框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 法律AI评估应超越聚合准确率，转向以条款特定、失败模式驱动和风险敏感为核心的评估范式，以更真实反映LLM在法律起草中的实际表现与潜在风险。
