Method: 将推荐系统可观测性定义为反事实测量问题，即估计在没有特定内容项或模型决策的情况下推荐系统和用户参与度的表现；提出了三个以利益相关者为中心的可观测性原则，并开发了涵盖偏差减少、相对性和增量性的测量方法论，适用于单阶段和级联推荐系统。

Result: 在Netflix多个生产部署中展示了该框架的有效性，成功增强了跨多个推荐系统的可观测性能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过统一的测量基础，有效解决了推荐系统评估中的归因难题，提升了内容创作者和模型开发者的决策透明度与效率。

Abstract: Understanding the performance of large-scale recommender systems remains an underexplored challenge, especially for content creators and model developers. The raw engagement signals available to them, such as views and clicks, conflate content quality, model behavior, presentation bias, and audience reach, making it hard to attribute outcomes to the right cause.
  In this work, we present a general evaluation framework that enhances observability across multiple recommender systems at Netflix and demonstrate its effectiveness through several production deployments. The framework treats recommender-system observability as a counterfactual measurement problem: estimating what the recommender would have done, and what engagement would have followed, in the absence of a specific content item or model decision. We articulate three stakeholder-centered observability principles for content creators and model developers, and propose measurement methodologies covering bias reduction, relativity, and incrementality, applicable to both single-stage and cascading recommender systems and serving both audiences from a single measurement foundation.

</details>


### [245] [Parameterized Dense-Sparse Fusion for Hybrid Retrieval: Tuning a Rank-Score Mix on BEIR SciFact with Qdrant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22770)
*Satyanarayan Pati,Srikanth Patil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种参数化混合排序器，通过融合密集嵌入和稀疏词汇列表进行检索。在SciFact数据集上通过网格搜索确定超参数（α=0.8, λ=0.75, κ=20），测试集表现优于基线模型（BGE和等权RRF）。然而，这些系数具有数据集特异性，在其他BEIR基准测试中，相同模型的等权RRF表现不如密集模型，但经过独立调优后在多数单元上优于两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索并优化一种能够结合密集语义匹配和稀疏关键词匹配的混合排序方法，以超越单一模态（仅密集或仅稀疏/RRF）的检索性能。

Method: 构建一个包含显式参数向量的混合排序器，包括密集先验α、分数-排名混合λ、RRF平滑参数κ、可选的列表几何系数及路由器边际τ。在SciFact训练集上进行网格搜索以确定最佳参数组合，并在测试集上冻结这些值进行评估；同时在其他数据集单元上重复此调优过程以验证泛化性。

Result: 在SciFact测试集上，调优后的混合排序器达到0.753 nDCG@10和0.889 recall@10，优于密集模型BGE（0.742 / 0.871）和等权RRF（0.707 nDCG@10）。但在跨数据集的BEIR宏观平均评估中，未调优的等权RRF（0.479）低于密集模型（0.519）。当在20个索引单元上独立重复训练-冻结调优流程时，该方法在20/20单元上击败等权RRF，在16/20单元上击败密集模型。

Conclusion: 混合排序器在特定数据集（如SciFact）上通过精细调参可显著提升性能，但其最优参数具有数据集特异性。虽然通用配置下密集模型可能优于简单混合，但在各单元独立调优后，混合方法展现出更强的鲁棒性和优越性。建议其他语料库复用参数范围而非直接复制特定数据集的点估计值。

Abstract: We study a parameterized hybrid ranker that fuses a dense embedding list and a sparse lexical list. The method has a small, explicit parameter vector: a dense prior $α\in [0,1]$, a score-versus-rank mix $λ\in [0,1]$, an RRF smoothing parameter $κ> 0$, optional list-geometry coefficients that move $α$ per query, and a router margin $τ$ that can turn sparse search off. We grid-search those ranges on SciFact train (809 queries) and freeze the chosen values on SciFact test (300). The tuned rank-score mix ($α= 0.8$, $λ= 0.75$, $κ= 20$) reaches 0.753 nDCG@10 and 0.889 recall@10, outperforming dense BGE (0.742 / 0.871) and equal-weight RRF (0.707 nDCG@10) on that test split. A list-conditioned $α$ adds +0.0006 nDCG; a sparse-off router is rejected by the same train split (any $τ$ that skipped approximately 50% of queries lost nDCG). These coefficients are dataset-specific. Equal RRF with the same models does not beat dense on a nine-zip BEIR macro-average (0.479 vs. 0.519 nDCG@10). Repeating the same train-then-freeze sweep independently on all 20 indexed units beats equal RRF on 20/20 and dense on 16/20 (unit-mean nDCG@10 0.467 vs. 0.462 dense vs. 0.420 RRF). Other corpora should reuse the ranges, not a copy of the SciFact point.

</details>


### [246] [Per-Query Gating of LLM Rerankers for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22880)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于预LLM特征的查询门控机制，旨在跳过昂贵的LLM重排序步骤以节省成本。虽然实现了约51%的调用跳过率，但存在严重的选择乐观偏差和校准不良问题，导致实际性能损失超出预期，且早期声称的“无损”节省结果因方法缺陷被纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）重排序器在提升检索覆盖率的同时，带来了显著的计算成本（每千次查询增加0.2-0.3美元）和延迟（尾延迟增加约一秒）。为了优化这一流程，研究旨在探索是否可以通过学习一个针对每个查询的门控机制，在确定重排序无益时自动跳过该步骤，从而降低成本并减少延迟。

Method: 使用在LLM调用前可用的特征（包括两个检索列表的27个分数和词汇统计量，以及小型查询嵌入的主成分分析PCA），训练一个可执行的门控模型。该方法包含一个执行回退机制（fallback），所有选择（包括回退策略和阈值）均在训练折叠内完成，并在保留查询上应用。研究评估了有害跳过（即重排序本应找到目标但回退未找到）与聚合覆盖率的关系，并引入了基于Platt校准后危害概率的预期有害跳过率预算规则来设定阈值。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue和HotpotQA九个单元格中，门控机制在平均保持外LastHop@K成本为1.2个百分点的情况下跳过了51%的调用；四个单元格满足预先注册的1个百分点规则。然而，有害跳过次数（190次）多于有益跳过（136次），随机门控在高增益单元格上损失更大。在1个百分点的危害预算下，门控跳过42%的调用，但实现的危害超过承诺值（均值1.45 vs 0.83 pp），揭示了选择乐观偏差。尽管危害概率经过校准，但区分度极低（AUC 0.16至0.70）。研究还纠正了早期版本中基于oracle回退和错误目标报告的73%“无损”节省数据。

Conclusion: 虽然通过预LLM特征进行门控以减少LLM重排序成本是可行的，并能实现显著的调用跳过率，但该策略面临严峻挑战：校准后的危害概率区分能力差，且存在明显的选择乐观偏差，导致实际性能损失往往高于预期。早期的高效节省声明因方法论缺陷而不可靠，表明在当前技术条件下，简单跳过重排序难以在保证召回率的前提下实现真正的成本效益平衡。

Abstract: LLM rerankers add of the order of \$0.2-0.3 per 1,000 queries and about a second of tail latency on top of a graph-augmented dense pipeline such as HippoRAG2, and on three multi-hop benchmarks they improve final-hop top-K coverage on seven of nine (dataset, K) cells, by up to +34.8 pp. We ask whether a learned per-query gate can skip the reranker where it will not help, using only features available before the LLM call (27 score and lexical statistics of the two retrieval lists plus a PCA of a small query embedding) with an executable fallback. Every choice, including the fallback and the threshold, is made inside the training fold and applied once to held-out queries, and harmful skips (the rerank would have found the target, the fallback did not) are reported next to the aggregate coverage. Across nine cells on 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue and HotpotQA the gate skips 51% of calls at an average held-out LastHop@K cost of 1.2 pp; four cells meet a pre-registered 1 pp rule, harmful skips occur in eight (190 harmful against 136 beneficial), and a random gate at the same skip rate loses 2 to 11 pp on the high-lift cells. A second rule sets each cell's threshold from a pre-specified budget on the expected harmful-skip rate over Platt-calibrated harm probabilities (ECE 0.025 after calibration, 0.094 before): at a 1 pp budget the gate skips 42% at -0.8 pp with 66 harmful skips and six cells within 1 pp, but realised harm exceeds the promise in six cells (mean 1.45 vs 0.83 pp), a selection optimism we quantify; a 0.5 pp budget realises about 1 pp. The harm probabilities are calibrated but barely discriminative (AUC 0.16 to 0.70). An earlier version reported 73% "lossless" savings; that figure rested on an oracle fallback and a wrong MuSiQue target, and we document both.

</details>


### [247] [Inherit4Rec: Parameter Inheritance for Efficient Scaling of Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23111)
*Ruihao Zhang,Bo Chen,Xiao Wang,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Qinglin Jia,Xiuqiang He,Xiangyu Zhao,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Inherit4Rec框架，解决工业推荐系统中模型扩容（D2D）和稀疏化（D2S）在动态数据下的性能下降问题，实现高效参数继承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模模型训练成本高且计算资源受限；现有的参数继承方法主要针对静态数据集，在动态演化的推荐数据场景下会出现显著的性能衰退。

Method: 提出Inherit4Rec框架：1. D2D路径：结合混合增长与不对称训练，保持扩展前的前向功能并维持更新连续性；2. D2S路径：通过感知共激活的分区和负载平衡损失构建SMoE网络，保留稠密模型能力并促进专家负载均衡。

Result: 在KuaiRand-1K和工业短视频推荐数据集上的实验表明，两种转换方法在所有预测目标上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Inherit4Rec有效解决了工业推荐系统中的持续容量扩展和计算高效的稀疏转换问题。

Abstract: Scaling model capacity has emerged as an effective approach to overcoming performance bottlenecks in industrial recommender systems. However, repeatedly training larger dense models from scratch demands substantial data and time, while their growing computation conflicts with the strict serving budgets of industrial systems. Parameter inheritance provides a promising route for both dense model growth and sparse conversion, yet existing methods are primarily designed for static corpora and can suffer sharp performance drops under dynamically evolving recommendation data. To address these challenges, we propose Inherit4Rec, a parameter-inheritance framework that supports both Dense-to-Dense (D2D) growth and Dense-to-Sparse (D2S) conversion. Inherit4Rec-D2D combines hybrid growth with asymmetric training to preserve the forward function at expansion and maintain update continuity. Inherit4Rec-D2S constructs SMoE networks through co-activation-aware partitioning and a load-balancing loss, preserving dense-model capabilities while promoting balanced expert activation. Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and an industrial short-video recommendation dataset show that both transformations consistently outperform the evaluated inheritance baselines across all prediction objectives. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Inherit4Rec for continual capacity expansion and computation-efficient sparse conversion in industrial recommender systems.

</details>


### [248] [Improving disruptive research in the EU: why strengthening European Research Council grants alone is not enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23115)
*Alonso Rodríguez-Navarro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 欧盟破坏性创新竞争力不足，石墨烯研究等案例显示其无法与新加坡、美国和中国竞争。报告呼吁改革研究政策以扭转局面，但面临决策者自满文化的阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决欧盟在破坏性创新方面的结构性与经济缺陷，保护公民享有的社会利益，并提升欧盟作为全球技术领导者的能力。

Method: 通过具体案例（如石墨烯研究）进行对比分析，比较欧盟成员国及欧洲研究理事会资助项目与新加坡、美国和中国的研究竞争力，并引用相关文献佐证其他技术领域存在类似情况。

Result: 欧盟在破坏性研究方面水平过低，德国、法国、意大利和西班牙等国在新加坡面前缺乏竞争力；欧洲研究理事会的石墨烯研究亦无法与新加坡抗衡；整体而言，欧盟远落后于美国和中国的技术水平。

Conclusion: 欧盟必须采取激进的研究政策变革才能成为与美国和中国匹敌的技术领导者，但这种变革受到长期宣称“虚假卓越”的决策者和科学家虚荣文化的严重阻碍。

Abstract: Disruptive innovation in the EU is not sufficiently competitive; this weakness puts at risk the social benefits that its citizens take for granted. This report argues that, in addition to addressing structural and economic deficiencies, the EU must improve disruptive research to strengthen its disruptive innovation capacity. Currently, the level of disruptive research is too low. Using graphene research as an example, for which the EU has a specific programme, this report shows that Germany, France, Italy, and Spain cannot compete with Singapore. Even more concerning, the research funded by the European Research Council on graphene fails to compete with research conducted in Singapore. Similarly, the EU is far from competing with the USA or China. A few examples in this report and cited references evidence that the situation is similar in other technologies. To overcome this situation, the EU must adopt drastic changes in research policy. However, such changes face a vanity culture among policymakers and, perhaps, scientists who have been proclaiming an inexistent research excellence for decades. Without drastic changes, the prospect of the EU becoming a technological leader at the level of the USA and China cannot be considered realistic.

</details>


### [249] [From Prompt to Recommendation: A Fitted Stage Model of Brand Visibility in AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23162)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究分析了近3.5万条提示词观察数据，发现模型提及目标品牌的概率高度依赖于“自身领域暴露”和“品牌衍生曝光”。结合历史记忆与实时检索信号的诊断模型预测准确率极高（AUC > 0.94），证实了上下文可见性对品牌提及率的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化分析大型语言模型（GPT和Gemini）在提示词工程中提及特定目标品牌的频率及其驱动因素，特别是探索“自身领域暴露”和“品牌衍生曝光”对品牌提及率的影响机制。

Method: 收集并分析来自75个匿名项目的34,960条未标记提示词工程观测数据；构建包含历史运行记录和实时检索指标的时序诊断模型（Logit回归）；通过保留样本集和独立验证集评估模型性能（AUC指标）。

Result: 1. 品牌提及率随上下文暴露增加而显著上升：无暴露时提及率极低（GPT 2.8%, Gemini 3.8%）；仅自身领域暴露时升至约50%-60%；两者兼备时高达91.4%-100%。
2. 同一项目内重复运行显示，自身领域暴露可使提及率平均提升40-49个百分点，且历史非提及/提及状态具有持续性影响。
3. 综合模型预测性能优异：GPT AUC为0.963，Gemini AUC为0.942，显著优于仅使用历史或仅使用实时信号的基础模型。
4. 相关性分析表明，对于Gemini，页面匹配度对曝光的预测能力优于GPT，暗示相关性在引擎中介效应之前起作用。

Conclusion: 模型对目标品牌的提及行为并非随机，而是强烈依赖于可观察的上下文线索（自身领域和品牌衍生曝光）及历史交互记录。所提出的诊断模型能有效预测品牌提及概率，但该结果为观测性描述，不反映专有引擎内部的因果机制。

Abstract: We analyze 34,960 unbranded prompt-engine observations from 75 anonymized Aiso projects, covering 2,854 distinct monitored prompts and repeated GPT and Gemini runs from June-September 2026. When neither the target brand nor its own domain appears in the observable live retrieval path, target mention rates are 2.8% for GPT and 3.8% for Gemini. With an own-domain citation but no branded fan-out, they rise to 49.0% and 58.4%. When both own-domain exposure and a branded fan-out occur, mention rates reach 91.4% and 100%.
  The relationship persists within the same project, prompt, and engine across repeated runs: among prompt cells that vary in own-domain exposure while holding branded fan-out absent, exposure is associated with a mean mention-rate increase of 40.2 percentage points on GPT and 49.0 points on Gemini. Prior visibility is independently persistent. A previous non-mention plus no current own-domain exposure yields next-run mention rates of 1.6% and 1.9%; previous mention plus current exposure yields 80.5% and 83.7%.
  We fit a chronological diagnostic model using prior-run history and contemporaneous retrieval indicators: $ \operatorname{logit}P(M_t=1)=α_e+β_e\operatorname{logit}(\widetilde P_{t-1})+γ_e E_t+δ_e F_t+θ_e^\top X. $ On the latest 30% holdout, the full model achieves AUC 0.963 on GPT and 0.942 on Gemini, compared with 0.937/0.917 for prior history alone and 0.880/0.840 for live signals alone. A manually curated prompt sensitivity gives nearly identical AUCs (0.960 and 0.943). A separate 199-prompt page-corpus validation finds that prompt-page match predicts Gemini exposure (AUC 0.641) more clearly than GPT exposure (0.545), placing relevance upstream of a larger engine-mediated exposure effect. The equation is predictive and observational, not a causal description of proprietary engine internals.

</details>


### [250] [Semantic Candidate-Job Matching: A Comparative Evaluation of Dense Embedding Models in Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23307)
*Sai Yashwant,Siddhartha Jain,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对比评估了用于高流量招聘工作流中语义候选-职位匹配的密集嵌入模型。通过LLM解析将职位描述转化为结构化文本，并构建候选人的语义简历索引。研究比较了EmbeddingGemma（基础版）与经过缓存多负样本排名损失（Cached MNRL）微调的版本，在融合向量相似度和全文相关性的混合检索管道中，并以MPNet为基准。最终确定Cached-MNRL为优选配置，并定义了包含Recall@K、nDCG等指标的评估框架，以解决通用嵌入基准与企业实际匹配需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决通用目的嵌入模型基准测试与企业级“职位-候选人”匹配实际约束之间的差距，提供在真实检索条件下比较不同嵌入策略的结构化依据。

Method: 1. 使用基于LLM的解析技术，将职位描述转换为结构化英文搜索文本和特定语言关键词；2. 将候选人档案索引为语义丰富的简历表示；3. 在统一的混合检索管道中融合向量相似度和全文相关性（通过RRF算法）；4. 对EmbeddingGemma基础模型和经Cached MNRL微调的模型进行批量对比评估，并与MPNet基准进行比较；5. 数学化记录多种对比微调目标（如AnglE/CoSENT），并实证论证保留仅Cached-MNRL适应方案的合理性；6. 使用Recall@K、mrr、nDCG及部署的AI-Match评分和独立LLM-as-a-Judge相关性评分进行评估。

Result: 1. EmbeddingGemma经过Cached MNRL微调后表现优于基础版本；2. 确定了Cached-MNRL-only适应作为首选配置；3. 提供了在混合检索协议下的标准化评估指标体系；4. 明确了评估范围，避免了声称测量了整个框架的误导。

Conclusion: 该研究通过详细的对比评估和数学分析，证明了针对企业招聘场景微调嵌入模型的有效性，并为未来类似系统的模型选择和评估提供了可复现的方法论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of dense embedding models for semantic candidate-job matching in high-volume staffing workflows. Incoming job descriptions are converted into structured English search text and language-specific keywords through LLM-based parsing, and candidate profiles are indexed as semantically enriched resume representations. We evaluate EmbeddingGemma (base) against EmbeddingGemma fine-tuned with Cached Multiple Negatives Ranking Loss (MNRL) within a unified hybrid retrieval pipeline that fuses vector similarity and full-text relevance via reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), and benchmark both against the MPNet model on a batch comparative evaluation dataset scored through the deployed job-candidate matching scoring pipeline. We further document, with mathematical detail, the broader set of contrastive fine-tuning objectives considered during model development (including AnglE/CoSENT-style refinement) and the empirical rationale for retaining Cached-MNRL-only adaptation as the preferred configuration. To support reproducible model selection, we define a broader evaluation framework comprising standard information retrieval metrics (Recall@K, mean reciprocal rank, nDCG) under the exact hybrid-retrieval protocol; the metrics used for the evaluation reported in this paper are fine-tuning convergence diagnostics and a batch comparative evaluation using the deployed AI-Match score and an independent LLM-as-a-Judge relevance score, and we state this scope explicitly rather than implying the full framework was measured. The paper addresses the gap between general-purpose embedding benchmarks and enterprise job-candidate matching constraints, providing a structured basis for comparing embedding strategies under realistic job-candidate retrieval conditions.

</details>


### [251] [From Ranked Documents to Reliable Contexts: An Answer-Oriented Context Construct Framework for AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23354)
*Yunfei Zhong,Yinqiong Cai,Lixin Su,Haosheng Qian,Lixin Zou,Yixing Fan,Sheng Xu,Jiafeng Guo,Daiting Shi,Jingzhou He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AI搜索的“答案导向上下文构建”三阶段框架（答案支持、内容可信度、上下文组织），并建立工业级工作流与评估协议，显著提升检索与回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Web搜索采用面向人的范式，用户需自行检查排序文档并综合信息；而AI搜索中检索文档作为生成模型的输入，目标从按“搜索满意度”排序文档转变为构建可靠上下文以生成正确答案。

Method: 构建三阶段框架：(1) 答案支持（识别对答案生成有贡献的候选文档）；(2) 内容可信度（从来源、时间、事实角度评估信息的可靠性）；(3) 上下文组织（在有限上下文预算内选择、整合和结构化保留的信息）。同时开发涵盖前置和后置优化的工业工作流，并建立系统性评估协议。

Result: 实验表明，该框架及其工业实现在检索层面（上下文质量）和回答层面（最终答案准确性）均取得一致性的提升。

Conclusion: 答案导向的上下文构建框架有效解决了AI搜索中检索目标转变的问题，通过系统化评估和工业实施证明了其在提升检索和生成效果方面的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional Web search follows a human-facing paradigm in which users inspect ranked documents and synthesize information themselves. In AI Search, retrieved documents instead serve as inputs to a generation model, shifting the retrieval objective from ranking documents by Search Satisfaction to constructing reliable context for correct answer generation. We formulate this shift as answer-oriented context construction through a three-stage framework: (1) Answer Support identifies candidate documents that contribute information to answer generation; (2) Content Trustworthiness assesses whether this information provides a reliable basis for correct answers from source, temporal, and factual perspectives; and (3) Context Organization selects, consolidates, and structures retained information under a finite context budget for consistent and robust generation. We further develop an industrial workflow spanning prior and posterior optimization and establish a systematic evaluation protocol covering both retrieval-side context and final answers. Experiments show consistent improvements at both Retrieval and Answer levels, demonstrating the effectiveness of the framework and its industrial implementation.

</details>


### [252] [PSD: Pseudo Self-Distillation of Memory Representation Capabilities for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23449)
*Pirzada Suhail,Menglin Xia,Xuchao Zhang,Mayukh Das,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出伪自蒸馏（PSD）框架，使小语言模型（SLM）通过提示词渠道从黑盒大模型获取知识，构建层级记忆，以低成本实现媲美GPT-4.1-mini的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大型专有语言模型的代理记忆系统构建和维护成本高昂，限制了其在大规模场景下的部署。

Method: PSD框架采用单模型双角色机制：教师模型接收包含Oracle答案的特权提示，学生模型仅接收任务提示。通过多阶段训练管道，学生模仿教师的输出分布，从而在不访问内部状态的情况下吸收Oracle引导的行为。

Result: 在LoCoMo数据集上，经PSD训练的Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B模型在下文检索任务中匹配或超越GPT-4.1-mini，且部署成本大幅降低；离策略PSD效果最佳。该能力可零样本迁移至LongMemEval数据集，表现出良好的分布外泛化性。

Conclusion: PSD有效解决了闭源模型蒸馏难题，证明了小模型可通过特定提示工程从黑盒Oracle中学习并构建高效记忆，为低成本部署智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Memory systems are becoming a core component of LLM agents, but constructing and maintaining memory remains expensive because it relies on repeated calls to large proprietary language models. This cost creates a major barrier to deploying memory-enhanced agents at scale. In this paper, we present Pseudo Self-Distillation (PSD), a framework that enables small language models (SLMs) to construct hierarchical memory representations by distilling behavior from a strong black-box oracle through a multi-stage training pipeline. Standard distillation methods require access to teacher logits or hidden states, which closed models do not expose. Unlike conventional self-distillation settings, where supervision is derived from a model's own predictions, sampled rollouts, or aggregated outputs, PSD enables a single-model distillation setup while channeling external oracle knowledge through the prompt. PSD uses a single small model in two roles: a teacher that sees a privileged prompt containing the oracle's answer as reference context, and a student that sees only the task prompt. The student learns to reproduce the teacher's output distribution, absorbing oracle-guided behavior into its own weights without accessing the oracle's internals. On LoCoMo, PSD-trained Qwen3-0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B match or exceed GPT-4.1-mini on downstream retrieval at a fraction of the deployment cost, with off-policy PSD achieving the strongest results across most conditions. We further show that this memory-construction capability transfers out-of-distribution to LongMemEval, despite the students being trained exclusively on LoCoMo with no exposure to LongMemEval data.

</details>


### [253] [Beyond Relevance: Structured Semantic Supervision for Product Search with LLM-Augmented Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23646)
*Girish A. Koushik,Swapnil Bhosale,Samarth Agrawal,Hadeel Sadany,Constantin Orasan,Xiatian Zhu,Diptesh Kanojia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）生成结构化属性及解释，增强电商搜索中的查询-商品对，以区分相关商品与满足用户意图的商品。实验表明，合成信号在困难查询上带来显著增益，且LLM主要价值在于近似结构化语义监督而非完全替代人工判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索需区分仅与查询相关的产品和直接满足用户购物意图的产品。现有方法可能缺乏细粒度的语义监督，难以精准捕捉用户意图，因此需要引入更丰富的结构化信号来提升检索和重排序效果。

Method: 1. 使用LLM生成查询和商品的属性、相关性解释及中心性判断，并结合人工验证数据构建增强数据集。
2. 采用双编码器检索器（Dual-encoder retriever）和多层感知机重排序器（MLP re-ranker）评估这些信号。
3. 在ESCI数据集的增强子集上进行实验，对比人类特征预言机（Oracle）、纯训练配置及合成近似信号的性能。
4. 通过消融实验分析不同信号来源（如编辑后的解释、注释员评论、标量中心性特征）对性能提升的贡献。

Result: 1. 人类特征预言机达到0.9382 nDCG@10。
2. 无人类干预的训练配置（Q+P）达到0.9258 nDCG@10。
3. 合成近似信号整体达到0.9150 nDCG@10，且在困难和低表现查询上提供显著增益。
4. 消融实验显示，大部分性能提升来自编辑后的解释和注释员评论，而非标量中心性特征。

Conclusion: LLM在电商搜索中最有用的作用是暴露和近似结构化语义监督，而非直接替代人类判断。合成信号能有效弥补人工标注的不足，特别是在处理困难查询时，为提升搜索相关性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: E-commerce search requires distinguishing products that are merely related to a query from those that directly satisfy the user's shopping intent. We augment query-product pairs with structured LLM-generated query and product attributes and human-validated relevance, explanations, and centrality judgments, and evaluate these signals using a simple dual-encoder retriever and MLP re-ranker. On an augmented subset of ESCI, a human-feature oracle reaches $0.9382$ nDCG@10, while a human-free trained $Q+P$ configuration reaches $0.9258$. Synthetic approximations of the human signals reach $0.9150$ overall but provide substantial gains for difficult, low-performing queries. Ablations show that most of the oracle improvement comes from post-edited explanations and annotator comments rather than the scalar centrality feature, suggesting that LLMs are most useful for exposing and approximating structured semantic supervision rather than replacing human judgment directly.

</details>


### [254] [MuSeR: Scalable Long-sequence Recommendation with Multi-interest Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23677)
*Yongkang Fu,Beining Bao,Yu Jiang,Xiangyu Zhao,Hongyang Wei,Guangxing Chen,Zuodong Yang,Shantao Li,Zonggang Wu,Yuqi Lu,Shouke Qin,Hanmeng Liu,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MuSeR是一个工业级检索框架，通过层级时间压缩、解耦多兴趣提取和多模态语义对齐技术，解决了超长用户行为序列利用不足及多意图表示困难的问题。该系统在百度APP上线后，显著提升了DAU和会话时长，同时降低了延迟与成本，实现了长周期、多兴趣、多模态建模在生产环境中的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常因延迟和内存限制截断用户历史行为，导致长期兴趣未被充分利用；同时，用户在新闻、问答、短视频等不同模态下表现出多种异构意图，仅靠稀疏ID嵌入难以有效表征这些复杂偏好。

Method: 提出Multi-interest Sequence Representation (MuSeR)框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 层级时间压缩：保留近期动作全分辨率，对旧段进行渐进式池化，使$10^4$-$10^5$级交互适配固定服务预算；(2) 解耦多查询兴趣提取：引入正交正则化以区分不同兴趣；(3) 多模态语义对齐：利用大语言模型蒸馏文本摘要增强稀疏物品ID。工程上采用异步用户表示刷新、自适应缓存及跨异构硬件的分层波束搜索检索。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个大规模工业数据集上，MuSeR在Recall@$K$指标上持续优于强大的长序列和多兴趣基线。在百度APP首页推荐、发现流和短视频场景的在线A/B测试中，日活跃用户数(DAU)提升+0.26%，总会话时长提升+0.89%（p<0.05），同时降低了服务延迟和成本。

Conclusion: 本研究的主要贡献并非提出新的建模原语，而是实现了一种系统级集成，使得长期、多兴趣和 multimodal 建模能够共同部署在实时生产管道中，并提供了维持该系统的必要工程实践。

Abstract: Ultra-long user behavior sequences carry rich signals of stable and diverse preferences, yet industrial recommender systems typically truncate histories to a few hundred actions under strict latency and memory budgets, leaving long-term interests under-utilized. Users also pursue multiple heterogeneous intents across modalities such as news, Q&A, and short video, which sparse ID embeddings alone struggle to represent. We present Multi-interest Sequence Representation (MuSeR), a retrieval framework built on the deployed MGS system, which integrates three components: (i) hierarchical temporal compression, which retains recent actions at full resolution while progressively pooling older segments, so that per-user histories of $10^{4}$-$10^{5}$ interactions fit within a fixed serving budget; (ii) disentangled multi-query interest extraction with orthogonality regularization; and (iii) multimodal semantic alignment, which augments sparse item IDs with textual summaries distilled from a large language model. For industrial deployment, MuSeR further adopts asynchronous user-representation refresh with adaptive caching and hierarchical beam-search retrieval across heterogeneous hardware. On three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, MuSeR consistently improves Recall@$K$ over strong long-sequence and multi-interest baselines. In online A/B tests on Baidu APP's homepage feed, discovery feed, and short-video scenarios, MuSeR yields +0.26% daily active users and +0.89% total session duration (both statistically significant, p<0.05), alongside reduced serving latency and cost. Rather than proposing a new modeling primitive, our contribution is a system-level integration that makes long-term, multi-interest, and multimodal modeling jointly deployable in a real-time production pipeline, together with the engineering practices required to sustain it.

</details>


### [255] [UNIQUE: A Unified Retrieval and Ranking System for Large-Scale Feed Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23718)
*Zhuang Liu,Yongkang Fu,Zuodong Yang,Guangxing Chen,Zonggang Wu,Yuqi Lu,Shouke Qin,Shantao Li,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出UNIQUE框架，通过单层扁平量化和早期融合架构统一检索与排序，解决现有工业推荐系统中量化不稳定和信息丢失问题，在百度手机App中上线后显著提升用户观看时长和分发量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业移动Feed系统依赖检索-排序流水线，面临候选检索中层级量化不稳定性以及检索与排序阶段间信息丢失两大关键问题，导致长尾和冷启动推荐效果差且服务效率复杂。

Method: 提出UNIQUE统一检索与排序推荐框架，采用单层扁平量化；整合基于生成代码的检索和目标感知排序至单一早期融合架构，实现共享表示下的端到端训练；引入平衡量化机制以缓解码本不平衡并改善长尾表示。

Result: 离线实验显示码本资源分配比层级量化更平衡；在线A/B测试表明总观看时长提升0.96%，总分发量提升1.08%，新用户和高活跃用户改善显著；P99延迟为89ms，在线推理MFU为44.23%。

Conclusion: UNIQUE提供了一个稳定、高效且适用于生产环境的统一检索与排序框架，成功应用于百度首页Feed、发现页及短视频推荐场景，证明了其在工业级大规模实时流量下的有效性。

Abstract: Industrial mobile feed systems rely on a retrieval-ranking pipeline to serve large-scale, heterogeneous, and fast-changing content under strict latency constraints. However, existing pipelines still suffer from two critical issues: hierarchical quantization instability in candidate retrieval and information loss between separated retrieval and ranking stages. These issues hurt long-tail and cold-start recommendation and complicate efficient serving. To address them, we present UNIQUE, a unified retrieval and ranking recommendation framework with single-layer flat quantization. UNIQUE integrates generative code-based retrieval and target-aware ranking into one early-fusion architecture, enabling end-to-end training under a shared representation while preserving efficient candidate generation. A balanced quantization mechanism is further introduced to mitigate codebook imbalance and improve long-tail representation. Offline experiments evaluate UNIQUE from both retrieval and ranking perspectives, while codebook analysis shows more balanced resource allocation than hierarchical quantization. We deploy UNIQUE in the homepage feed, discovery-page, and short-video recommendation scenarios of Mobile Baidu, serving large-scale real-world traffic. Online A/B tests achieve a 0.96% gain in total watch duration and a 1.08% gain in total distribution volume, with notable improvements for new users and highly active users. Serving measurements show 89 ms P99 latency and 44.23% online inference MFU. These results show that UNIQUE provides a stable, efficient, and production-ready framework for unified retrieval and ranking in industrial recommendation.

</details>


### [256] [A Redundancy Reduction Approach for Controllable Sequential Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23849)
*Veronika Ivanova,Marina Munkhoeva,Ivan Razvorotnev,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BT-SR框架，通过特征去相关正则化解决序列推荐中的长尾分布和热门物品集中问题，平衡准确率与曝光度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决序列推荐中因长尾分布和热度驱动导致的“短列表准确率”与“长尾曝光”之间的权衡难题，抑制热门物品的全局评分优势。

Method: 构建基于Barlow Twins目标的去相关正则化训练框架（BT-SR），利用共享相同下一项目标的用户历史形成标签一致的正样本对，无需合成扰动；引入基于桶的对齐集中度指标量化几何效应。

Result: 在五个公共基准测试中，BT-SR持续提升了下一项排序质量；去相关强度可作为控制旋钮，重新分配头部和尾部物品的准确率，实现准确率与曝光度的灵活权衡。

Conclusion: 特征去相关能有效抑制用户表示空间中共有的低秩方向，从而调节热门与长尾物品的曝光差异，其效果受数据时间结构影响。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation must operate under long-tailed item distributions and popularity-driven concentration, often forcing practitioners to trade short-list accuracy against long-tail exposure. In this work, we study feature decorrelation as a mechanism for shaping representation geometry in dot-product sequential recommenders, and analyze how this, in turn, affects popularity-driven concentration. We propose a decorrelation-regularized training framework that augments next-item prediction with an auxiliary redundancy-reduction term, and instantiate it with BT-SR, which uses the Barlow Twins objective. To form label-consistent positive pairs without synthetic corruptions, we pair user histories that share the same next-item target. Beyond accuracy, we provide a geometric analysis showing how decorrelation suppresses shared low-rank directions in the user representation space that can give popular items a global scoring advantage, and we introduce a bucket-based alignment concentration metric to quantify this effect. Experiments on five public benchmarks show that BT-SR consistently improves next-item ranking quality, while the decorrelation strength acts as a simple control knob that reallocates accuracy across head and tail items, enabling accuracy-exposure trade-offs. Our analysis also reveals that the impact on head-vs-tail exposure differs across datasets, reflecting interactions between decorrelation and data temporal structure.

</details>


### [257] [Graded-Relevance Composed Multimodal Retrieval for E-commerce Visual Search at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24152)
*Anubhav Gupta,Hrushikesh Mohapatra,Prijith Chandra,Asish Mohapatra,Anuj Garg,Arvind Maan,Sudip Datta,Venkat Bulusu,Sitesh Kumar Jalan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GradCIR方法，通过VLM自动标注多级相关性标签、迭代难负样本挖掘和层次感知角度损失，训练基于多模态编码器的图像检索模型，在Walmart真实数据和FashionIQ基准上显著提升检索性能并投入生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合图像检索（CIR）方法将相关性视为二值关系且仅使用单个正样本进行三元组训练，无法适应真实电商目录中大量候选项部分满足用户查询的场景，导致排序效果不佳，影响客户体验。

Method: 提出GradCIR方法论：(1) 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）自动生成查询（目标检测+修饰符合成）及4级相关性标签，无需人工标注；(2) 构建迭代反馈循环，从训练中的检索器中挖掘难负样本以扩充训练集；(3) 采用层次感知角度目标函数，直接在多级相关性标签上训练检索器，而非将其坍缩为二分类。

Result: 在3.5M级Walmart数据上训练的PaliGemma2模型，通过控制消融实验证明监督粒度提升带来NDCG@10提高4.9%-5.9%；应用于其他多模态编码器可使早期融合骨干网络NDCG@10提升高达8.5%；在FashionIQ基准上达到0.6703平均召回率，优于最强有监督基线，匹配或超过所有已发表的CLIP-L类零-shot CIR方法。

Conclusion: GradCIR通过引入多级相关性监督和自动化数据构建流程，有效解决了传统CIR方法在部分匹配场景下的局限性，显著提升了检索精度，并在沃尔玛实际生产中成功部署服务实时视觉搜索流量。

Abstract: Visual search on large e-commerce catalogs must serve both "similarity" queries that ask for items resembling an uploaded image and "modifier" queries that comprise an image and text describing a desired modification (e.g. a color change or style swap). The latter is the setting known as composed image retrieval (CIR). Existing CIR methods, however, treat relevance as binary and train on triplets with a single positive target - a poor fit for real catalogs where many candidates partially satisfy a user query and ranking across that partial-match spectrum drives the customer experience. We propose a methodology for training CIR retrievers on graded relevance, consisting of: (i) a VLM to curate training data, generating both queries (object detection + modifier synthesis) and 4-level relevance labels without manual annotation, (ii) an iterative relevance-feedback loop that expands the training set by mining hard negatives from the in-training retriever, and (iii) a hierarchy-aware angular objective to train the retriever directly on the graded labels rather than collapsing them to a binary split. We call this methodology GradCIR and instantiate it on a PaliGemma2 bi-encoder trained on 3.5M graded pairs curated from raw Walmart catalog data. A controlled graded-vs-binary ablation isolates the supervision granularity and shows lift of 4.9%-5.9% in NDCG@10. The same recipe applied to other multimodal encoders lifts early-fusion backbones by up to 8.5% NDCG@10. On the public FashionIQ benchmark, GradCIR (applied to PaliGemma2) reaches 0.6703 average recall when fine-tuned, slightly ahead of the strongest peer-reviewed supervised baseline we compare against, and matching or exceeding all published CLIP-L-class zero-shot CIR methods. The system is deployed in production at Walmart, where it's serving live visual-search user traffic.

</details>


### [258] [Auditing Source Exposure in Baidu and Google AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24407)
*Yibo Li,Enci Guan,Yuedan Cai,Geng Liu,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究对百度和谷歌在中文搜索中的AI摘要行为进行了跨语言审计，发现不同平台-语言设置在摘要触发、来源可见性及分布上存在显著差异，表明答案语义相似性与来源曝光度是衡量AI搜索质量的两个独立维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI生成的概述层在搜索界面中日益重要，但其在中文搜索环境下的行为表现尚缺乏深入探索。

Method: 使用MS MARCO数据集中的英文查询及其对应的中文翻译作为样本，对百度和谷歌进行跨语言审计。分析涵盖：1）不同平台-语言设置下摘要的触发时机；2）中文摘要中可见的主机域名（host domains）及其集中度；3）不同设置下的来源重叠情况；4）匹配查询意图下生成答案的基于嵌入的语义相似度比较。

Result: 1）不同平台-语言设置在摘要可用性和可见来源曝光方面存在显著差异；2）聚合层面可见主机域名库存的重叠度低；3）匹配查询的答案中位数余弦相似度介于0.701至0.813之间。

Conclusion: 答案级的语义相似性与聚合层面的来源曝光捕捉了AI中介搜索的不同维度。因此，AI搜索评估不仅应考虑生成内容的质量，还需考察来源在不同平台、语言和信息系统中的可见性分布。

Abstract: AI-generated overviews are becoming an increasingly prominent layer of search interfaces, yet their behavior in Chinese-language search remains underexplored. We conduct a cross-lingual audit of AI overview behavior on Baidu and Google using English queries sampled from MS MARCO and their translated Chinese counterparts. Our analysis examines when overviews are triggered across platform-language settings, which host domains receive visible exposure in Chinese-language overviews, how concentrated that exposure is, and how source overlap varies across settings. We also compare the embedding-based semantic similarity of generated answers for matched query intents. The results reveal substantial differences across platform-language settings in overview availability and visible source exposure. At the aggregate level, the settings exhibit low overlap in visible host-domain inventories, while matched-query answers yield median cosine similarities ranging from 0.701 to 0.813. These findings indicate that answer-level semantic similarity and aggregate source exposure capture distinct dimensions of AI-mediated search. Evaluations of AI search should therefore consider not only the content of generated answers but also how source visibility is distributed across platforms, languages, and information environments.

</details>


### [259] [What Makes a Good Semantic ID for Generative Recommendation? A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24430)
*Yufei Chen,Junchen Fu,Jujia Zhao,Yukun Zhao,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模可复现研究，系统评估了生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）设计的影响。研究发现SID设计的效果是非单调的，没有单一方案在所有数据集上最优；代码本利用率虽具诊断价值但不足以决定性能；增加骨干网络或SID长度并非总是有益；不同SID设计在局部语义保持上各有互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式推荐中基于语义ID（SID）的方法取得了显著成果，但SID在构建策略、码本组织和长度等方面差异巨大，导致其真实性能影响尚不明确，亟需系统性研究以厘清关键因素。

Method: 在统一的实验框架下开展大规模可复现研究，从四个方面评估SID设计：1) 不同设计的相对有效性；2) 码本利用率与推荐质量的关系；3) 语义码长度的影响；4) 对局部项目语义保持的作用。结合统一评估与跨数据集控制分析进行验证。

Result: 1. SID设计效果呈非单调性，无通用最优方案，常用方法（如RQ-VAE和OPQ）在不同数据集表现不一致；2. 第一层码本最平衡的方法并非始终最佳，表明利用率是必要但不充分条件；3. 扩展生成骨干网或SID长度并不总是带来提升；4. 语义邻域分析显示，不同设计在局部语义保持上具有互补且稳定的优势，无单一主导设计。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了关于语义ID设计的可控且可复现的理解，揭示了其效果的复杂性与情境依赖性，为未来生成式推荐系统的SID设计提供了实践指导，强调需根据具体场景权衡不同设计而非依赖单一标准。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has emerged as an active research direction, where items are commonly represented by semantic IDs (SIDs): discrete codes generated token by token. Despite strong empirical results, SID designs vary widely in construction strategy, codebook organization, and code length, making their true impact on recommendation performance unclear.
  We conduct a large-scale reproducibility study to systematically investigate the impact of semantic ID design on generative recommendation under a unified experimental framework. We focus on a fundamental question: What makes a good semantic ID for generative recommendation? To answer this question, we examine four aspects: the relative effectiveness of different semantic ID designs, the connection between codebook utilization and recommendation quality, the effect of semantic code length, and the influence of semantic ID design on local item semantic preservation. Through a unified evaluation and additional cross-dataset controlled analyses, we find that the effects of SID design are largely non-monotonic: no single SID design is universally best, and commonly used RQ-VAE- and OPQ-based designs can behave inconsistently across datasets. The method with the most balanced first-level codebook is not consistently the best recommender, showing that utilization is diagnostic but insufficient. Scaling either the generative backbone or the SID length is also not always beneficial. Finally, semantic-neighborhood analysis reveals that no single SID design dominates all notions of local semantic preservation; instead, different designs exhibit complementary strengths that remain stable across datasets and neighborhood sizes. Our study provides a controlled and reproducible understanding of semantic ID design and offers practical insights for future generative recommender systems.

</details>


### [260] [UK-PRBENCH: A Paragraph-Level Precedent Retrieval Benchmark for United Kingdom Case Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24613)
*Damith Premasiri,Tharindu Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出UK-PRBench，首个英国判例法段落级先例检索基准，解决现有文档级检索粒度粗、不贴合法律实务的问题，并建立基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先例检索（PCR）基准和方法主要在文档层面操作，将整份判决书视为相关性单位。然而，判决书涉及多个法律问题，仅少量段落与特定查询相关，这种粗粒度方法对法律从业者次优。

Method: 从英国国家档案馆获取判决书，构建覆盖广泛英国法院和法庭的段落级先例检索基准UK-PRBench；评估最先进的检索模型并建立基线结果。

Result: 实验表明，当前检索方法在段落级先例检索上仍面临挑战，存在显著改进空间；UK-PRBench为细粒度先例检索提供了标准化评估基准。

Conclusion: 段落级检索比文档级更贴合法律实务需求，现有模型性能不足，UK-PRBench有助于推动英国法律领域检索系统的进步。

Abstract: Prior case retrieval (PCR) aims to identify precedent cases relevant to a given query case. Existing PCR benchmarks and methods predominantly operate at the document level, treating entire judgments as the unit of relevance. This formulation is suboptimal for legal practitioners, as judgments address multiple legal issues and only a small subset of paragraphs is relevant to a particular query. Addressing this gap, we introduce UK-PRBench, a benchmark for paragraph-level precedent retrieval in UK case law, constructed from judgments obtained from the UK National Archives and covering a broad range of UK courts and tribunals. Furthermore, we evaluate state-of-the-art retrieval models and establish baseline results. Our experiments show that paragraph-level precedent retrieval remains challenging for current retrieval approaches, highlighting substantial room for improvement. UK-PRBench provides a standardised benchmark for evaluating fine-grained precedent retrieval and advancing retrieval systems for the UK legal domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Didactic knowledge or Clinical Cases? How Data Types Shape Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22161)
*Yuzheng Fan,Haochun Wang,Sendong Zhao,Xiao Han,Ming Ma,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 医学大语言模型中，临床数据主要提升临床推理能力，而教材数据主要提升知识记忆；二者存在不对称迁移效应，且存在“知-行”鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确教学数据（如教科书）与临床数据（如病历记录混合训练对医学大模型不同能力维度的具体影响尚不明确。

Method: 通过控制词元数量的实验，改变教学数据与临床数据的比例，分析数据组成对知识密集型任务和临床导向任务的性能、能力画像及错误模式的影响。

Result: 发现不对称迁移现象：临床数据显著提升临床任务且在知识任务上保持竞争力，而教学数据仅主要提升知识任务；存在“知-行”鸿沟，即知识回忆的提升不必然转化为临床推理能力的提升少量临床数据即可带来电子健康记录相关任务的主要增益。

Conclusion: 医学大语言模型的数据构建应以应用为导向，针对推理密集型应用场景应优先增加临床数据比例。

Abstract: Medical large language models are commonly trained on mixtures of didactic data (e.g., textbooks) and clinical data (e.g., patient records), yet how these data types differentially shape model capabilities remains unclear. We address this issue with token-matched experiments that vary the didactic-to-clinical ratio and analyze how data composition affects performance, capability profiles, and error patterns across knowledge-intensive and clinic-oriented tasks. We uncover an asymmetric transfer across task types: clinical data improves clinic-oriented tasks while remaining competitive on knowledge-intensive ones, whereas didactic data mainly improves knowledge-intensive tasks. Error analysis suggests a knowing-doing gap, where improvements in knowledge recall do not reliably generalize to clinical reasoning. We further observe that modest amounts of clinical data yield most of the gains on EHR-grounded tasks, while the optimal mixture ratio varies with the knowledge and clinical reasoning demands of downstream tasks. These findings suggest that medical LLM data curation should be application-driven, with higher proportions of clinical data preferred for reasoning-intensive use cases.

</details>


### [262] [An Affordable AI-Integrated Smart Cane for Multimodal Mobility Assistance of Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22277)
*Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Toqeer Ali Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Raspberry Pi Zero 2W的廉价（88美元）离线AI智能拐杖，通过融合RGB视觉与ToF测距，利用量化SSD MobileNet V1模型实现障碍物检测，并结合触觉反馈和音频警报提供多模态辅助。系统在室内场景中F1分数达0.82，延迟330ms，功耗2.8W，验证了边缘侧隐私保护辅助导航的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统白盲杖无法识别高处障碍或提供语义环境信息；现有AI辅助导航系统依赖昂贵硬件或云端连接，限制了在资源受限环境中的可访问性。

Method: 设计了一款集成AI的智能拐杖，核心为超低功耗Raspberry Pi Zero 2W。系统融合RGB视觉与飞行时间（ToF）测距数据，采用INT8量化的SSD MobileNet V1模型进行物体检测。通过多进程架构隔离传感器采集、神经推断和触觉反馈以确保鲁棒性，并配备距离感知振动触觉反馈和实时音频警报。

Result: 在室内移动场景实验中，系统达到宏观平均F1分数0.82（精确率0.85，召回率0.81），端到端平均延迟330毫秒，峰值功耗2.8瓦。12名参与者的初步可用性研究显示SUS评分78.5，用户感知积极且障碍物意识增强。

Conclusion: 该原型验证了在成本敏感的移动辅助应用中，部署隐私保护、原生边缘计算辅助智能的可行性，为解决视障人士导航问题提供了低成本、离线的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual impairment affects over 2.2 billion people worldwide, yet conventional white canes cannot detect elevated hazards or provide semantic environmental context. Existing AI-assisted navigation systems typically rely on expensive hardware or cloud connectivity, limiting accessibility in resource-constrained settings. This paper presents an affordable (\$88 USD), fully offline AI-integrated smart cane designed for multimodal mobility assistance on an ultra-low-power Raspberry Pi Zero 2W. The system fuses RGB vision sensing with Time-of-Flight (ToF) distance estimation, pairing an INT8-quantized SSD MobileNet V1 model with distance-aware vibrotactile feedback and real-time audio alerts. To ensure operational robustness on constrained hardware, a multiprocessing architecture isolates sensor acquisition, neural inference, and haptic feedback into independent processes with fail-safe sensing support. Experimental evaluation across indoor mobility scenarios demonstrates a macro-averaged F1-score of 0.82 (precision: 0.85, recall: 0.81), a mean end-to-end latency of 330\,ms, and a peak power draw of 2.8\,W. A preliminary usability study with 12 participants (SUS: 78.5, NASA-TLX) demonstrated positive user perception and enhanced obstacle awareness. The proposed prototype validates the feasibility of deploying privacy-preserving, edge-native assistive intelligence for cost-sensitive mobility assistance.

</details>


### [263] [PAANI : On Device Visual Evidence Fusion and Explainable Guidance for River Robot Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22353)
*Savio Cardoz,Santhiya Rajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAANI架构，在资源受限的Arduino UNO Q上实现可解释的视觉感知与引导，结合YOLO11n和MobileNetV3，提供带有证据溯源的实时决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动河流监测机器人需处理地理航点无法描述的障碍物和水域边界；在资源受限平台上，将不完美的视觉预测转化为及时、可检查的引导存在挑战，传统标签或指令缺乏对决策依据及不确定性的解释。

Method: 构建PAANI边缘感知到引导架构：集成预训练YOLO11n检测器和自定义MobileNetV3 Small语义分割器，通过时间戳对齐的证据融合在Arduino UNO Q上运行；采用有界跟踪保证目标持久性，结合显式走廊策略（整合表面标签、检测置信度、紧急程度和掩码不确定性）生成最终建议；使用ROS 2接口连接Gazebo仿真测试床；基于WaterScenes和MaSTr1325数据集进行训练与验证。

Result: FP32 ONNX模型大小14.817 MB；检测器mAP@0.5 IoU达0.7388（测试）/0.7367（验证），分割器mIoU达0.9750；在0.5 Hz频率下，UNO Q记录显示中位延迟467.8 ms，95百分位延迟580.3 ms；识别出黑色输入误分类和采样率不匹配导致的诊断问题。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了可检查、可复用的边缘机器人基础框架，成功区分了模型精度与板载执行性能，但明确表明当前系统尚未达到经水面试验验证的防碰撞标准。

Abstract: Mobile river monitoring robots must interpret obstacles and water boundaries that geographic waypoints alone cannot describe. On resource constrained platforms, converting imperfect visual predictions into timely and inspectable guidance is a distinct challenge. An object label or steering command does not explain which evidence supports a decision or when that evidence is unreliable. We present PAANI, an on-device perception to guidance architecture that combines a project trained YOLO11n detector and a custom MobileNetV3 Small semantic segmenter with timestamp aligned evidence fusion on Arduino UNO Q. Bounded tracking supplies object persistence, while an explicit corridor policy combines surface labels, accepted detections, urgency and mask uncertainty. Each final advisory exposes its contributing evidence and policy reasons. ROS 2 interfaces connect the local AI pipeline to a separate Gazebo vessel, localization and control testbed. Training uses 10,000 WaterScenes images for four-class detection and 1,127 MaSTr1325 images for segmentation, including 198 segmentation validation images. The selected FP32 ONNX models occupy 14.817 MB. Detector checkpoint test mAP at 0.5 IoU is 0.7388, while the separately evaluated rectangular ONNX export achieves validation mAP at 0.5 IoU of 0.7367. Segmentation ONNX validation mIoU is 0.9750. A five-minute UNO Q recording produced median and 95th percentile pipeline latencies of 467.8 ms and 580.3 ms at a configured 0.5 Hz cadence. The evaluation also identifies black input misclassification and a sampling rate mismatch that prevents the diagnostic apparent motion estimator from collecting sufficient evidence. These results support an inspectable and reusable edge robotics foundation while clearly distinguishing model accuracy and on-board execution from validated on-water collision avoidance.

</details>


### [264] [Social Influence and the Allocation of Scientific Attention in AI Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22408)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟实验发现，社会信息（即观察他人的选择）会显著减少AI对学术文献的总体关注度，增加选择的集中度，并加剧社区间的差异；初始选择能显著提升后续被选概率，且AI的选择与外部引用相关性弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能系统正逐渐参与科研评估，但其对基于人类读者的信号（如引用、下载量）的集体反应及其后果尚不明确。

Method: 将“音乐实验室”设计应用于学术注意力市场：1) 1000个AI智能体从2025年《美国经济评论》114篇文章中选题，分为独立选择和社交影响两组；2) 另一组200个智能体实验中，随机给予部分文章5个初始选择以观察其对后续选择率的影响。

Result: 1) 社交影响组比独立选择组每智能体少选17.2%的文章，选择更集中，覆盖论文数更少（73 vs 90），社区间变异更大；2) 随机赋予5个初始选择使后续选择率提高45.55个百分点；3) AI选择与外部引用仅有适度相关性，与下载量几乎无相关。

Conclusion: 简单的信息规则（如社会影响力）能够塑造人工群体中科学注意力的总量、广度和分布模式。

Abstract: AI systems are becoming participants in the evaluation and use of scientific research. They encounter citation counts, download statistics and lists of popular articles developed around human readers, but the collective consequences of these signals for artificial readers remain uncertain. This paper adapts the Music Lab design to a market for academic attention. In the first experiment, 1,000 AI agents choose papers from the titles and abstracts of all 114 regular research articles published in the American Economic Review in 2025. The experiment has five independent-choice communities and five social-influence communities, each with 100 sequential agents. Only agents in the social-influence condition observe earlier selections within their community. Agents may select any number of papers. Social-information communities select 17.2 percent fewer papers per agent, concentrate their choices more heavily, and collectively cover 73 papers, compared with 90 independently. Between-community variation is greater under social information. In a second experiment with 200 agents across twenty social communities, randomly assigning papers five initial selections raises their subsequent selection rate by 45.55 percentage points (95% CI: 41.20 to 49.90). Choices have modest correspondence with external citations and little correspondence with download counts. The results show how a simple information rule shapes the volume, breadth and distribution of scientific attention in an artificial population.

</details>


### [265] [Learning 3D biophysical cell properties from 2D images and cell-population statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22410)
*Santiago Hernández-Orozco,Hector Zenil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种群体监督框架，通过2D红细胞图像推断3D生物物理属性（如MCV、RDW、MCH），无需个体细胞标签，相关系数达0.86-0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从2D显微镜图像推断3D细胞属性时，若参考仪器仅提供群体统计信息而非单个细胞的标签，传统方法难以实现。

Method: 开发了一个群体监督框架，结合共享局部推理、具有生物物理结构的解码器（用于体积和血红蛋白）、学习的实例权重和设备特定校准，将单张2D红细胞图像映射到潜在生物物理量并聚合为标准血液学指标。

Result: 在包含390个样本和1,105次采集的六个设备数据集上，与Sysmex分析仪相比，皮尔逊相关系数为0.86--0.98。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种从2D图像和群体监督推导至3D细胞生物物理学的可测试途径，无需显式进行3D重建即可识别受限的实例预测器。

Abstract: Inferring 3D cellular properties from 2D microscopy is difficult when a reference instrument reports only population statistics rather than labels for individual cells. Here we develop a population-supervised framework that maps single 2D red-cell images to latent biophysical quantities and aggregates them to mean corpuscular volume, red-cell distribution width and mean corpuscular haemoglobin. The model combines shared local inference, a biophysically structured decoder for volume and haemoglobin, learned instance weighting and device-specific calibration. We formalise conditions under which aggregate observations identify restricted instance predictors, show why population agreement does not by itself identify single-cell properties or 3D geometry, and derive the dispersion penalty induced by subset mean matching. The development dataset comprises 390 specimens and 1,105 acquisitions across six devices, with reported Pearson correlations of 0.86--0.98 against a Sysmex analyser. The framework provides a testable route from 2D images and population supervision to 3D cellular biophysics without claiming explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [266] [Goal-driven Variant Categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22475)
*Daniel Calegari,Daniel Amyot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于目标驱动的变体分类方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合组织目标模型，自动将流程变体映射到业务类别，替代传统手动聚类解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流程发现产生的变体缺乏一致性，分析师需手动聚类并赋予业务含义，随着变体数量和复杂性增加，这一判断密集型步骤变得极其困难且效率低下。

Method: 首先构建预定义分类轴的组织目标模型；将每个流程变体转化为描述其行为的文本叙事；利用大语言模型（LLM）在目标模型背景下进行语义推理，将变体分配到最合适的业务类别中。

Result: 该方法在三个规模和行为多样性差异显著的公开日志上进行了端到端实例化和评估。结果显示，受目标模型指导的分区与无引导归纳产生的分区不同，且能响应对声明替代方案的受控编辑，证明了其有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 通过引入组织目标模型作为引导，LLM能够连接底层流程行为与分析人员定义的业务目标，实现了更精准、符合业务意图的流程变体自动化分类，尽管需要预先编写目标模型，但显著降低了人工解释的负担。

Abstract: Process discovery rarely yields a single coherent process structure. For analysis, a common step is to cluster process variants based on structural similarity and then assign business meaning to the resulting groups. Since these partitions are not derived from the organization's goals, analysts must manually interpret and consolidate variants into business-meaningful categories. This judgment-intensive step becomes increasingly difficult as the number and complexity of variants grow. In this paper, we propose a goal-driven approach to variant categorization that reverses this workflow. We first author an organization's goal model that predefines the categorization axis. Each variant is transformed into a textual narrative describing its behavior, and a Large Language Model (LLM) interprets it in the context of the goal model and assigns the variant to the most appropriate category. LLM-based semantic reasoning connects low-level process behavior with analyst-defined business goals. We instantiate this approach end-to-end and evaluate it on three public logs differing substantially in scale and behavioral diversity. Goal-model guidance yields partitions that differ from those produced by unguided induction and respond to controlled edits to the declared alternatives, at the cost of authoring a goal model.

</details>


### [267] [Replication Without Persistence in Hosted LLMs: Measurement Sensitivity in Action-Time Belief Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22478)
*Bhushan Kashinath Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Regent Chess环境研究托管语言模型行为评估的可变性，区分了复制性、测量敏感性和持续性三个验证问题。研究发现，尽管在新鲜数据下复现了Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite的性能缺陷，但在同一标识符下重建配置时性能有所改善，且不同版本间表现存在反转。结论指出，评估结果受标识符、服务期、测量工具和推理配置影响，建议对托管模型的行为声明进行显式索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管语言模型的行为评估在不同运行中可能因评估服务、测量工具或两者不同而产生差异，导致评估结果不一致。需要明确区分复制性、测量敏感性和持续性这三个关键验证维度，以提高评估的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 使用Regent Chess这一顺序环境，其中隐藏的可变状态被精确记录，允许在行动时将陈述信念与事实真相进行比对。通过比较正端点值（表示比匹配均匀比较器更差的表现）来评估模型性能。具体包括：在历史配置下用新鲜数据复制先前发现；在同一公共标识符下重建配置以测试测量敏感性；以及在一共同测量工具下跨后续测试标识符检查持续性。

Result: 1. 复制性：Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite在新鲜游戏的历史配置下复现了性能缺陷（+0.0530, 95% CI [+0.0329,+0.0714]）。
2. 测量敏感性：在同一标识符下，重建配置后模型减去均匀比较器的端点降低了0.0429（H-minus-R对比的95% CI为[+0.0182,+0.0667]），但所有六个配置组件共同变化，无法隔离单一组件的影响。
3. 持续性：在重建的R下，前瞻性冻结的交错同窗口4K比较显示Gemini 3.1和Gemini 3.7之间的符号反转，表明不同发布和产品层级的标识符间存在方向性模式。额外的服务期贡献未确定（-0.0166, [-0.0483,+0.0157]）。

Conclusion: 复制性、测量敏感性和持续性在同一评估中可能导致不同的结论。因此，建议根据测试标识符、服务期、测量工具和推理配置对托管模型的行为声明进行显式索引，以提高评估结果的透明度和可比性。

Abstract: Behavioural evaluations of hosted language models can vary because the evaluated service, the measurement instrument, or both differ across runs. We separate three validation questions: whether a prior finding recurs on fresh data under its historical configuration (replication), whether the endpoint changes when the evaluation-and-inference configuration is rebuilt under the same identifier (measurement sensitivity), and whether the finding persists across subsequently tested identifiers under one common instrument (persistence). We study these questions in Regent Chess, a sequential environment in which a hidden, mutable state is recorded exactly, allowing stated beliefs to be scored against ground truth at action time; positive endpoint values mean worse performance than a matched-uniform comparator. The previously reported Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite deficit recurs on fresh games under its historical configuration (+0.0530, 95% CI [+0.0329,+0.0714]). In a back-to-back same-day H/R comparison under the same public identifier, the model-minus-uniform endpoint is 0.0429 lower under the rebuilt configuration (95% CI for the H-minus-R contrast [+0.0182,+0.0667]); all six configuration components vary jointly, so no component is isolated. Under rebuilt R, the prospectively frozen, interleaved same-window 4K comparison reverses sign between Gemini 3.1 and Gemini 3.7, identifiers that differ in release and product tier; additional descriptive and exploratory cells show the same directional pattern. Any additional serving-period contribution remains unresolved (-0.0166, [-0.0483,+0.0157]). Replication, measurement sensitivity, and persistence can therefore yield different conclusions within one evaluation, motivating explicit indexing of hosted-model behavioural claims by tested identifier, serving period, measurement instrument, and inference configuration.

</details>


### [268] [The Wisdom of Artificial Deliberative Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22497)
*Federico Barrera-Lemarchand,Mariano Sigman,Joaquin Navajas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，大型语言模型（LLM）通过群体协商（deliberation）能显著提升预测准确性，优于简单的独立聚合。这一优势依赖于模型的多样性，而非单一模型的克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证人类“群体智慧”中通过协商达成共识从而提升准确性的效应，是否同样适用于大语言模型之间的交互。

Method: 采用三阶段协商范式，测试来自三个不同家族的大语言模型在四个领域（视觉数值估算、同行评审、恶意行为检测、体育预测）的表现，对比独立聚合与协商后的效果。

Result: 1. 协商显著降低了集体错误率，且个体判断在协商后保留了集体增益；2. 该优势要求模型具有多样性，单一模型的克隆组无法从协商中获益。

Conclusion: 确立了机器协商作为一种通用的聚合机制，并指出“多样性”是实现性能提升的关键要素。

Abstract: The aggregation of many lay estimates often outperforms individual expert judgment, a phenomenon known as the wisdom of crowds. While this is usually attributed to the independence of estimates, an even stronger effect arises through deliberation: averaging the consensus estimates of small deliberating groups outperforms the classical wisdom of crowds, with individual judgments themselves also becoming more accurate after deliberation. Whether these improvements transfer to large language models deliberating amongst themselves is unknown. Here we adapt a three-stage deliberation paradigm previously used with human participants for use with large language models from three different families, and test it across four domains of increasing real-world stakes: visual numerical estimation (Study 1), peer review of machine-learning papers (Study 2), detection of hidden malicious behavior by an artificial intelligence agent (Study 3), and sports forecasting against a real prediction market (Study 4). Across domains, deliberation reduced collective error beyond passive aggregation of independent responses, and post-deliberation individual judgments retained this collective gain. Notably, the advantage required model diversity: groups composed of clones of a single model did not benefit from deliberating. These results establish machine deliberation as a general-purpose aggregation mechanism, and point to diversity as an active ingredient.

</details>


### [269] [Agreement Overstates Evidence: Error Dependence in LLM Judge Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22512)
*Elias Hossain,Niloofar Yousefi,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）裁判间的共识常被误认为正确性证据，但研究发现裁判间存在显著的误差相关性。这种依赖性导致共识提供的统计信息远低于预期，忽略共享错误可能导致错误的评估结论。建议通过可信样本估计裁判准确性并识别共享错误，以优化投票方法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点假设LLM裁判独立犯错，因此其共识可作为决策正确的强证据。然而，由于训练和评估方式的相似性，不同LLM裁判可能产生相同的错误。本研究旨在探究这种依赖性如何影响共识的可靠性，并揭示当前评估方法的潜在缺陷。

Method: 研究分析了开放权重和前沿LLM裁判之间的误差相关性。通过评估十个主要裁判对同一任务的判断，计算裁判间错误的成对相关系数，并比较在考虑与不考虑共享错误的情况下，系统性能差异的显著性结论。同时，研究了不同错误模式（普遍共享 vs. 小群体集中）对共识的影响，并提出了基于可信示例校准的方法。

Result: 1. LLM裁判间存在显著的误差相关性，平均成对相关系数为0.21。2. 十个裁判提供的统计信息仅相当于3.5个独立裁判。3. 在前端高准确率裁判中，依赖性更强。4. 在高达28%的比较中，忽略共享错误会导致得出一个系统显著优于另一个系统的错误结论，而考虑共享错误后该结论不再成立。5. 错误模式（分布范围）影响共识结果及适用的投票方法。

Conclusion: 仅测量总体相关量不足以全面评估裁判可靠性，因为错误模式也至关重要。为确保评估的准确性，应使用少量可信示例来估计裁判准确性、识别共享错误，并在应用新数据前选择合适的投票方法，同时在分析结果时纳入对共享错误的考量。

Abstract: Consensus among LLM judges is often taken as strong evidence that a decision is correct. This assumes that judges make their errors independently. In practice, LLM judges are often trained and evaluated in similar ways, so they can make the same mistakes. We study how this dependency affects the reliability of consensus. We find substantial error correlation across both open-weight and frontier LLM judges. In our main bank of ten judges, the average pairwise correlation between judge errors is 0.21. As a result, the ten judges only provide roughly as much statistical information as 3.5 independent judges. The dependency is even stronger among the high-accuracy frontier judges we evaluate, including judges from different providers. In up to 28% of our comparisons, ignoring shared errors leads to the conclusion that one system is significantly better, while accounting for them does not. We also find that the pattern of errors matters. Errors shared by most judges and errors concentrated among a smaller group affect consensus differently and favor different voting methods. Measuring the overall amount of correlation alone is therefore insufficient. Our results suggest a simple approach: use a small set of trusted examples to estimate judge accuracy and identify shared mistakes. These shared errors should then be considered when analyzing the results, and the voting method should be chosen using trusted examples before it is applied to new data.

</details>


### [270] [IntLawNER: A Named Entity Recognition Dataset and Benchmark in International Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22529)
*Genis Skura,Roland Bouffanais,Didier Wernli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IntLawNER，首个针对国际法文本的命名实体识别（NER）数据集与基准，采用算法-智能体混合流水线构建。研究发现传统人机一致性指标具有误导性，且现有LLM在零样本下表现极差，需通过少样本提示优化，其中Claude Opus 4.6取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管国际法是规范国家行为和保障人权的重要框架，但缺乏面向其文本细粒度（token-level）的命名实体识别资源，阻碍了相关法律信息的自动化提取与分析。

Method: 1. 数据构建：涵盖ICJ判决、UN安理会决议及ECtHR判决书中的2,987个标注句子和8,094个实体跨度，定义7种机构特定实体类型。
2. 流水线：采用“候选检索+LLM筛选+人工审核”的混合算法-智能体管道，高效将46.8万源句子缩减为紧凑标注集。
3. 评估分析：对比银层（机器生成）与金层（人工标注），揭示Cohen's kappa指标在掩盖边界错误和标签修正方面的局限性；测试GLiNER、微调Transformer及不同提示策略下的LLM性能。

Result: 1. 银到金分析显示：虽然边界匹配span的Cohen's kappa高达0.964，但若计入缺失实体、边界误差和标签修正，宏观F1仅为0.753，表明高一致性指标可能误导领域内NER评估。
2. 模型表现：零样本GLiNER因依赖机构功能而非表面形式的实体类型导致崩溃（micro-F1仅0.243）；微调Transformer难以处理稀有标签。
3. 优化效果：精心选择的少样本示例显著优于零样本提示，Claude Opus 4.6达到最高micro-F1得分0.873。

Conclusion: IntLawNER作为首个国际法NER基准和资源已发布，旨在促进国际法律文本中引用关系的提取。研究强调了在领域特定NER中，传统一致性指标的局限性以及少样本学习对提升大型语言模型性能的关键作用。

Abstract: International law provides the normative framework through which states coordinate action, regulate armed conflict, and protect human rights, yet its texts remain without token-level named entity recognition (NER) resources. We introduce IntLawNER, a NER dataset and benchmark for codified sources of international law, covering 2,987 gold-annotated sentences and 8,094 entity spans from International Court of Justice (ICJ) decisions, UN Security Council resolutions, and European Court of Human Rights (ECtHR) judgments, annotated with seven institution-specific entity types. We construct IntLawNER with a cost-effective hybrid algorithmic-agentic pipeline that reduces 468k source sentences to a compact annotation set through candidate retrieval, LLM-based vetting, and human review, with 89.6% of gold spans accepted unchanged from the silver layer. However, the silver-to-gold analysis reveals that human-machine aggregate agreement metrics can be misleading in domain-specific NER: Cohen's kappa=0.964 on boundary-matched spans masks a macro-F1 of 0.753 when missing entities, boundary errors, and label corrections are included. The benchmark shows that zero-shot span-based GLiNER collapses on entity types dependent on institutional function rather than surface form (0.243 micro-F1), while fine-tuned transformers struggle on rare labels. Carefully selected few-shot examples that demonstrate label contrasts improve every LLM over zero-shot prompting, with Claude Opus 4.6 reaching the best score of 0.873 micro-F1. We release IntLawNER as a benchmark and reusable resource for extracting references in international legal texts.

</details>


### [271] [EvidenT: Building Trustworthy Enterprise Assistants through Evidence Groundedness and Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22537)
*Anubha Kabra,Katie Jooyoung Kim,Colin Zhiwei Kou,Helene Sajer,Yimei Fan,Radomir Cisar,Heather Greenhalgh,Gabriel Martinez Vidiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvidenT轻量级流水线，通过结构化段落提取和确定性词汇对齐，在回答生成前验证证据，解决企业RAG系统中的引用漂移、无依据内容和溯源弱问题，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI助手需生成可验证且可追溯至源证据的响应，但异构企业数据上的检索增强生成（RAG）常面临引用漂移、无依据内容及来源溯源性差的问题。

Method: EvidenT方法结合结构化段落提取与确定性词汇对齐，在答案生成前对提取的证据与检索文档进行验证，以过滤无依据内容、纠正引用漂移并保留源跨度可追溯性。

Result: 在约500个真实企业查询上，EvidenT相比提示基线将黄金来源命中率平均提高29%，不产生指向未检索URL的引用，并实现了接近饱和的答案-源词汇覆盖率。

Conclusion: EvidenT作为一种无需模型重训练的轻量级方案，能有效提升企业RAG系统的证据可信度、透明度和可追溯性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI assistants must produce responses that are verifiable and traceable to source evidence. However, retrieval augmented generation (RAG) over heterogeneous enterprise data can suffer from citation drift, unsupported content, and weak source traceability. We present EvidenT (T = Trust + Transparency + Traceability), a lightweight pipeline that verifies extracted evidence against retrieved documents before answer generation, without model retraining. EvidenT combines structured passage extraction with deterministic lexical alignment to filter unsupported content, correct citation drift, and preserve source-span traceability. On approximately 500 real enterprise queries, EvidenT improves gold-source hit rate by an average of 29% over prompting baselines, produces no citations to nonretrieved urls, and achieves near-saturated answer-to-source lexical coverage.

</details>


### [272] [AutoGym: Blueprint-First Generation of Verifiable Agent Gyms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22592)
*Aarati Andrea Noronha,Kavya Ravikumar,Carly Xiaoyu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoGym框架，通过蓝图优先、显式参数控制和主动课程合成机制，从种子数据自动生成包含任务、环境和验证器的完整强化学习环境，解决现有环境构建成本高、难度虚假及模型污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习环境的构建依赖人工，成本高昂且静态；随着模型能力提升，现有任务集趋于饱和并面临数据污染风险；现有的单遍合成方法生成的任务难度往往具有欺骗性，且正确性依赖不可靠的LLM事后判断。

Method: 提出AutoGym框架，引入三种机制：(1) 蓝图优先生成：在环境实例化前确定有效解空间和环境要求，确保可解性；(2) 显式生成参数：控制任务拓扑、交互深度等以精细调节难度；(3) 主动课程合成：根据模型性能动态调整参数分布。

Result: 在生产力与时序推理设置下，AutoGym生成了覆盖能力谱系的完整环境，包括能够挑战前沿模型的实例。

Conclusion: AutoGym提供了一种自动化、可扩展的环境生成方案，能够有效应对模型能力提升带来的评估挑战，并为强化学习提供高质量、动态调整的训练环境。

Abstract: Training agents with reinforcement learning requires a gym, comprising a task, an executable environment in which the task can be attempted, and a verifier that reliably distinguishes success from failure. Constructing such gyms remains manual, expensive, and static. Task sets saturate as models improve and are increasingly exposed to contamination. Synthetic generation offers scale, but single-pass synthesis produces tasks whose difficulty is largely cosmetic. Models comparable in capability solve them despite convoluted phrasing, and correctness must be adjudicated post-hoc by unreliable LLM judges. We present AutoGym, a framework that generates complete gyms (tasks, executable environments, and verifiers) from a minimal domain seed or prior model trajectories. AutoGym introduces three mechanisms. (1) Blueprint-first generation specifies the valid solution space, environment requirements, and verification criteria before the environment is materialized, making solvability a construction prerequisite rather than a property verified after the fact. (2) Explicit generation parameters control task topology, interaction depth, capability axes, question obfuscation, and distractor composition, enabling fine-grained difficulty steering. (3) Active curriculum synthesis uses performance-informed calibration to adjust the distribution over these parameters as model capabilities evolve. Across productivity and temporal-reasoning settings, AutoGym generates gyms spanning the capability spectrum, including instances that challenge frontier models.

</details>


### [273] [MAWILE: Multi-Axis Workbench for Inspecting LLM Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22599)
*Jackson Hassell,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAWILE是一个面向开发者的工作区，用于审计大语言模型（LLM）裁判在提示词、评分标准、输入和输出四个层面的敏感性，无需金标准标签即可衡量鲁棒性和对有意义变化的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判虽然灵活可扩展，但其判决易受无关变量（如响应微调、指令变化）影响；现有系统仅覆盖部分失效模式，且审计需同时测试裁判工具和被评估项目，缺乏统一高效的审计机制。

Method: 提出MAWILE工具，通过构建和验证受控扰动（perturbations），重新执行裁判并定位敏感性。每个扰动预设判决应保持不变或按指定方向变化，从而同时测量对无关变化的鲁棒性和对有意义变化的敏感性，支持二元、序数和成对裁判，无需金标签。

Result: MAWILE成功实现了对四种表面（裁判提示、裁判标准、目标系统输入、目标系统输出）的敏感性审计，能够自动化检测判决的不稳定性，并提供开源代码支持。

Conclusion: MAWILE为LLM裁判提供了一种标准化、无需金标签的审计框架，有助于提升评价系统的可靠性和可解释性，促进更稳健的模型评估实践。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges provide a flexible and scalable method for evaluating model and agent outputs, but their verdicts can be sensitive to incidental changes in the evaluated response, judge instructions, and scoring rubric. Existing systems examine important subsets of these failure modes, but auditing a configured judge requires testing both the judge instrument and the items it evaluates. We introduce MAWILE, a developer-facing workbench for auditing judge sensitivity across four surfaces: the judge prompt, judge rubric, target-system input, and target-system output. Given a user-supplied judge and representative evaluation items, MAWILE constructs and validates controlled perturbations, re-executes the judge, and localizes the resulting sensitivity. Each perturbation declares whether the verdict should remain invariant or change in a specified direction, allowing the same system to measure both robustness to irrelevant variations and sensitivity to meaningful changes. MAWILE audits binary, ordinal, and pairwise judges without requiring gold labels. The code for this tool is available at: github.com/megagonlabs/mawile-judge.

</details>


### [274] [GaitVista: Reliability-Aware AI Measurement toward Accessible Longitudinal Gait Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22619)
*Nethmi Jayasinghe,Mihir Parashar,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GaitVista，一种基于可靠性的步态测量层，通过智能分配视觉贡献解决传感器故障与患者变化混淆的问题，显著降低误差并提升康复评估的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客观3D步态测量受限于专业实验室；现有小型相机和惯性传感器虽提高了可及性，但可靠性差异大，传感失败易被误判为患者功能变化，阻碍了康复过程中有意义的步态变化检测。

Method: 开发GaitVista轻量级门控机制，根据摄像头覆盖范围、局部视觉质量、跨模态不一致性和根运动连续性，为关节和帧分配特定的视觉贡献权重，并提供可视化检查接口。

Result: 在TotalCapture数据集上，全身和下身平均误差分别降低27.7%和27.8%，最差条件误差最低；在MoVi数据集上，唯一能同时优于双模态流的部署方法，标记支持误差降低6.4%，膝关节屈曲波形准确性提高18.9%。

Conclusion: GaitVista有效解决了多模态融合中的可靠性问题，推动了可访问的步态评估发展，但鉴于测试对象为健康人群且环境可控，目前尚未达到临床验证部署阶段。

Abstract: Tracking recovery of walking function requires detecting meaningful gait change across rehabilitation sessions, yet objective 3D measurement remains confined to specialized motion-capture laboratories. Small camera sets and body-worn inertial sensors broaden access, but reliability varies across joints and time, allowing sensing failures to masquerade as patient change. We present \textsc{GaitVista}, a reliability-aware measurement layer whose lightweight gate assigns joint- and frame-specific visual contributions using camera coverage, local visual quality, cross-modal disagreement, and root-motion continuity, and exposes them for inspection. Across seven clean and degraded sensing conditions on TotalCapture, \textsc{GaitVista} reduces average full-body and lower-body error by \textbf{27.7\%} and \textbf{27.8\%}, attains the lowest worst-condition error among fusion methods, and reduces the gap to a joint-frame oracle from $2.76$--$5.33$~cm for condition-blind baselines to $1.11$~cm. On MoVi with image-derived keypoints, it is the only deployable fusion method to improve over both unimodal streams, reducing marker-supported error by \textbf{6.4\%} relative to the strongest learned fusion baseline. On TotalCapture, it improves bilateral knee-flexion waveform accuracy by \textbf{18.9\%}. Raw inertial measurements from five TotalCapture participants show location- and time-varying magnetic disturbance, supporting the design's reliability premise. Both benchmarks contain neurologically healthy participants in controlled settings and retain participant-specific IMU calibration; we therefore report progress toward accessible gait assessment, not validated clinical deployment.

</details>


### [275] [Splitting Documents at Lower Cost: Multi-Split Boundary Decisions for LLM-Based Page Stream Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22620)
*Nikhil Reddy Pottanigari,Sepideh Kharaghani,Saverio Vadacchino,Alejandro Posada,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多分割边界决策（MSBD）方法，通过单次调用预测多个文档边界，显著提升零样本页面流分割的推理效率与准确率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决扫描邮件、PDF等页面流在下游任务前需拆分的问题；现有单边界决策模型每次调用仅识别一个边界，导致推理请求次数多、效率低。

Method: 引入多分割边界决策（MSBD），在单个页面窗口内一次性预测多个文档边界，减少LLM调用次数；在多语言模型、文档集合、输入模态和窗口大小下进行评估。

Result: MSBD在特定模型和语料库的操作范围内保持高分割准确率并显著提升推理效率；过大窗口导致准确率急剧下降及过/欠分割现象；MSBD整体实现最优的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 多边界预测可通过为特定语料库选择合适窗口大小，有效提升零样本页面流分割的效率，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Scanned mail, uploaded PDFs, and consolidated attachments often arrive as page streams that must be split into individual documents before downstream classification, extraction, or routing. Zero-shot large language models can detect document boundaries without task-specific training, but standard Page Classification (PC) and Boundary Decision (BD) formulations resolve only one boundary per model call. We introduce Multi-Split Boundary Decision (MSBD), which predicts multiple boundaries within a page window in a single call, reducing the number of inference requests. We evaluate MSBD across multiple language models, document collections, input modalities, and window sizes. The results reveal a model- and corpus-dependent operating range in which MSBD preserves strong segmentation accuracy while substantially improving inference efficiency, followed by a sharp decline at larger windows. MSBD provided the strongest overall accuracy--efficiency trade-off, while large windows expose distinct over- and under-segmentation behavior across models. These findings show that multi-boundary prediction can make zero-shot page stream segmentation more efficient when the window size is selected for the target corpus.

</details>


### [276] [Text, Pixels, or Both? Evaluating Input Representations for Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22628)
*Nikhil Reddy Pottanigari,Sepideh Kharaghani,Saverio Vadacchino,Alejandro Posada,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了文档QA系统中使用页面图像、提取文本或两者结合的效果。发现图像在准确率上占优但延迟和成本高，文本则相反。两者互补，通过简单的TF-IDF路由器根据问题选择输入，可显著提升准确率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档问答系统初始阶段对输入模态（图像、文本或两者）的选择往往缺乏独立深入的研究，需要明确不同模态的优劣及互补性以优化系统性能。

Method: 在固定提示词、评判标准和评分流程的前提下，对比四种商业模型端点、两个语料库和两种上下文环境（黄金证据页与全文），评估纯文本、纯图像及混合输入的准确率、延迟和成本。随后提出一种仅读取问题文本的轻量级TF-IDF路由器来动态选择输入模态。

Result: 1. 图像输入在所有文档长度下准确率更高，但随文档长度增加，其延迟和成本显著上升，而文本输入的延迟基本保持不变。2. 文本和图像在错误类型上存在互补性，约19-25%的问题中仅有一种表示形式正确。3. 基于TF-IDF的路由器方法相比始终使用文本提升了2.6个准确率点，同时相比始终使用图像将中位延迟降低了30%。

Conclusion: 图像和文本在文档QA任务中具有显著的互补性，单一模态无法完全涵盖另一种的优势。通过简单的路由机制根据问题内容动态选择最优输入模态，可以在保持高准确率的同时有效降低计算成本和延迟。

Abstract: Every document QA system begins with a choice that is rarely studied on its own: whether to feed the model page images, extracted text, or both. We isolate this choice, holding the prompt, judge, and scoring pipeline fixed, across four commercial model endpoints, two corpora, and two context regimes (gold evidence pages and the full document). On documents that fit the image budget, page images lead on accuracy at every document length on both corpora, but this advantage carries a growing latency and cost premium: text latency stays roughly flat as documents lengthen while image latency rises steadily. Text and images also fail on different questions, with exactly one representation correct on 19--25% of items across the reported cells, so neither subsumes the other. Exploiting this complementarity, a lightweight TF-IDF router that reads only the question text gains 2.6 points over always-text while cutting median latency 30% relative to always-vision, on a document-disjoint held-out split.

</details>


### [277] [Self-Organizing Agent Teams Learn to Reason Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22682)
*Aneesh Pappu,Mirac Suzgun,Yongchan Kwon,Federico Bianchi,Batu El,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Hancheng Cao,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自组织智能体团队（SAT），使AI智能体能从过往协作中学习可复用的策略，实现角色分配、对话阶段和信息流动的自适应组织。该方法在数学和物理基准测试中显著超越单个最强智能体及完美路由策略，证明团队协作能力本身可成为一种智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体团队依赖固定协议或显式任务分解，缺乏像人类团队那样根据未知问题结构动态适应和组织推理的能力。需要一种机制让智能体团队从经验中学习如何组织工作。

Method: 引入自组织智能体团队（SAT），通过少量样本（15道数学题和25道研究生级知识题）学习团队协作策略。这些策略包括角色、对话阶段、参与度和信息流动的重组，用于交换、挑战、修复和综合部分推理以形成解决方案。

Result: 在五个数学和物理基准测试中，SAT平均准确率为66.7%，高于最强成员（48.8%）、计算量匹配的推理（58.7%）和完美路由器（59.0%）。在AIME 2026上超过路由器13.4分。研究表明，“可演示性”（团队区分正确与错误推理的能力）与性能提升高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.90）。

Conclusion: 组织结构本身可以成为智能体的一种能力。智能体团队能够学习如何共同推理，并产生其成员独立无法达到的解决方案。

Abstract: Collective intelligence depends not only on what team members know, but also on how they organize their work. When the structure of a solution is unknown, useful roles and divisions of labor cannot be specified in advance; teams must learn from experience how to organize reasoning as it unfolds. Human teams routinely adapt this way, while existing AI agent teams rely on fixed protocols, explicit task decomposition, or routing. We introduce Self-Organizing Agent Teams (SAT), fixed teams of AI agents that learn reusable strategies from prior collaborations to organize roles, conversational phases, participation, and information flow. These strategies enable what we call collaborative computation: agents exchange, challenge, repair, and synthesize partial reasoning into solutions no member produced independently. In two independent settings, we learn teamwork strategies that transfer unchanged to unseen benchmarks, using only 15 mathematics and 25 graduate-level knowledge problems. Across five mathematics and physics benchmarks, self-organizing teams average 66.7% accuracy, versus 48.8% for their strongest member, 58.7% for compute-matched inference by that agent, and 59.0% for a perfect router over members' independent answers; on AIME 2026, they exceed this router by 13.4 points. Because gains vary across benchmarks, we ask when self-organizing collaboration helps. Across eight benchmarks, demonstrability (the organizational-psychology construct of whether a team can distinguish correct from incorrect reasoning) strongly tracks improvement over the strongest member (Spearman $ρ=0.90$, $p=0.005$): teams benefit most when correct reasoning can be recognized once it appears. More broadly, these results suggest that organization itself can become an agent capability: agent teams can learn how to reason together and produce solutions their members could not reach independently.

</details>


### [278] [Generative Embodied Multiple Behavior Control Systems for Human-like Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22691)
*Chongyu Bao,Haokai Yang,Yuhan Wang,Zhaochong An,Kunpeng Liu,Xiaolan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受人类行为控制机制启发的框架，通过习惯控制器、目标导向控制器和仲裁器的协同工作，结合3D动作生成模块，显著提升虚拟智能体的人类相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人智能体框架主要关注目标导向行为，忽视了在日常生活中起关键作用的习惯性行为，导致模型在行为逼真度上存在不足。

Method: 构建多行为控制系统：1) 习惯控制器从个性化习惯记忆中检索线索触发的行为；2) 目标导向控制器利用上下文感知的世界模型预测行动后果并评估价值；3) 仲裁器根据个体差异和即时内部状态动态平衡两者影响；4) 开发关键帧引导的3D运动生成模块以重建多样化行为指令。

Result: 通过广泛的评估方法、人类研究及消融实验，证明该方法显著提升了智能体的人类相似性（human-likeness）表现。

Conclusion: 利用习惯行为和多行为控制系统的协调机制，能够有效增强具身类人智能体的可信度和逼真度。

Abstract: An enduring and richly elaborated dichotomy in cognitive neuroscience is that of human behavior control mechanisms, divided into habitual versus goal-directed. While existing human-like agent frameworks primarily focus on modeling goal- directed behavior, habitual behavior has been largely overlooked, though it plays a crucial role in human daily life. In this paper, we address this gap by studying multiple behavior control systems that jointly model goal-directed and habitual behaviors. We propose a human behavior control mechanism-inspired framework which the Habitual Controller retrieves cue-triggered behaviors from personal- ized habit memory, while the Goal-directed Controller employs a context-aware world model to predict action consequences and estimate their values. The Arbiter dynamically balances the influence of both systems according to individual differ- ences and momentary internal states. To reconstruct diverse human-level behavior instructions in 3D environments, we further develop a keyframe-guided 3D mo- tion generation module.
